# 工业视觉异常检测深度学习方法综述：全文中英对照

> 用途：辅助阅读原论文。本文件采用机器翻译，并统一部分常见术语；保留原文论述，不把原文结论改写为新的研究建议。
> 页码：以本地原始 PDF 的物理页序为准，从封面/首页计为第 1 页。每组英文与中文使用相同页码和段落编号。
> 范围：覆盖 PDF 可提取的正文、标题、图表说明、表格文字/数值、声明与参考文献；仅省略重复页眉、页脚和独立页码。插图本身及其中不可提取的文字请对照原 PDF。
> 排列：每段英文占一行，下一行为中文；长段落按句拆分，跨页段落在页界处分开。公式和密集表格以文字形式保留，数学排版或行列关系请同时核对原 PDF。
> 校读提示：机器译文可能存在歧义；模型名、数据集名、数值、引文及原文中的论述准确性均应回查原文。

[PDF 第 1 页 | A001_008_1] EN: Abstract  
[PDF 第 1 页 | A001_008_1] 中文：摘要。

[PDF 第 1 页 | A001_009_1] EN: Industrial visual anomaly detection is critical for ensuring system reliability, safety, and efficiency. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of state-of-the-art anomaly detection techniques, analyzing methodologies, implementations, and recent advancements. Our survey aims to accelerate researchers’ understanding of emerging trends while providing a structured foundation for newcomers. We systematically review 196 recent papers covering five learning strategies, including fully supervised, semi-supervised, self-supervised, weakly supervised, and unsupervised approaches. This paper provides a detailed introduction to twelve industrial anomaly detection methods, revealing their theoretical foundations, technical principles, and practical applications. Additionally, we provide a detailed overview to 2D and 3D datasets for industrial visual anomaly detection. In addition, we critically analyze and summarize the experimental results, identify key performance indicators, and discuss the latest trends in the field of industrial anomaly detection. Beyond analysis, we contribute actionable insights for selecting optimal models for real-world deployment. Finally, we highlight open challenges and outline future research directions to drive innovation in this evolving field. The detailed resources are available at h t t p s: / / g i t h u b . c o m / I H P C R i t s / I A D - S u r v e y .  
[PDF 第 1 页 | A001_009_1] 中文：工业视觉异常检测对于保障系统的可靠性、安全性和效率至关重要。本文对当前先进的异常检测技术进行全面综述，分析其方法、实现及近期进展。本文希望帮助研究人员更快理解新兴趋势，同时为初学者提供结构清晰的基础。我们系统梳理了近期的 196 篇论文，涵盖全监督、半监督、自监督、弱监督和无监督五种学习策略。本文详细介绍十二类工业异常检测方法，说明其理论基础、技术原理和实际应用；还全面介绍工业视觉异常检测的二维与三维数据集。此外，我们对实验结果进行批判性分析与总结，确定关键性能指标，并讨论工业异常检测领域的最新趋势。除分析之外，本文还提供具有可操作性的建议，帮助为实际部署选择合适的模型。最后，本文指出开放挑战并概述未来研究方向，以推动这一不断发展的领域取得创新。详细资源见：https://github.com/IHPCRits/IAD-Survey。

[PDF 第 1 页 | A001_010_1] EN: Keywords Deep learning · Computer vision · Industrial visual anomaly detection  
[PDF 第 1 页 | A001_010_1] 中文：关键词：深度学习；计算机视觉；工业视觉异常检测。

[PDF 第 1 页 | A001_011_1] EN: 1 Introduction

With the advancement of computer vision and deep learning technologies, the field is rapidly transitioning from traditional automation to intelligent visual inspection systems. Industrial visual anomaly detection dif-  
[PDF 第 1 页 | A001_012_1] 中文：工业视觉异常检测是现代制造业中保障产品质量、提高生产效率的一项关键技术。随着计算机视觉与深度学习技术的发展，该领域正在从传统自动化迅速转向智能视觉检验系统。工业视觉异常检测与……存在差异（原文该词在页末断开，下页续）。

## PDF 第 2 页

[查看原 PDF 第 2 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=2>)

[PDF 第 2 页 | A002_001_1] EN: fers fundamentally from general visual anomaly detection primarily due to the distinct challenges posed by industrial environments. Specifically, industrial visual anomaly detection often involves anomalies characterized by subtle appearance variations, limited availability of defective samples, and stringent requirements on detection accuracy and real-time processing. In contrast, general visual anomaly detection typically deals with more diverse anomaly types without the stringent constraints of industrial applications. Moreover, anomalies in industrial settings can frequently exhibit specific structural and semantic characteristics, such as minor surface defects, precise dimensional deviations, or logical misalignments that are challenging to capture using standard visual anomaly detection approaches. Industrial visual anomaly detection faces several practical challenges inherent to real-world industrial settings. First, the scarcity or complete absence of labeled anomaly data presents a major obstacle, complicating supervised learning approaches and demanding the development of effective unsupervised or semi-supervised techniques. Second, variability in manufacturing conditions-including changes in lighting, camera angles, and environmental factors-can significantly affect the visual appearance of products, requiring anomaly detection methods to maintain robustness under diverse operating conditions. Third, industrial datasets often exhibit severe imbalance, as normal instances vastly outnumber anomalous ones, leading to challenges in accurately modeling anomalies without incurring excessive false positives.  
[PDF 第 2 页 | A002_001_1] 中文：具体地说,工业视觉异常的发现往往涉及一些反常现象,其特点是外观变化微妙、有缺陷的样品有限以及对探测准确性和实时处理要求严格。 相反,一般视觉异常检测通常涉及更为多样的异常类型,而没有严格的工业应用限制。 此外,工业环境中的异常现象常常会显示出特定的结构和语义特征,例如轻微表面缺陷、精确的尺寸偏差或逻辑上的失调,这些都难以使用标准的视觉异常检测方法来捕捉。 首先,缺乏或完全没有贴有标签的异常数据是一大障碍,使监督学习方法复杂化,并要求开发有效的、无监督或半监督的技术。 其次,生产条件的变化(包括照明变化)、摄像角度和环境因素的变化会严重影响产品的视觉外观,需要异常检测方法来维持不同运行条件下的稳健性。 第三,工业数据集往往表现出严重的不平衡,因为正常情况远远超过异常情况,导致在准确模拟异常情况而不产生过度假阳性（误检）方面遇到挑战。

[PDF 第 2 页 | A002_001_2] EN: Lastly, high levels of image noise due to sensor imperfections or harsh environmental conditions further compound detection difficulty. As production processes become increasingly refined and production lines operate at higher speeds, manual inspection can no longer meet the dual demands of efficiency and accuracy. This has led to a growing need for high-pre-cision, automated, and adaptive visual anomaly detection techniques. In recent years, deep learning-based approaches have emerged as the dominant paradigm in this field, achieving remarkable performance across various benchmarks. However, the diversity of data types, detection tasks, and model architectures has resulted in a large volume of fragmented research. Despite this progress, there remains a lack of systematic surveys that comprehensively organize, categorize, and compare existing methods. Based on this, we provide a structured and in-depth survey of recent deep learning-based methods for industrial visual anomaly detection.  
[PDF 第 2 页 | A002_001_2] 中文：最后,由于传感器不完善或环境条件恶劣导致图像噪音高发,使探测困难进一步复杂化;随着生产工艺日益精炼和生产线以更高速度运行,人工检查无法再满足效率和准确性的双重要求,这导致对高精度、自动化和适应性视觉异常检测技术的需求日益增加;近年来,深层次的学习方法逐渐成为该领域的主要模式,在各种基准中取得了显著的成绩;然而,数据类型、检测任务和模型结构的多样性导致了大量零碎的研究。 尽管取得了这一进展,但目前仍缺乏全面组织、分类和比较现有方法的系统调查。 在此基础上,我们提供了对近期深入学习的工业视觉异常检测方法的结构性深入调查。

[PDF 第 2 页 | A002_002_1] EN: Recently, Li et al. (2023) provide a comprehensive and structured survey of state-of-theart explainable anomaly detection techniques, presenting a taxonomy based on characterizing the key aspects of each explainable anomaly detection technique, with the aim of helping practitioners and researchers to find the explainable anomaly detection method that best suits their needs. Cao et al. (2024) investigate a survey of the challenges, methods, and prospects for visual anomaly detection, in which they investigate visual anomaly detection by outlining its background and conceptual definitions while systematically categorizing, emphasizing, and discussing recent advances from the perspectives of sample size, data modality, and anomaly hierarchy. Liu et al. (2024) present a comprehensive review of deep learning-based image anomaly detection techniques from the perspectives of neural network architecture, supervision level, loss function, metrics, and datasets. Lin et al. (2024) summarize a review of RGB, 3D and Multimodal methods for unsupervised industrial anomaly detection, providing a comprehensive summary of unsupervised industrial anomaly detection in all three modal environments. While these surveys offer valuable insights, they either focus on general image anomaly detection, specific learning paradigms, or individual data modalities. To bridge this gap and offer a more complete perspective tailored to real-  
[PDF 第 2 页 | A002_002_1] 中文：最近,Li等人(2023年)对最先进的可解释异常检测技术进行了全面和结构化的调查,根据每种可解释异常检测技术的主要方面的特点,提出了分类法,目的是帮助从业人员和研究人员找到最适合其需要的可解释异常检测方法。 Cao等人(2024年)调查了视觉异常检测的挑战、方法和前景,通过概述其背景和概念定义,并系统地进行分类、强调和讨论从样本规模、数据模式和异常现象等级划分的角度来调查视觉异常检测的最新进展。 Liu等人(2024年)从神经网络结构、监督级别、损失函数、度量和数据集的角度,对基于深层次学习的图像异常检测技术进行了全面审查。 Lin等人(2024年)总结了对无监督的工业异常检测RGB、3D和多模式方法的审查,提供了在所有三个模式环境中无监督的工业异常检测的综合概要,这些调查提供了宝贵的见解,但它们要么侧重于一般图像异常检测、具体的学习范式,要么侧重于个人数据模式。 弥合这一差距,提供更完整的视角,以适应现实 --

## PDF 第 3 页

[查看原 PDF 第 3 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=3>)

[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_001_1] EN: world industrial applications, we conduct a comprehensive survey on deep learning-based industrial visual anomaly detection. Specifically, we conducted an in-depth study of deep learning-based industrial vision anomaly detection methods and applications, covering five learning paradigms: fully supervised, semi-supervised, weakly supervised, self-supervised, and unsupervised learning. We systematically discussed solutions for scenarios involving scarce defect samples or noisy labels. Beyond covering major deep learning paradigms, we compile and analyze open-source resources, including widely used datasets, performance metrics, publicly available codebases, and benchmark comparisons. This facilitates reproducibility, enhances practical relevance, and supports more efficient research. Furthermore, we propose a structured taxonomy of industrial visual anomaly detection methods based on their learning strategies, aligning them with real-world annotation conditions and deployment constraints. Throughout this survey, we highlight impactful work in industrial visual anomaly detection. This study aims to comprehensively analyze the latest developments in industrial visual anomaly detection, covering two detection granularities: image-level (classifying the entire image as normal or abnormal) and pixel-level (locating abnormal regions). Additionally, we investigate industrial visual anomaly detection methods under multi-class anomaly settings. The contributions of this study are summarized as follows:  
[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_001_1] 中文：世界工业应用,我们对深层学习基础的工业视觉异常检测进行了全面调查。 具体地说,我们对深层学习基础的工业视觉异常检测方法和应用进行了深入研究,包括五个学习范式:全监督、半监督、监管薄弱、自监督和无监督的学习。 我们系统地讨论了涉及稀缺缺陷样本或噪音标签的情景解决方案。 除了主要深度学习范式外,我们还汇编和分析开放源码资源,包括广泛使用的数据集、性能指标、公开可用的代码库和基准比较。 这有利于复制,增强实用相关性并支持更有效的研究。 此外,我们建议根据学习策略对工业视觉异常检测方法进行结构化分类,使其与现实世界的标注条件和部署限制保持一致。在整个调查过程中,我们强调工业视觉异常检测中的影响性工作。本研究旨在全面分析工业视觉异常检测的最新发展,包括两个探测粒子:图像层(将整个图像归类为正常或异常)和像素级(定位异常区域)。 此外,我们调查了在多级异常情况下的工业视觉异常检测方法。

[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_002_1] EN: ● We systematically organize the existing research paradigms based on learning strategies and supervision levels, providing precise methodological guidance according to different types of annotation availability. ● We present a comprehensive analysis of industrial anomaly detection methods, high-lighting their applicability and limitations in real-world production environments. ● We summarize key challenges and research gaps in this field and outline promising future research directions to support further development.  
[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_002_1] 中文：我们系统地组织基于学习战略和监督水平的现有研究模式,根据不同种类的说明提供精确的方法指导。 我们全面分析工业异常检测方法,突出其在现实世界生产环境中的适用性和局限性。 我们总结了该领域的主要挑战和研究差距,并概述了支持进一步发展的有希望的未来研究方向。

[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_003_1] EN: Figure 1 illustrates the overall organization of this paper. Section 2 provides an in-depth and structured overview of deep learning-based industrial anomaly detection, covering both learning approaches and implementation techniques. Section 3 summarizes the industrial visual anomaly detection datasets. Section 4 presents the evaluation metrics and experimental results in industrial visual anomaly detection. Section 5 gives an outlook on the challenge and future directions in industrial visual anomaly detection. Finally, we conclude this survey in Sect. 6.  
[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_003_1] 中文：图1说明了本文件的总体组织情况:第2节深入、有条理地概述了基于学习的深刻工业异常检测,包括学习方法和实施技术;第3节概述了工业视觉异常检测数据集;第4节介绍了工业视觉异常检测的评价度量和实验结果;第5节介绍了在工业视觉异常检测方面的挑战和未来方向;最后,我们在第6节结束这项调查。

[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_004_1] EN: 2 Deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection  
[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_004_1] 中文：2 深入学习,以探测工业视觉异常

[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_005_1] EN: To provide a structured and coherent overview of industrial anomaly detection methods, we categorize the literature from two complementary perspectives: learning strategy and methodological implementation. The first perspective emphasizes the degree of supervision, including fully supervised, semi-supervised, weakly supervised, self-supervised, and unsupervised learning. These categories reflect the availability of labeled data in different industrial scenarios. The second perspective focuses on the core methodological characteristics such as reconstruction-based models, feature embedding, generative modeling, and detection strategies. In practice, many methods lie at the intersection of these two dimensions. For instance, an unsupervised method may employ a feature embedding mechanism  
[PDF 第 3 页 | A003_005_1] 中文：为了对工业异常检测方法进行有条理和连贯的概述,我们从两个互补的角度对文献进行了分类:学习战略和方法实施。第一种观点强调监督的程度,包括全监督、半监督、监管薄弱、自监督和无监督的学习。这些类别反映了不同工业情景中贴有标签的数据的可用性。第二种观点侧重于基于重建的模式、特征嵌入、基因建模和探测策略等核心方法特征。 在实践中,许多方法位于这两个层面的交叉点上。 例如,一种无监督的方法可能采用特征嵌入机制。

[PDF 第 4 页 | A004_003_1] EN: In different learning paradigms for industrial vision anomaly detection, the composition of annotated data varies significantly. Fully supervised methods rely on complete and detailed annotations-i.e., pixel-level or instance-level labels for normal samples and all known defect categories. These methods incur the highest annotation costs but provide the most accurate semantic information about defects. In semi-supervised scenarios, only a small portion of normal and abnormal images are fully labeled, while the large-scale unlabeled data contains both normal images and images with unknown defects. These models balance annotation cost and performance by leveraging a small amount of high-quality labeled data together  
[PDF 第 4 页 | A004_003_1] 中文：在工业视觉异常检测的不同学习范式中，标注数据的构成有显著差异。全监督方法依赖完整、细致的标注，即对正常样本和所有已知缺陷类别提供像素级或实例级标签。这类方法的标注成本最高，但能够提供最准确的缺陷语义信息。在半监督场景中，只有少量正常及异常图像得到完整标注，而大规模无标签数据同时包含正常图像和含未知缺陷的图像。这些模型将少量高质量标注数据与……结合使用，以平衡标注成本与性能（下页续）。

## PDF 第 5 页

[查看原 PDF 第 5 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=5>)

[PDF 第 5 页 | A005_001_1] EN: with a large volume of raw data. Self-supervised methods apply a uniform “normal” label only to normal images and do not require any defect annotations. When needed, additional unlabeled normal images can be introduced for pretext tasks to learn richer feature representations. In contrast, weakly supervised methods use only coarse-grained, image-level OK/ NG labels for normal and abnormal samples. They do not involve pixel-level or instance-level annotations, which significantly reduces manual labeling costs but also introduces issues such as low localization accuracy and label noise. Finally, unsupervised methods rely entirely on annotations from normal samples, where all normal images are labeled simply as “normal,” with no defect information. The model learns to detect anomalies by identifying deviations from the learned normal distribution, though this approach introduces greater challenges in threshold determination and false positive control. Table 1 summarizes the advantages, limitations, and application scenarios of various supervised learning methods for industrial visual anomaly detection.  
[PDF 第 5 页 | A005_001_1] 中文：大量原始数据结合使用（承接上页）。自监督方法只为正常图像统一赋予“正常”标签，不需要任何缺陷标注。必要时，可以引入更多无标签的正常图像，通过前置任务学习更丰富的特征表示。相比之下，弱监督方法只对正常及异常样本使用粗粒度、图像级的 OK/NG 标签，不涉及像素级或实例级标注；这显著降低了人工标注成本，但也带来定位精度低、标签噪声等问题。最后，无监督方法完全依赖正常样本的标注：所有正常图像都只被标为“正常”，不包含缺陷信息。模型通过识别相对于已学习的正常分布的偏离来发现异常，不过这种方式会增加阈值确定和误检控制的难度。表 1 总结了用于工业视觉异常检测的不同监督学习方法的优势、局限和应用场景。

[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_001_1] EN: 2.1.1 Fully supervised anomaly detection  
[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_001_1] 中文：2.1.1 全监督的异常检测

[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_002_1] EN: Although fully supervised anomaly detection has advantages in terms of detection accuracy, its reliance on large amounts of labeled data and limited generalization ability constrain its practical applications. Furthermore, industrial defects typically follow a long-tailed distribution, with a very small number of critical defects accounting for only a tiny fraction of the total samples. To address these challenges, recent research has focused on reducing labeling costs, improving model generalization, and enabling real-time detection-thereby enhancing the practicality and effectiveness of fully supervised anomaly detection. Meanwhile, to mitigate data imbalance, current approaches generally tackle the problem from two perspectives: loss function reweighting (e.g., Focal Loss, Class-Balanced Loss) and sample-level resampling. The former increases the gradient contribution of hard examples or minority classes to help the model focus on them more effectively; the latter leverages techniques such as hard example mining, class-balanced batch sampling, and minority class oversampling-including synthetic defect generation via GANs or diffusion models-to increase the effective exposure of rare defects. Currently, YOLO-based methods such as YOLOv3-Tiny, YOLOv5, and YOLOv8 are widely used in fully supervised industrial anomaly detection due to their speed and accuracy Yuan et al. (2022); Zhou et al. (2023); Song et al. (2024). To enhance the performance of the YOLO series in industrial anomaly detection, Yuan et al.  
[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_002_1] 中文：尽管全监督异常检测在检测精度方面具有优势，但其对大量标注数据的依赖以及有限的泛化能力，限制了实际应用。此外，工业缺陷通常服从长尾分布，一些关键缺陷的样本极少，只占全部样本的很小一部分。为解决这些问题，近期研究主要关注降低标注成本、提升模型泛化能力以及实现实时检测，从而提高全监督异常检测的实用性与有效性。与此同时，为缓解数据不均衡，现有方法通常从两个角度入手：损失函数重加权（例如 Focal Loss、Class-Balanced Loss）和样本级重采样。前者增加困难样本或少数类样本对梯度的贡献，帮助模型更有效地关注这些样本；后者采用困难样本挖掘、类别平衡的批量采样、少数类过采样，以及利用 GAN 或扩散模型合成缺陷等技术，增加稀有缺陷在训练中被有效学习的机会。目前，YOLOv3-Tiny、YOLOv5、YOLOv8 等基于 YOLO 的方法因其速度与精度，被广泛应用于全监督工业异常检测（Yuan 等，2022；Zhou 等，2023；Song 等，2024）。为提升 YOLO 系列在工业异常检测中的表现，Yuan 等……（下文续）。

[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_002_2] EN: (2022) significantly improved the accuracy and efficiency of surface defect detection by optimizing the network structure of YOLOv3-Tiny, improving the loss function, and introducing data augmentation and preprocessing techniques. Zhou et al. (2023) incorporated the CSPlayer module and a global attention mechanism into YOLOv5 to improve the accuracy and speed of small object defect detection on metal surfaces. Song et al. (2024) propose an improved YOLOv8-based model for steel surface defect detection. By integrating deformable convolution, a bidirectional feature pyramid network, and the BiFormer attention mechanism, along with adjusting the loss function to WIoUv3, their model achieves more effective multi-level feature fusion and adaptive attention allocation. These enhancements significantly improve detection accuracy and generalization performance, particularly for defects with complex textures and irregular shapes.  
[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_002_2] 中文：(2022年)通过优化YOLOv3-Tiny的网络结构、改进损失函数以及采用数据增强和预处理技术,大大提高了表面缺陷检测的准确性和效率。周等人(2023年)将CSPlayle模块和全球注意力机制纳入了YOLOv5, 以提高金属表面小型物体缺陷检测的准确性和速度。宋等人(2024年)提出改进以YOLOv8为基础的钢表面缺陷检测模型。 通过整合变形变异、双向特征金字塔网络和BiForemer注意力机制,同时将损失函数调整为WIOUv3,其模型实现了更有效的多级特性融合和适应性关注分配。 这些改进极大地提高了检测准确性和概括性绩效,特别是对于复杂的质地和不正常形状的缺陷而言。

[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_003_1] EN: 2.1.2 Semi-supervised anomaly detection  
[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_003_1] 中文：2.1.2 半监督异常检测

[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_004_1] EN: Semi-supervised learning is a machine learning approach that integrates collaborative training and prediction using a small amount of labeled data along with a large amount of unlabeled data Cai and Gao (2023). In a semi-supervised framework, a severe imbalance often exists between labeled and unlabeled datasets: labeled defects are typically much fewer than normal samples and fail to cover all defect categories. The mainstream approach first generates confidence-based pseudo labels for unlabeled samples, followed by consistency regularization for knowledge distillation. To prevent further aggravating the imbalance through pseudo labeling, researchers have proposed adaptive re-weighting and uncertainty-aware sampling strategies, which dynamically increase the contribution of high-confidence minority-class samples during training. Additionally, semi-synthetic defect interpolation-such as MixUp or CutMix applied to labeled anomalies-can help augment the apparent variations of rare defects. Semi-supervised learning is particularly suitable for industrial scenarios where labeled data is scarce, defective, or difficult to obtain and annotate. By learning the distribu-  
[PDF 第 6 页 | A006_004_1] 中文：半监督的学习是一种机器学习方法,它使用少量贴标签的数据以及大量未贴标签的数据Cai和Gao(2023年)将合作训练和预测结合起来。 在半监督的框架内,标签数据集和非标签数据集之间往往存在着严重的不平衡:标签缺陷通常比正常样本少得多,无法涵盖所有缺陷类别。 主流方法首先为未贴标签样品创建基于信任的假标签,然后对知识蒸馏进行一致性规范化。 为了防止通过假标签进一步加剧不平衡,研究人员提出了适应性重新加权和有不确定性的取样战略,这在训练期间会积极增加高度自信少数类样本的贡献,此外,对标签异常现象应用半合成缺陷内插法,如MixUp或CutMix,可以帮助增加稀有缺陷的明显变异。 在标签数据稀缺、缺陷或难以获取和标注的工业情景中,半监督学习特别合适。 通过学习分配 --

## PDF 第 7 页

[查看原 PDF 第 7 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=7>)

[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_001_1] EN: tion of normal samples or leveraging limited labeled abnormal samples, semi-supervised learning can significantly improve the accuracy and adaptability of anomaly detection while reducing the cost of data labeling. For example, Akcay et al. (2019) propose GANomaly, which is based on a conditional generative adversarial network. It achieves efficient anomaly detection by jointly learning image-space generation and latent-space inference. Rudolph et al. (2021) propose a semi-supervised defect detection method called DifferNet to address the challenges posed by the scarcity of defect samples and unknown features in manufacturing. Specifically, DifferNet uses a convolutional neural network to extract image features and a normalizing flow to perform density estimation of multi-scale features. Combined with a multi-projection layer, DifferNet can achieve high-precision detection and localization of defects. Since only a small number of defect samples are required to train the model, DifferNet is of high practical value in large-scale industrial scenarios where defects are difficult to obtain. Yang et al. (2023) propose MemSeg, a semi-supervised learning method for high-precision, real-time surface defect detection of industrial product images. MemSeg uses synthetic and memory samples to assist model learning, and combines multi-scale feature fusion with a spatial attention mechanism. It transforms the semi-supervised detection process into an end-to-end semantic segmentation task, significantly enhancing the model’s ability to distinguish between normal and abnormal patterns, thereby achieving efficient and accurate defect detection. Yao et al.  
[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_001_1] 中文：例如,Akcay等人(2019年)提议采用以有条件的生成对抗网络为基础的GANomaly,通过共同学习图像-空间生成和潜在空间推断,实现有效的异常检测。Rudolph等人(2021年)提出一种称为DifferNet的半监督缺陷检测方法,以应对因缺少缺陷样本和制造过程中未知特征而构成的挑战。 具体地说,DifferNet利用一个卷积神经网络来提取图像特征和归一化流以进行多尺度特征的密度估计。DifferNet与多投图层相结合,可以实现高精度检测和缺陷定位。由于模型训练只需要少量缺陷样本,DifferNet在难以获得缺陷的大规模工业情景中具有很高的实际价值。Yang等人(2023年)提出MemSeg,这是对工业产品图像进行高精度实时表面缺陷检测的一种半监督的学习方法。 MemSeg利用合成和内存样本协助模型学习,并将多尺度特征融合与空间注意力机制相结合。 它将半监督检测过程转化为端到端的语义分割任务,极大地增强了模型区分正常和异常模式的能力,从而实现高效和准确的缺陷检测。

[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_001_2] EN: (2023) propose BGAD, a boundary-guided adversarial learning method for anomaly detection in semi-supervised settings. This approach enhances the model’s discriminative ability by learning the boundaries of known anomalies and effectively mitigates bias introduced by these anomalies.  
[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_001_2] 中文：(2023年)提出BGAD,这是一个在半监督环境下发现异常现象的边界引导对抗学习方法。 这一方法通过了解已知异常现象的界限和有效减少这些异常现象带来的偏差来增强模型的歧视性能力。

[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_002_1] EN: 2.1.3 Self-supervised anomaly detection  
[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_002_1] 中文：2.1.3 自监督的异常检测

[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_003_1] EN: Self-supervised learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for industrial anomaly detection, especially in scenarios where labeled data is scarce, noisy, or expensive to obtain. Unlike traditional supervised methods that rely on detailed annotations, self-supervised approaches generate supervision signals automatically by designing pretext tasks on unlabeled data. Common tasks-such as image restoration, contrastive learning, feature enhancement, and sequence prediction-enable models to learn robust and informative feature representations, thereby reducing reliance on labeled samples and improving generalization. In industrial contexts, self-supervised learning serves multiple purposes: (1) it enables effective representation learning from abundant normal samples in the absence of labeled anomalies; (2) it facilitates the detection of previously unseen defects by modeling the intrinsic structure of normal data; and (3) it enhances the model’s adaptability to diverse and evolving defect patterns in complex real-world environments.  
[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_003_1] 中文：自监督的学习已成为工业异常检测的有力范例,特别是在标签数据稀缺、吵闹或昂贵的情况下。 与依赖详细说明的传统监管方法不同,自监督的方法通过设计未标数据上的借口任务自动生成了监督信号。 共同的任务 — — 如图像恢复、对比学习、特征增强和序列预测 — — 可以学习强健且信息丰富的特征描述模型,从而减少对标签样本的依赖并改进概括化。 在工业领域,自监督的学习具有多种目的:(1) 能够在没有标签异常的情况下从丰富的正常样本中有效学习;(2) 通过对正常数据的内在结构进行建模,帮助发现先前看不见的缺陷;(3) 加强模型在复杂的现实世界环境中适应多样化和不断变化的缺陷模式的能力。

[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_004_1] EN: Although current deep learning models perform well on known defect types, their performance often degrades in the presence of unknown defects that did not appear during training. This is mainly because model learning is strongly dependent on the distribution of training data. To address these challenges, researchers have proposed various generalization enhancement strategies. On the one hand, methods based on self-supervised learning, such as CutPaste Li et al. (2021) and PatchSVDD Yi and Yoon (2020), improve the model’s ability to identify unknown defects without labels by constructing pseudo-anomaly samples or using local image structure for feature learning. On the other hand, semi-supervised methods such as DifferNet Rudolph et al. (2021) and MemSeg Yang et al. (2023) improve the dis-  
[PDF 第 7 页 | A007_004_1] 中文：虽然目前的深层次学习模式在已知的缺陷类型上表现良好,但其性能往往在训练期间没有出现未知缺陷的情况下会下降,这主要是因为模型学习在很大程度上取决于训练数据的分布。为了应对这些挑战,研究人员提出了各种一般化强化战略。 一方面,基于自监督学习的方法,如CutPaste Li等人(2021年)和PatchSVDD Yi和Yoon (2020年),通过建立假冒样本或使用本地图像结构进行特征学习来提高模型在没有标签的情况下识别未知缺陷的能力。 另一方面,DifferNet Rudolph等人(2021年)和MemSeg Yang等人(2023年)等半监督方法改进了脱阴法。

[PDF 第 8 页 | A008_002_1] EN: Self-supervised learning is a machine learning method that automatically generates supervision signals by designing pre-training tasks on unlabeled data Hojjati et al. (2024); Li et al. (2024); Luo et al. (2022); Tsai and Wang (2023). It usually uses tasks such as image restoration, contrastive learning, feature enhancement or sequence prediction to learn high-quality feature representations, thereby significantly reducing the dependence on labeled data Wei et al. (2025); Bin and Wang (2024); Huang et al. (2024); Qin et al. (2024); Bauer et al. (2024); Lijie et al. (2024). In industrial anomaly detection, self-supervised learning can achieve efficient anomaly recognition and localization by modeling the features of normal samples, and is especially suitable for application scenarios where abnormal samples are scarce and the data distribution is complex.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | A008_002_1] 中文：自监督学习是一种机器学习方法,它通过设计关于未贴标签数据Hojjati等人(2024年)、Li等人(2024年)、Luo等人(2022年)、Tsai和Wang(2023年)的训练前任务,自动生成监督信号。 Bin和Wang(2024年);Huang等人(2024年);Qin等人(2024年);Bauer等人(2024年);Lijie等人(2024年)在工业异常检测中,自监督的学习可以通过对正常样本特征进行建模来实现有效的异常现象识别和定位,特别适合异常样品稀缺、数据分布复杂的应用情景。

[PDF 第 8 页 | A008_003_1] EN: During the self-supervised training phase, only normal images are used. However, when constructing pretext tasks, a form of “category” imbalance may arise among different transformation operations (e.g., occlusion, rotation, color perturbation), leading the model to become overly sensitive to certain transformations. To address this, common strategies include introducing memory queue balancing in contrastive learning to ensure that stored feature vectors are evenly distributed across various augmented views, as well as applying random occlusion ratio sampling and transformation-balancing schedules in reconstruction-based tasks. These techniques help the model maintain balanced representation capabilities across diverse potential anomaly patterns. Although such methods do not generate defective samples directly, they can enhance the model’s robustness and generalization to various types of anomalies.  
[PDF 第 8 页 | A008_003_1] 中文：在自监督的训练阶段,只使用正常图像。 但是,在构建借口任务时,不同转换作业(例如,隐蔽、旋转、颜色扰动)之间可能会出现某种“类别”不平衡现象,导致模型对某些变异过于敏感。 要解决这一问题,共同的战略包括引入记忆排队,通过对比学习确保存储的特性矢量在各种扩大的视图中均匀分布,以及在重建工作中采用随机隔离率抽样和转换平衡时间表。 这些技术帮助模型保持了各种潜在异常模式的均衡代表性能力。 虽然这些方法并不直接产生有缺陷的样本,但它们可以提高模型的坚固性和对各类异常现象的概括性。

[PDF 第 8 页 | A008_004_1] EN: Yi et al. (2020) propose PatchSVDD to improve the ability to detect and locate anomalies in images. Unlike traditional SVDD, PatchSVDD uses self-supervised learning to enable the model to automatically aggregate image patches with similar semantics, thereby betteridentifying anomalies. In addition, PatchSVDD also introduces a strategy of multi-level coding and multi-scale detection, which further improves the accuracy of anomaly detection by integrating results across different scales. Li et al. Li et al. (2021) propose a self-supervised learning method called CutPaste, which aims to train high-performance defect detection models using only normal sample data. The method constructs “pseudo-anomaly” samples for training by cutting out parts of normal images and randomly pasting them onto another image to simulate defects. CutPaste adopts a two-stage framework: first, a deep representation of normal data is learned, and then a generative one-class classifier is used to distinguish normal data from data containing abnormal patterns. In addition, CutPaste can extend the learning to the image patch level, so that the defective areas in high-resolution images can be accurately located without using labeled data. Zou et al. (2022) propose a self-supervised pre-training method SPot-the-Difference (SPD) to address the problem of insufficient local sensitivity in existing self-supervised learning for anomaly detection and segmentation tasks. By introducing the SmoothBlend local enhancement technique and the SPD training strategy, the method effectively improves the model’s local sensitivity to abnormal patterns, significantly enhancing the performance of anomaly detection and  
[PDF 第 8 页 | A008_004_1] 中文：Yi等人(2020年)建议PatchSVDD提高探测和定位图像异常现象的能力。 与传统的 SVDD不同,PatchSVDD使用自监督的学习使模型能够自动合成具有类似语义的图像图像块,从而更好地识别异常现象。 此外,PatchSVDD还引入了多级编码和多尺度检测策略,通过整合不同尺度的结果进一步提高异常检测的准确性。 Li等人(2021年)提出了一种自监督的学习方法,称为CutPaste,其目的是仅仅利用正常样本数据来训练高性能缺陷检测模型。该方法通过切除正常图像的部分并随机将其粘贴到另一张图像上以模拟缺陷来构建“假冒异常”的训练样板。 CutPaste采用了一个两阶段框架:首先,学会了对正常数据的深度描述,然后用基因化单级分类法将正常数据和包含异常模式的数据区分开来。 此外,CutPaste可以将学习扩展至图像图像块级别,这样高分辨率图像中的缺陷区域就可以在不使用标签数据的情况下得到准确定位。 Zou等人(2022年)提议采用自监督的训练前方法SPot-The-Difference(SPD)来解决现有自监管的异常检测和分解任务学习中当地敏感性不足的问题。 通过引入平滑本地增强技术和SPD训练战略,该方法有效地提高了模型对异常模式的当地敏感度,大大提高了异常检测和分解任务的性能。

[PDF 第 9 页 | A009_001_1] EN: segmentation. Ristea et al. (2022) propose a new self-supervised pre-trained convolutional attention module (SSPCAB) for effectively reconstructing and identifying anomalous samples. This method trains the model by masking or removing portions of input data and pre-dicting the missing information, using reconstruction error as an anomaly metric. Madan et al. (2023) propose the self-supervised masking convolutional transformer block (SSMCTB) to address the shortcomings of traditional anomaly detection methods in capturing global structural features. SSMCTB achieves anomaly detection by masking input information at any layer of the neural network and reconstructing the masked data using 3D convolutions and transformer blocks, thus only learning from normal samples and not relying on anomalous samples. Cai et al. (2023) present an innovative and robust anomaly detection framework, DAF, which improves on the existing self-supervised learning methods that rely too heavily on the appearance of synthetic data. Although traditional self-supervised methods perform well on anomaly detection tasks, their sensitivity to synthetic data often limits their generalization ability in practical applications. To this end, DAF introduces a teacher-student network to generate appearance-independent difference maps as guidance, and combines a segmentation decoder with an auxiliary head to accurately localize anomalies.  
[PDF 第 9 页 | A009_001_1] 中文：Ristea等人(2022年)提出了一套新的自监督的事先训练的革命关注模块,以有效地重建和识别异常抽样。这种方法通过掩蔽或删除输入数据部分和预先判别缺失的信息来训练模型,使用重建错误作为异常指标。Madan等人(2023年)建议采用自监督的蒙面革命变异器块(SSMCTB),以解决传统异常检测方法在捕捉全球结构特征方面的缺陷。 Cai等人(2023年)提出了一个创新而有力的异常检测框架,即DAF,它改进了现有过于依赖合成数据外观的自监督学习方法。虽然传统的自监督方法在异常检测任务方面表现良好,但它们对合成数据的敏感度往往限制了其在实际应用中的一般化能力。 为此,DAF引入了师生网络,制作外观独立的差异图作为指导,并将分解解器与辅助头相结合,以准确确定异常。

[PDF 第 9 页 | A009_002_1] EN: Tien et al. (2023) review the reverse distillation Deng and Li (2022) technique and propose the RD++ method, which simulates pseudo-anomalous images by adding random noise to normal images to enhance the model’s generalization ability to anomalies. RD++ introduces a multi-projection layer on this basis to learn feature representations from different scales. Additionally, a self-supervised optimal transport loss is used to further compress the feature space, making the feature distribution of normal images more compact. Finally, RD++ combines contrastive loss and reconstruction loss under a multi-task learning framework, which not only effectively compresses the feature distribution of normal samples, but also suppresses abnormal signals, thereby achieving high-precision and real-time inference capabilities for anomaly detection and localization on multiple datasets. Yoo et al. (2023) propose ST-SSAD, a self-supervised outlier detection framework for systematically tuning the hyperparameters of data augmentation in an unsupervised setting. The method combines data augmentation strategies with the model tuning process and enables automatic and effective parameter tuning of outlier detection models via an unsupervised validation loss and a differentiable augmentation function. As it does not rely on label information, ST- SSAD is particularly suitable for outlier detection tasks where labels are difficult to obtain. Zuo et al. (2024) propose ReconFA, a self-supervised domain adaptation method that aims to solve the problem of mismatched pre-trained CNN features and the object data domain.  
[PDF 第 9 页 | A009_002_1] 中文：Tien等人(2023年)审查了反蒸馏法Deng和Li (2022年),并提出了RD++方法,该方法通过在正常图像中添加随机噪音来模拟假蛋白图象,以加强模型对异常现象的泛化能力。 RD++在此基础上引入了一个多投图层,以便从不同尺度上学习地貌表现。此外,还使用一种由自监督的最佳传输损失来进一步压缩功能空间,使正常图像的特征分布更加紧凑。 最后,RD++结合了多任务学习框架下的对比性损失和重建损失,不仅有效压缩正常样本的特征分布,而且抑制异常信号,从而实现高精确度和实时推导能力,用于在多个数据集上探测异常和定位。 Yoo等人(2023年)提议采用ST-SSAD,这是一个在无监督的情况下系统调整数据扩增超参数的自监督外部探测框架。 该方法将数据扩增策略与模型调试过程结合起来,并通过一个无监督的验证损失和不同的增强功能对外部探测模型进行自动和有效的参数调整。由于ST-SSAD并不依赖标签信息,因此特别适合难以获得标签的外部检测任务。 Zuo 等人(2024年)提出了“ReconFA”,这是一种自监督域适应方法,旨在解决预先训练的CNN功能不匹配问题和对象数据领域。

[PDF 第 10 页 | A010_001_1] EN: subset of pre-trained features; and reconstruction residuals selection (RRS) adaptively screens reconstruction residuals that can assist in locating anomalous regions. Through the cooperation of these three modules, RealNet achieves outstanding performance in anomaly detection tasks. Tu et al. (2024)propose local-to-global self-supervised feature adaptation (LSFA) to fine-tune adapters and learn task-oriented representations for anomaly detection through local-to-global self-supervised feature adaptation. The core of LSFA lies in solving the problem of domain gaps between pre-trained models and object industrial and source data, optimizing the feature representation through in-module feature compactness (IFC) optimization and cross-modal local-to-global consistency (CLC) alignment. IFC optimizes the compactness of features at the local (patch-wise) and global (prototype-wise) levels through a dynamically updated memory bank, making normal features more concentrated thus improving the sensitivity to abnormal features. CLC, on the other hand, aligns features from different modalities (RGB images and 3D point clouds) through contrastive learning, enhancing the interaction of multimodal information and ensuring the consistency of features from different modalities at the local and global levels. LSFA uses pre-trained vision and point transformer as feature extractors, and introduces an adapter to fine-tune the pre-trained features to adapt to specific anomaly detection tasks.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | A010_001_1] 中文：通过这三个模块的合作,RealNet在异常检测任务方面取得了杰出的成绩。 Tu等人(2024年)提出地方到全球自监督特征调整,以微调适应者,并学习通过地方到全球自监督特征调整来发现异常的任务性表现。 LSFA的核心在于解决预先训练的模型与对象工业数据和源数据之间的域差问题,通过模块内特征压缩优化和跨模式的地方-全球一致性优化地貌表现方式,IFC通过动态更新记忆库优化地方(批量)和全球(原型)各级特征的紧凑性,使正常特征更加集中,从而提高对异常特征的敏感度。 另一方面,CLC通过对比性学习将不同模式(RGB图像和3D点云)的特征统一起来,加强多式联运信息的互动,确保地方和全球各级不同模式的特点的一致性。 LSFA使用预先训练的视觉和点Transformer作为特征提取器,并引入一个适应器来微调事先训练的特征,以适应具体的异常检测任务。

[PDF 第 10 页 | A010_003_1] EN: 2.1.4 Weakly supervised anomaly detection  
[PDF 第 10 页 | A010_003_1] 中文：2.1.4 监视不力的异常检测

[PDF 第 10 页 | A010_004_1] EN: Weakly supervised learning can effectively reduce labeling costs while reducing the dependence on finely labeled data, and improve the automation and accuracy of detection. It is particularly advantageous in scenarios with complex data distributions and scarce abnormal samples, offering an efficient and flexible solution for real-world industrial applications Shenxing et al. (2023); Wang et al. (2024). Weakly supervised methods rely solely on image-level OK/NG labels, resulting in extremely imbalanced positive and negative samples and a lack of precise localization information. To reduce the model’s bias toward normal samples, one common approach is multi-instance learning with heatmap reweighting: higher weights are assigned to the highest-scoring patches in NG images, encouraging the model to focus on potential defect regions. Alternatively, pseudo-defects can be synthesized at the local level to enrich the diversity of negative samples. In parallel, pseudo-label re-estimation can iteratively refine the defect regions within NG samples, progressively balancing the number of effective positive and negative patches and enhancing localization accuracy. Ding et al. (2022) propose DRA, an anomaly detection method based on decoupled representation learning, designed to detect both known and unknown anomaly types with only a limited number of labeled anomaly samples. DRA significantly improves the model’s generalization ability for various anomalies by learning multiple decoupled anomaly representations, including known anomalies, pseudo-anomalies, and potential residual anomalies.  
[PDF 第 10 页 | A010_004_1] 中文：监管薄弱的学习可有效降低标签成本,同时减少对贴有细标签的数据的依赖,提高检测自动化和准确性,在数据分布复杂和异常样本稀少的情况下特别有利,为现实世界工业应用Shenxing等人(2023年);王等人(2024年)提供高效而灵活的解决方案。 为了减少模型对正常样本的偏向,一种常见的方法是进行热图再加权的多元素学习:将较高重量分配给NG图像中最有分数的图像块,鼓励该模型侧重于潜在的缺陷区域。 或者可以在地方一级合成假缺陷,以丰富负面样本的多样性。 与此同时,伪标签重新估量可以反复完善NG样本中的缺陷区域,逐步平衡有效正和负偏差区的数量并提高定位的准确性。 Ding等人(2022年)提出DRA,这是一种基于脱钩表态学习的异常检测方法,旨在检测已知和未知异常类型,只有数量有限的标签异常样本。 DRA通过了解多种脱钩异常现象,包括已知异常现象、假异常现象和潜在的残余异常现象,大大提高了模型对各种异常现象的概括化能力。

[PDF 第 11 页 | A011_001_1] EN: residuals between abnormal and normal patterns, and reconstructs the segmentation map of abnormal regions by combining multi-scale prototypes and multi-dimensional self-attention mechanisms, so as to achieve accurate detection and localization of abnormal regions of different sizes, shapes and quantities. In addition, PRN also incorporates diverse anomaly generation strategies to expand and diversify abnormal samples, further enhancing model generalization and ultimately achieving efficient detection and accurate localization. Xie et al. (2024) propose the IM-IAD benchmark to address the lack of a unified evaluation benchmark in the field of industrial image anomaly detection. IM-IAD evaluates the performance of various algorithms under multiple levels of supervision, different learning paradigms, and efficiency requirements, and conducts large-scale experiments on seven datasets and multiple algorithms, providing a valuable reference for improving industrial image anomaly detection algorithms and identifying follow-up research directions.

[PDF 第 11 页 | A011_002_1] EN: Zhu et al. (2024) propose abnormal heterogeneity learning (AHL), which aims to detect and identify previously unseen anomalies during training, even with only a few anomaly samples in the open set anomaly detection scenario. AHL addresses the generalization limitations of existing methods, which struggle due to their assumption that abnormal samples follow a homogeneous distribution. By simulating a diverse abnormal distribution and collaboratively training a differentiable model, AHL enables the learning of a unified model applicable to various new domains while efficiently detecting different types of heterogeneous anomalies. Cao et al. (2024) propose BiaS, a framework for anomaly detection that aims to address the problem that unsupervised image anomaly localization methods are prone to overfitting in supervised tasks and cannot effectively utilize seen anomaly information. BiaS first generates bias knowledge about known and unknown anomalies, which is then passed to two specialized student networks for detection. By elevating unsupervised methods to a level similar to supervised learning, BiaS improves the performance and generalization ability of unsupervised methods in open world anomaly detection. Rolih et al. (2024) propose the tiling ensemble (TE) method, which specifically addresses the high memory usage problem faced by high-resolution industrial anomaly detection when detecting small-sized anomalies. TE divides high-resolution images into multiple smaller tiles and trains an independent model for each tile, significantly reducing memory consumption. Additionally, TE also introduces an overlapping division strategy to improve anomaly detection performance.  
[PDF 第 11 页 | A011_002_1] 中文：Zhu等人(2024年)提出了异常异质性学习(AHL),其目的是在训练期间发现和识别先前未见的异常现象,即便在公开设置异常检测假设中仅存在少量异常样品。AHL处理现有方法的概括局限性问题,这些方法由于认为异常样本分布相同而难以克服。通过模拟多种异常分布并合作训练一种不同的模型,AHL得以学习出适用于不同新领域的统一模式,同时有效地探测到不同类型的差异异常现象。 Cao等人(2024年)提出BiaS,这是一个发现异常现象的框架,旨在解决无监督的图像异常情况定位方法容易在监管任务中过度配置和无法有效利用已发现的异常信息的问题。BiaS首先生成已知和未知异常情况的偏差知识,然后传递到两个专业学生网络进行检测。通过将无监督的方法提升至类似于监管学习的水平,BiaS提高了开放世界异常检测中无监督的方法的性能和普及能力。 Rolih等人(2024年)提议采用平整式拼图方法,这种方法具体解决高分辨率工业异常检测在发现小型反常现象时面临的高记忆使用问题。TE将高分辨率图像分为多个小瓦片,并为每张瓷砖培养一个独立的模型,大大减少内存消耗。此外,TE还引入了重叠的分界线战略,以改善异常检测性能。

[PDF 第 11 页 | A011_002_2] EN: Bovzivc et al. (2021) propose a hybrid supervision method that combines fully supervised and weakly supervised approaches to reduce reliance on fine annotations and flexibly adapt to varying levels of annotation granularity. The method employs two subnetworks: one for pixel-level defect segmentation and the other for image-level defect classification. It achieves detection performance comparable to that of fully supervised models, even when relying only on weak annotations, while significantly lowering the dependence on accurate annotations and reducing annotation costs.  
[PDF 第 11 页 | A011_002_2] 中文：Bovzivc等人(2021年)提出一种混合监督方法,将全监督和监管不力的方法结合起来,以减少对细注和灵活地适应不同程度的注解颗粒。该方法采用两个子网络:一个用于像素级缺陷分解,另一个用于图像级缺陷分类。该方法的检测性能可与完全监控模型相比,即使仅依赖弱注解,同时大大降低对准确注解的依赖度并降低注解成本。

[PDF 第 11 页 | A011_003_1] EN: 2.1.5 Unsupervised anomaly detection  
[PDF 第 11 页 | A011_003_1] 中文：2.1.5 未受监督的异常检测

[PDF 第 11 页 | A011_004_1] EN: Compared with supervised learning, which requires annotated abnormal samples, unsupervised anomaly detection identifies anomalies by uncovering patterns within the data itself, without relying on any defect labels. The training data may consist of purely normal samples or mixed samples without distinction between normal and abnormal. A wide range of methods are employed, including density estimation, clustering, reconstruction-based gen-  
[PDF 第 11 页 | A011_004_1] 中文：与需要附加说明的异常样本的监督学习相比,无监督的异常检测通过在数据本身内部发现模式,而不必依赖任何缺陷标签,从而识别出异常现象。训练数据可以包括纯正常的样本或混合样本,而不区分正常和不正常。使用了多种方法,包括密度估计、集群、重建型原生物。

## PDF 第 12 页

[查看原 PDF 第 12 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=12>)

[PDF 第 12 页 | A012_001_1] EN: eration, and contrastive learning. With strong generalization ability, unsupervised methods can detect previously unseen defects. Given these unique features, unsupervised learning has found valuable applications in industrial visual inspection. In this context, unsupervised learning identifies anomalies using unlabeled data, extracting distribution patterns and feature representations from the dataset Qiangwei et al. (2024); Haohao et al. (2023). A common approach involves constructing a normal pattern representation based on statistical properties of normal samples or generative models and detecting anomalies as deviations from this pattern. Unsupervised learning is particularly suitable for detection tasks with a large number of normal samples and relatively few abnormal samples, and has demonstrated a high degree of automation and excellent detection results in practical applications. These characteristics and advantages make unsupervised learning an indispensable tool in the field of industrial anomaly detection. It is also important to note that dataset imbalancenamely the overwhelming predominance of normal samples over anomalies-has a significant impact on model training and performance. In industrial visual anomaly detection, this imbalance can lead to biased decision boundaries, degraded detection sensitivity for rare events, and high false negative rates. Unsupervised methods are often considered suitable for such settings, as they typically assume that only normal data is available during training. However, the absence of anomalous samples can limit the model’s ability to generalize to subtle or unknown defects.  
[PDF 第 12 页 | A012_001_1] 中文：光化和对比性学习。 有很强的普及能力, 无监督的方法可以检测出先前看不见的缺陷。 鉴于这些独特的特征, 无监督的学习在工业视觉检查中找到了有价值的应用。 在这方面, 无监督的学习利用无标签数据识别异常现象,提取分布模式和从数据集Qiangwei等人(2024年); Haoha等人(2023年)。 一个共同的办法涉及根据正常样本或基因模型的统计特性构建一个正常的模式代表,并发现与该模式不同的异常现象。 无监督的学习特别适合使用大量正常样本和相对较少异常样本的检测任务,并显示出高度自动化和实际应用方面的出色检测结果,这些特点和优势使得无监督的学习成为工业异常检测领域不可或缺的工具,同样重要的是要注意的是,数据集不平衡,即普通样本压倒性高于异常,对模型训练和性能有重大影响。 在工业视觉异常检测中,这种失衡可能导致偏差决定界限、稀有事件的检测灵敏度降低以及高负率。 无监督的方法往往被认为适合这种设置,因为它们通常假定在训练期间只有正常的数据。 但是,没有异常的样本会限制模型概括到微妙或未知缺陷的能力。

[PDF 第 12 页 | A012_001_2] EN: To alleviate this, many recent approaches incorporate pseudoanomalies, anomaly synthesis, or contrastive learning strategies to balance the latent feature space. These techniques aim to mimic the characteristics of rare anomalies, improve the separability of learned representations, and ultimately enhance detection robustness in highly imbalanced environments. In industrial visual anomaly detection, data augmentation plays a crucial role, especially under the constraint of limited anomalous samples. Effective augmentation strategies can significantly enhance the model’s generalization and robustness by simulating realistic anomaly scenarios. For example, Patch-wise Augmentation Fan et al. (2024) reformulates unsupervised anomaly detection as a supervised object detection task by synthesizing similar anomalies through self-supervised learning, thus enabling direct localization of anomalies. RealNet Zhang et al. (2024) introduces a strength-controllable diffusion anomaly synthesis (SDAS) module to generate diverse and realistic synthetic anomalies, enriching the training distribution. Compared to traditional augmentation techniques (e.g., flipping, cropping), these anomaly-aware strategies focus more on the local variability and contextual consistency of defective regions.  
[PDF 第 12 页 | A012_001_2] 中文：为了缓解这种情况,许多最近的方法都包含假冒现象、异常合成或对比学习策略以平衡潜在特征空间。 这些技术旨在模仿稀有异常特征的特性,改善所学表现的分离性,并最终在高度不平衡的环境中加强检测强度。 在工业视觉异常检测中,数据增强发挥着关键作用,特别是在有限的异常样本的限制下。 有效的强化战略可以通过模拟现实的异常情景来大大增强模型的泛化能力和稳健性。 例如,Patch-Wise Agentiation Fan等人(2024年)通过自监督的学习合成类似的异常现象,将无监督的异常检测作为一项受监督的物体探测任务进行重新配置,从而使异常现象能够直接定位。RealNet Zhang等人(2024年)推出了一个强度可控扩散异常现象合成模块,以产生多样化和现实的合成异常现象,丰富训练分布。与传统的增强技术(如翻转、裁剪)相比,这些异常现象战略更侧重于有缺陷的区域的局部变异性和背景一致性。

[PDF 第 12 页 | A012_002_1] EN: Wang et al. (2021) propose using both local and global feature comparisons to address the problem of obtaining and labeling a large amount of abnormal data in industrial scenarios. Specifically, the method uses Local-Net to extract the local features and Global-Net to obtain the global features. In addition, two detection heads are introduced: inconsistency anomaly detection, which is used to capture inconsistencies between global and local features, and distortion anomaly detection, which is used to detect subtle defects within patches. By combining feature information at both local and global levels, the method effectively detects various types of anomalies without requiring many annotated anomaly samples.. Yu et al. (2021) propose FastFlow, which utilizes a lightweight 2D normalized flow model to map the features of the input image to a standard normal distribution. The method then uses the probability value as the anomaly score to efficiently and accurately detect and localize anomalies. McIntosh et al. McIntosh and Albu (2023) propose InReaCh, which is designed to eliminate the reliance of traditional image anomaly detection meth-  
[PDF 第 12 页 | A012_002_1] 中文：Wang等人(2021年)建议使用本地和全球地物比较来解决在工业情景中获取和标注大量异常数据的问题,具体而言,该方法利用本地网提取当地地物,并采用全球网络获取全球地物。此外,还引入了两个检测头目:不一致的异常检测,用于捕捉全球与本地地物之间的不一致之处;扭曲性异常检测,用于发现图像块中的微妙缺陷。通过将地方和全球层面的地物信息结合起来,该方法可有效检测各类异常,而无需许多附加说明的异常样本。 Yu等人(2021年)提出Fast Flow,该模型使用轻量2D正统流模式将输入图像的特征映射为标准正态分布。然后,该方法将概率值用作异常的分数,以高效和准确地检测并定位异常。 McIntosh等人(McIntosh和Albu)(2023年)提出了InReaCh,其目的是消除对传统图像异常检测冰毒的依赖。

[PDF 第 13 页 | A013_001_1] EN: ods on purely normal training data. Specifically, InReaCh constructs multiple channels by associating high-confidence normal image patches in the training set and then uses these channels to evaluate image patches in new inputs. This enables efficient anomaly detection and localization in complex images containing anomalies. Liang et al. (2023) propose OCR-GAN to address the performance bottleneck caused by the insufficient reconstruction ability of traditional reconstruction methods. Specifically, OCR-GAN introduces an innovative Frequency Decoupling (FD) module to decompose the input image into different frequency distributions. Additionally, a Channel Selection (CS) module facilitates information exchange among multiple frequency branches, significantly enhancing the performance of reconstruction-based unsupervised anomaly detection. Zhou et al. (2024) propose MSFlow, a multi-scale flow model framework designed to address the challenges of unsupervised anomaly detection caused by variations in object size. MSFlow employs heterogeneous parallel flows and fusion flows to capture features and exchange information across different scales, thereby significantly enhancing performance and generalization in both image-level and pixel-level anomaly detection and localization tasks. Yang et al. (2024) propose reconstruction as sequence (RAS), a method designed to address the issue of insufficient context awareness in existing feature reconstruction approaches for unified unsupervised anomaly detection. RAS is built upon the transformer framework and introduces the RAS- Transformer module to enhance context correspondence during reconstruction. Chen et al. Chen et al.

[PDF 第 13 页 | A013_001_2] EN: (2024) propose PBAS, a novel progressive boundary-guided anomaly synthesis strategy that synthesize key feature-level anomalies in a directed manner without relying on auxiliary textures. PBAS comprises three core components: approximate boundary learning (ABL), anomaly feature synthesis (AFS), and refined boundary optimization (RBO). To make the distribution of normal samples more compact, ABL first learns an approximate decision boundary through a center constraint, and improves center initialization via feature alignment. Then AFS synthesizes anomalies with more flexible scales guided by the hypersphere distribution of normal features. Since the initial boundary is loose and may contain real anomalies, RBO refines the decision boundary by performing binary classification between artificial anomalies and normal features. Radicioni et al. (2025) accurately estimated changes in chain pitch through power spectral density analysis of vibration signals. They combined these findings with local temperature measurements to evaluate sliding rail wear. This approach provides robust technical support for improving the reliability and efficiency of industrial production.  
[PDF 第 13 页 | A013_001_2] 中文：(2024) 提议采用新的渐进式边界引导异常合成战略,在不依赖辅助质谱的情况下,以直接方式综合关键特征级异常。 PBAS由三个核心部分组成:近似边界学习(ABL)、异常特征合成(AFS)和精细的边界优化(RBO)。为使正常样本的分布更加紧凑,ABL首先通过中心限制了解了大致的决定边界,并通过特征对齐改进了中心的初始化。 然后,美国战地服务团在正常特征的超视距分布指导下,以更灵活的尺度合成异常。由于初始边界松散,可能包含真正的异常,因此,RBO通过对人造异常和正常特征进行二元分类来完善决定界限。Radicioni等人(2025年)通过振动信号的电光谱密度分析准确估计了链状变化。这些结果与当地温度测量相结合,以评估滑动铁路磨损情况。这一方法为提高工业生产的可靠性和效率提供了强有力的技术支持。

[PDF 第 14 页 | A014_003_1] EN: As shown in Fig. 3, the teacher-student network-based industrial anomaly detection method integrates transfer learning and knowledge distillation. The teacher network is typically a large, fully trained model that has learned a rich representation of normal data features from a large dataset. The student network is smaller in size and is trained by imitating the output of the teacher network. During the training phase, the teacher network is first trained using a dataset containing only normal samples. Subsequently, the student network learns the feature distribution of the teacher network through a knowledge distillation process, with the intermediate output or feature map of the teacher network as the learning object. During the inference phase, the industrial image to be detected is input into both the teacher and student network, and the difference between the two outputs is compared. If the difference is signifi-  
[PDF 第 14 页 | A014_003_1] 中文：如图3所示,以教师—学生网络为基础的工业异常检测方法结合了转移学习和知识蒸馏,教师网络通常是一个大型的、经过充分训练的模式,它从一个庞大的数据集中吸取了丰富的正常数据特征;学生网络的规模较小,通过模仿教师网络的产出进行训练;在训练阶段,教师网络首先使用只包含正常样本的数据集接受训练;随后,学生网络通过知识蒸馏过程了解教师网络的特点分布,而教师网络的中间输出或特征地图则是学习的对象。 在推论阶段,要检测到的工业图像是既输入教师网络,也输入学生网络,并且对两种输出之间的差异进行比较。

## PDF 第 16 页

[查看原 PDF 第 16 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=16>)

[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_001_1] EN: The image is classified as abnormal if the anomaly score  
[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_001_1] 中文：图像被归类为异常, 如果异常得分

[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_002_1] EN: A  
[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_002_1] 中文：A

[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_003_1] 中文：> □,哪里是□异常

[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_004_1] EN: detection threshold.  
[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_004_1] 中文：检测门槛。

[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_005_1] EN: Specifically, Bergmann et al. (2020) propose uninformed students (US), an unsupervised anomaly detection method based on the teacher-student framework. US trains a student network to mimic the output of a pre-trained teacher network and detects anomalies by analyzing the prediction uncertainty and regression error of the student network. Wang et al. (2021) propose an unsupervised anomaly detection method that simultaneously compares local and global features. The method uses a local-net to extract features from image patches and a global-net to capture contextual features from the surrounding environment. Additionally, it introduces inconsistency anomaly detection and distortion anomaly detection heads to detect mismatches between local and global features and subtle defects within patches, respectively. Salehi et al. (2021) propose multiresolution knowledge distillation (MKD) to learn representations that distinguish normal and anomalous samples when only normal samples are available. MKD leverages a pre-trained expert network on ImageNet and distills its features from multiple intermediate layers into a simpler clone network. Anomalies are detected and localized by analyzing the feature differences between the expert and clone networks when processing anomalous inputs. Deng et al. (2022) propose an anomaly detection method called reverse distillation (RD). RD employs teacher and a student encoders, where the teacher extracts a single-class embedding, and the student reconstructs a multi-scale representation.  
[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_005_1] 中文：具体地说,Bergmann等人(2020年)提出不知情学生(美国),这是基于师生框架的一种无监督的异常检测方法。 美国训练学生网络模仿预训练教师网络的产出并通过分析学生网络的预测不确定性和回归错误来探测异常现象。 王等人(2021年)提出了一种与本地和全球特征同时比较的无监督的异常检测方法。 该方法使用本地网从图像图像块中提取特征,并用全球网捕捉周围环境的背景特征。 Salehi等人(2021年)提出多分辨率知识蒸馏法(MKD),以在只有正常样品的情况下学习区分正常和异常样本的表示方式。 MKD利用一个经过预先训练的图像网络专家网络,将其特征从多个中间层蒸发成一个简单的克隆网络。 Deng等人(2022年)建议采用反向蒸馏法(RD)。 RD雇用了教师和学生编码员,由教师提取单级嵌入器,而学生则重建了一个多级代表制。

[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_005_2] EN: To improve anomaly detection accuracy, RD introduces a trainable one-class bottleneck embedding module, which compresses high-dimensional features into compact embeddings, preserving normal pattern information while suppressing anomalous disturbances. RD performs knowledge distillation on multi-scale features, utilizing cosine similarity loss to quantify activation differences between the teacher and student models. The loss function is computed by aggregating multi-scale anomaly maps, enabling the student network to mimic the teacher network’s behavior and achieve high-performance anomaly detection. Wan et al. (2022) propose an unsupervised image anomaly detection and segmentation method based on pre-trained feature mapping (PFM). PFM detects anomalies by mapping images from one pre-trained feature space to another. To further enhance performance, PFM introduces bidirectional PFM (B-PFM) and multi-level bidirectional PFM (MB-PFM). B-PFM incorporates a reverse mapping mechanism to enable bidirectional feature utilization, significantly improving information efficiency and model performance. MB-PFM integrates multi-level feature mapping, enhancing the model’s ability to detect complex anomalies through multi-scale feature fusion. Yamada et al. (2022) propose student-teacher pyramid matching (STPM), which enhances accuracy by integrating a student-teacher network with a discriminative network. Specifically, the student network reconstructs normal image features, while the teacher network provides feature representations for anomalous regions.  
[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_005_2] 中文：为提高异常检测的准确性,RD引入了一个可训练的单级瓶颈嵌入模块,该模块将高维特性压缩到紧凑嵌入中,保存正常模式信息,同时抑制异常扰动。 RD在多尺度特征上进行知识蒸馏,利用连线相似性损失来量化教师和学生模型之间的激活差异。 损失函数通过集成多级异常图计算,使学生网络能够模仿教师网络的行为并实现高性能异常检测。 Wan等人(2022年)根据预先训练的地貌图谱,提出了一种无监督的图像异常检测和分解方法。PFM通过从一个预训练的地貌空间向另一个空间绘制图像来检测异常现象。为了进一步提高性能,PFM引入双向PFM(B-PFM)和多级双向PFM(MB-PFM)。B-PFM包含一种反向绘图机制,以便实现双向地貌利用,大大提高信息效率和模型性能。 MB-PFM将多层次的地貌制图整合在一起,通过多级地貌融合增强模型检测复杂异常的能力。 Yamada 等人(2022年)提出学生和教师之间的金字塔匹配(STPM ) , 通过将师生网络与歧视网络相结合来提高准确性。 具体而言,学生网络重建了正常的图像特征,而教师网络则为异常地区提供地貌表现。

[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_005_3] EN: Additionally, STPM employs a discriminative network trained on pseudo-anomalies generated from anomalous images, enabling more precise differentiation between normal and anomalous regions. Rudolph et al. Rudolph et al. (2023) propose the asymmetric student-teacher network (AST), which employs normalized flows as the teacher network for density estimation and trains a feed-forward network as the student network to detect anomalies at larger distances. Cao et al. (2022) propose an anomaly segmentation method based on information knowledge distillation (IKD). IKD addresses overfitting issues arising when a neural network’s capacity does not align with its knowledge by extracting informative knowledge and providing strong supervisory signals. To enhance  
[PDF 第 16 页 | A016_005_3] 中文：此外,STPM还利用了一个关于异常图象产生的假反常现象的歧视性网络,从而能够更准确地区分正常区域和异常区域。 Rudolph等人(2023年)提议采用不对称学生-教师网络(AST),将正常流动用作密度估计的教师网络,并将进料-前进网络训练成学生网络,以在较远的地方发现异常现象。 Cao等人(2022年)提出基于信息知识蒸馏的异常分割法。 国际知识开发公司通过提取信息知识和提供强有力的监督信号,解决神经网络能力与其知识不匹配时产生的过多问题。

## PDF 第 17 页

[查看原 PDF 第 17 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=17>)

[PDF 第 17 页 | A017_001_1] EN: segmentation performance, IKD incorporates context similarity loss (CSL) and adaptive hard sample mining (AHSM). CSL captures contextual information within the normal data manifold. AHSM prioritizes hard samples that contain valuable information, encouraging the model to focus on more challenging cases. Gu et al. (2023) propose memory-guided knowledge distillation (MemKD), which enhances the normality of features generated by the student model by storing normal data information. Additionally, MemKD employs a normalcy representation learning strategy to construct a normal sample set, enabling the normal knowledge recall module to learn prior knowledge without anomalous data and retrieve it during inference. Zhang et al. (2023) propose DeSTSeg to address the lack of constraints on anomalous data and the empirical nature of multi-level feature fusion in traditional teacher-student anomaly detection frameworks. DeSTSeg integrates a pre-trained teacher network, a denoising student encoder-decoder, and a segmentation network. By introducing a denoising process, the student network generates feature representations that differ from those of the teacher network for anomalous inputs. Additionally, the segmentation network adaptively fuses multi-level features, significantly enhancing anomaly detection performance. Tong et al. (2024) propose EMMFRKD, an enhanced anomaly detection and localization model based on inverse distillation. EMMFRKD extracts discriminative features across multiple scales by incorporating an enhanced multi-scale feature mutual mapping fusion module.  
[PDF 第 17 页 | A017_001_1] 中文：分类性能,IKD 包含背景相似性损失(CSL)和适应性硬采样采矿(AHSM) 。 CSL 在正常数据元数中捕获上下文信息。 AHSM 将含有宝贵信息的硬样本列为优先事项,鼓励该模型侧重于更具挑战性的案件。Gu等人(2023年)提出了记忆引导知识蒸馏法(MemKD),通过存储正常的数据信息来提高学生模式所产生特征的正常性。此外,MEMKD 采用了一种常态代表学习战略来构建一个普通的样本集,使正常的知识回溯模块能够在不提供异常数据的情况下学习先前的知识并在推断过程中检索。 Zhang等人(2023年)建议,DESTEG解决对异常数据缺乏限制以及多级特征融合在传统的师生异常检测框架中的经验性质问题。DeSTSeg整合了经过训练的教师网络、取消学生编码器解密器和隔离网络。通过引入一个分级程序,学生网络生成了不同于教师网络的异常情况说明。 此外,分解网络还根据情况将多层特征引信,大大提高了异常现象的探测性能。Tong等人(2024年)提出了EMMFRKD,这是一个以反蒸馏为基础的强化异常检测和定位模型。EMMFRKD通过纳入一个强化的多尺度特征相互映射聚合模块,在多个尺度上提取歧视性特征。

[PDF 第 17 页 | A017_001_2] EN: Additionally, it employs a coordinate attention mechanism and a single-category embedding memory bank to enhance the representation of abnormal regions and improve model reconstruction quality. Wu et al. (2024) propose autoencoder knowledge distillation (AEKD), which addresses the model generalization problem in traditional knowledge distillation methods by introducing an asymmetric teacher-student model structure. Additionally, it incorporates a trainable multi-scale feature fusion module to mitigate anomaly interference and designs distinct data flows for the teacher and student models, enhancing their differences in anomaly representation. Gu et al. (2024) propose ASTMFR, a novel asymmetric student-teacher network framework designed to address the challenge of collecting anomalous samples. ASTMFR enhances the feature alignment of the student network on normal samples by introducing a masked feature regeneration (MFR) module. Additionally, it leverages an asymmetric network structure to increase the feature diversity of unseen anomalous samples during testing. This approach mitigates the issue of high feature similarity among anomalous samples in traditional methods, significantly improving anomaly detection and localization accuracy.  
[PDF 第 17 页 | A017_001_2] 中文：此外,它采用协调注意力机制和单一类别嵌入记忆库,以提高不正常地区的代表性和改善模型重建质量。 Wu 等人(2024年)提出自动编码知识蒸馏法,通过引入不对称师生模式结构来解决传统知识蒸馏方法中的典型概括问题。此外,它还包括一个可训练的多级特征聚合模块,以减轻异常干扰,并为教师和学生模式设计不同的数据流,从而增加他们在异常代表性方面的差异。 Gu等人(2024年)提出ASTTMFR,这是一个新的不对称学生-教师网络框架,旨在应对采集异常样本的挑战;ASTMFR采用蒙面特征再生模块,加强了学生网络在正常样本上的特征一致性;此外,它利用不对称网络结构提高检测过程中未见异常样本的特征多样性;这一方法缓解了传统方法中异常样本具有高度相似性的问题,大大提高了异常检测和定位准确度。

[PDF 第 17 页 | A017_002_1] EN: Li et al. (2024) propose MCAD, a multi-class anomaly detection framework. Specifically, MCAD integrates relational knowledge distillation in the distillation process to enhance knowledge transfer and improve the expressiveness of student networks, so as to achieve high-performance anomaly detection, localization, and classification. Zhang et al. (2024) propose DSKD, a dual-student knowledge distillation framework for structural and logical anomaly detection. Specifically, the local student focuses on accurate low-level feature reconstruction, primarily for detecting structural anomalies, while the global student learns global correlations to enhance logical anomaly detection. To improve global information capture, the global context concentration block (GCCB) is introduced in the global student. GCCB retains the most critical global information by compressing high-level semantic features and restoring them to their original size. To further enhance the global student’s ability to learn global context, a context affinity loss is introduced. This loss is computed by measuring the cosine similarity between the student’s feature vector and all feature vectors  
[PDF 第 17 页 | A017_002_1] 中文：Li等人(2024年)提议采用多级异常检测框架MCDD,具体地说,MCAD将关系知识蒸馏纳入提炼过程,以加强知识转让和提高学生网络的表达性,从而实现高性能异常检测、定位和分类。张等人(2024年)提出DSKD,这是一个用于结构性和逻辑异常检测的双阶段学习知识提炼框架。具体地说,当地学生侧重于精确的低水平特征重建,主要用于发现结构异常现象,而全球学生则学习全球相关性,以加强逻辑异常检测。 为改善全球信息获取,全球背景集中区块(GCCB)引入到全球学生中。 通过压缩高层次语义特征并恢复其原始大小,该区块保留了最关键的全球信息。 为了进一步提高全球学生学习全球背景的能力,引入了近似性损失。 这一损失是通过测量学生特性矢量和所有特性矢量之间的相近性来计算的。

[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_001_1] EN: in the teacher feature map, converting the results into a probability distribution. The KL divergence quantifies the difference between the teacher and student distributions, optimizing the global student’s feature learning. Batzner et al. (2024) propose EfficientAD, a visual anomaly detection method designed to balance computational efficiency and detection performance in existing anomaly detection approaches. EfficientAD introduces a lightweight feature extractor and a student-teacher network to detect abnormal features and identify logical anomalies. The student-teacher network, inherited from the encoder, enables high-accuracy anomaly detection while maintaining low latency and high throughput. EfficientAD achieves precise detection of structural and logical anomalies with millisecond latency. Zhang et al. (2024) propose FCACDL, a feature distillation learning method designed to address the limitations of teacher-student networks in feature representation and anomaly detection performance constrained by the teacher network. FCACDL employs a vision transformer as the backbone network and integrates the central feature strategy, self-attention mask strategy, and multi-view consistency constraint. By leveraging feature distillation and self-supervised learning, it enhances visual anomaly detection performance. Liu et al. (2024) propose dual-modeling decoupling distillation (DMDD) to address the overgeneralization problem in existing knowledge distillation methods for anomaly detection.  
[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_001_1] 中文：KL 差异量化了师生分布的差异,优化了全球学生的特征学习。 Batzner等人(2024年)提出了高效的视觉异常检测方法,以平衡现有异常检测方法中的计算效率和检测性能。高效的AD引入了一个轻量级特征提取器和一个学生-教师网络,以检测异常特征并识别逻辑异常。从编码器中继承下来的学生-教师网络可以发现高精度异常现象,同时保持低潜值和高吞吐量。 高效的AD能够精确地发现结构上和逻辑上的异常,且有毫秒的延迟。 Zhang等人(2024年)提出FCACDL,这是一种特别蒸馏学习方法,旨在解决教师—学生网络在特征表现和异常检测性能方面受到教师网络限制的局限性。 FCACDL使用一个视觉Transformer作为主干网,并整合了核心特征战略、自我注意面具战略和多视角一致性限制。它通过利用特性蒸馏和自监督的学习,提高了视觉异常检测性能。 Liu等人(2024年)建议采用双重型号脱钩蒸馏法,以解决现有知识蒸馏方法中的异常检测问题。

[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_001_2] EN: DMDD enhances anomaly detection accuracy and robustness by constructing a decoupled student-teacher network and introducing a dual-mode distillation strategy, consisting of normal distillation and anomalous distillation. Additionally, it employs a multi-sensory segmentation network to optimize anomaly graph fusion.  
[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_001_2] 中文：DMDD通过建立分离的师生网络和采用双模蒸馏战略,包括正常蒸馏和异常蒸馏,提高了异常检测的准确性和稳健性,此外,它使用多感应分解网络优化异常图聚变。

[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_002_1] EN: 2.2.2 One-class classification  
[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_002_1] 中文：2.2.2 单类分类

[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_003_1] EN: One-class classification serves as a transitional paradigm between self-/unsupervised and weakly supervised learning. During the training phase, only normal samples are involvedsimilar to unsupervised learning-but the objective function explicitly learns a single-class boundary to distinguish potential anomalies, rather than merely performing reconstruction or density estimation. This approach offers labeling costs comparable to those of unsupervised learning, while delivering discriminative performance close to that of supervised methods, making one-class classification one of the most cost-effective strategies in industrial applications. As shown in Fig. 4, one-class classification refers to a classification problem where only one class is known during training. It learns the feature space distribution of data points in this class to establish a model for determining whether new data points belong to the same class. One-class classification focuses solely on identifying data points within a single class, without requiring prior knowledge of multiple classes. The core idea of OCC  
[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_003_1] 中文：单级分类是自学/无人监督的学习和监管薄弱之间的过渡模式,在训练阶段,只有普通样本与无监督的学习相似,但客观功能明确学会了单级界限来区分潜在的异常现象,而不仅仅是进行重建或密度估计。 这一方法将成本与无监督的学习相提并论,同时提供类似于受监督方法的歧视性表现,使一级分类成为工业应用中最具成本效益的战略之一。 如图4所示,单级分类是指在训练期间只知道一个类的分类问题。它学习了该类中数据点的特征空间分布,以建立一个模型来确定新数据点是否属于同一类。单级分类仅侧重于确定某一类中的数据点,而无需事先了解多个类。 OCC 的核心概念

[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_004_1] EN: Fig. 4 One-class classification method. Training phase: The model is trained exclusively on normal  
[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_004_1] 中文：图4 单级分类方法:训练阶段:该模型完全按照正常情况进行训练

[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_005_1] EN: samples, constructing a boundary within the feature space that encloses these normal data points. Inference phase: The validation data undergo the same feature extraction process to produce feature vectors, which are then compared against the previously learned boundary. Any point falling within the boundary is deemed normal; otherwise, it is classified as anomalous  
[PDF 第 18 页 | A018_005_1] 中文：推断阶段:验证数据经过同样的特征提取过程产生特性矢量,然后与先前所学的边界进行比较。

Common OCC methods include boundary-based and probability distribution-based models. Among these, Support Vector Data Description (SVDD) is a representative algorithm that classifies new data points by constructing a minimal hypersphere enclosing all training samples: data points inside the hypersphere are considered normal, while those outside are deemed anomalous. For an input industrial image I, the feature representation f is defined as:

[PDF 第 20 页 | A020_014_1] EN: Specifically, Li et al. (2021) propose CutPaste, a self-supervised learning method designed to train high-performance defect detection models using only normal images. The method simulates anomalies by cutting patches from a normal image and pasting them at random locations within the same image, thereby introducing local structural inconsistencies. Cut- Paste follows a two-stage framework: in the first stage, it learns deep feature representations of normal data; in the second stage, it constructs a one-class classifier to distinguish between normal patterns and those altered by synthetic anomalies. Furthermore, the method extends to patch-level representation learning, which enables effective defect localization in high-resolution images without requiring any labeled data during training. Cao et al. (2023) present ADShift, a generalized normal learning method designed to address anomaly detection under distribution shifts. ADShift enhances the accuracy and robustness of anomaly detection by minimizing the distribution gap between in-distribution and out-of-distribution normal samples during both training and inference in an unsupervised manner. This approach effectively mitigates the impact of distribution inconsistency. Liu et al. (2023) propose SimpleNet, which consists of four key components: a pre-trained feature extractor, a feature adapter, an anomaly feature generator, and an anomaly discriminator. SimpleNet generates anomalous features by introducing Gaussian noise into the feature space and employs a binary anomaly discriminator to distinguish between normal and anomalous features, thereby enabling efficient and accurate anomaly detection and localization.  
[PDF 第 20 页 | A020_014_1] 中文：具体地说,Li等人(2021年)提出CutPaste,这是一种自监督的学习方法,旨在仅使用正常图像来训练高性能缺陷检测模型。该方法通过从正常图像中切除图像块并将其粘贴在同一图像中的随机位置来模拟异常现象,从而造成局部结构上的不一致。切-粘贴遵循了两个阶段的框架:在第一阶段,它学会对正常数据的深度特征表现;在第二阶段,它建造了一个单级分类器,以区分正常模式和合成异常模式所改变的模式。 此外,该方法还延伸到图像块级代表学习,使高分辨率图像在训练期间无需任何标签数据即可有效定位。Cao 等人(2023年)介绍了AdShifft,这是一种通用的正常学习方法,旨在解决分配变化中的异常检测问题。 Adshift通过以无监督的方式尽量减少在训练和推断过程中分配和分配外正常样本之间的分布差距,提高了异常检测的准确性和稳健性。 这一方法有效地减轻了分销不一致的影响。 Liu等人(2023年)提出了SympleNet,它由四个关键部分组成:一个预先训练的特征提取器、一个功能适配器、一个异常特性生成器和一个反常歧视器。 SypicNet通过将高斯噪音引入地物空间并使用二元异常区分器来区分正常和异常特征,从而能够有效和准确地发现和定位异常现象。

[PDF 第 20 页 | A020_014_2] EN: Yang et al. (2024) propose SLSG, a semi-supervised anomaly detection method for industrial images, designed to address challenges in feature embedding and one-class classification. SLSG integrates self-supervised learning and self-attention convolutional networks, leveraging a generative pre-trained network to assist the encoder in learning feature embeddings of normal patterns. Additionally, it introduces simulated anomalous samples to provide pseudo-prior knowledge of anomalies and constructs a general graph structure to model relationships between image elements comprehensively. SLSG significantly enhances industrial image anomaly detection performance, particularly in logical anomaly detection. Strater et al. (2024) propose GeneralAD, an anomaly detection framework designed to overcome the limitations of existing methods in cross-domain applications. GeneralAD leverages a visual transformer for feature extraction and integrates a self-supervised anomaly feature generation module to create hard-to-recognize pseudo-anomalies. It then employs an attention-based discriminator to accurately detect image-level anomalies and generate interpretable anomaly maps. GeneralAD enables generalized anomaly detection across semantic, near-distribution, and industrial settings. He et al. (2024) propose DS2, a two-stage intensive pre-training model designed to address the poor transferability of models in image anomaly localization caused by the lack of comprehensive anomaly samples. DS2 integrates double-positive sample selection criteria and multi-scale feature learning to enhance the model’s ability to distinguish small and subtle anomalies effectively.  
[PDF 第 20 页 | A020_014_2] 中文：Yang 等人(2024年)提出SLSG,这是工业图像的半监督异常检测方法,旨在应对地物嵌入和单级分类方面的挑战;SLSG整合了自监督学习和自我关注进化网络,利用基因学前训练网络帮助编码者学习正常模式的特征嵌入;此外,还引入模拟异常现象样本,以提供对异常现象的假原始知识,并构建一个总图结构来全面建模图像元素之间的关系。 SLSG大大加强了工业图像异常检测性能,特别是在逻辑异常检测方面。Strater等人(2024年)提议建立GNAD,这是一个旨在克服跨域应用中现有方法局限性的异常检测框架。 GSAD利用视觉Transformer进行地貌提取,并整合一个自监督的异常特征生成模块,以创建难以识别的伪异常现象。然后,它使用关注歧视器来准确检测图像水平异常现象并绘制可解释的异常图。 GeneralAD使得能够在整个语义、近分布和工业环境中普遍发现异常现象。 他等人(2024年)提出了DS2,这是一个两阶段密集的训练前模式,旨在解决因缺乏全面的异常抽样而导致图像异常化模型的可转移性差的问题。 DS2将双重积极的样本选择标准和多尺度特征学习结合起来,以加强该模式有效区分小和微妙异常的能力。

[PDF 第 20 页 | A020_014_3] EN: Compared to end-to-end models, DS2 demonstrates greater transferability on new datasets and exhibits higher stability and robustness, even with small training datasets and small batch sizes. Chen et al. (2024) propose GLASS, a unified anomaly detection framework designed to overcome the limitations of existing strategies in industrial anomaly detection, particularly in anomaly coverage and controllability. It specifically addresses the challenge of detecting weak defects that closely resemble normal regions. GLASS improves weak defect detection by integrating global anomaly syn-  
[PDF 第 20 页 | A020_014_3] 中文：与端到端模型相比,DS2显示新数据集的可转移性更大,并显示出更高的稳定性和稳健性,即使训练数据集小且批量小。Chen等人(2024年)提议采用GLASS,这是一个统一的异常检测框架,旨在克服工业异常检测现有战略的局限性,特别是在异常覆盖和控制性方面。它具体解决了发现与正常区域相近的薄弱缺陷的挑战。GLASS通过整合全球异常现象同步改进弱点检测。

## PDF 第 21 页

[PDF 第 21 页 | A021_001_1] EN: thesis (GAS) and local anomaly synthesis (LAS). Additionally, it employs gradient ascent and truncated projection-guided Gaussian noise to generate near-distribution anomalies in a controllable manner, further enhancing defect detection. Furthermore, GLASS synthesizes diverse anomalies at the image level through texture superposition, achieving a broader and more controllable anomaly synthesis strategy, which significantly enhances industrial anomaly detection and localization performance.  
[PDF 第 21 页 | A021_001_1] 中文：此外,它使用梯度升温和短速投影制导噪音来以可控制的方式产生近分布异常现象,从而进一步加强缺陷检测。 此外,GLAS通过纹理叠加,在图像层面合成多种异常现象,实现更广泛、更可控制的异常现象合成战略,这极大地加强了工业异常检测和定位的性能。
