[PDF 第 22 页 | A022_056_1] EN: Specifically, Yu et al. (2021) propose FastFlow, which employs a lightweight 2D normal-izing flow model to transform input image features into a standard normal distribution and uses probability values as anomaly scores to efficiently and accurately detect and localize anomalies. Rudolph et al. (2021) propose DifferNet, a semi-supervised defect detection method designed to address the challenge of defect detection caused by the scarcity and unknown characteristics of defect samples in the manufacturing process. DifferNet leverages a convolutional neural network for feature extraction and employs a normalizing flow to estimate feature density using multi-scale feature extraction and multi-transform scoring strategies, enabling high-precision defect detection and localization. With only a small number of training samples, DifferNet effectively detects and localizes defects, making it particularly suitable for real-world industrial applications where acquiring large defect datasets is challenging. Zhang et al. (2022) propose PEDENet, which integrates a block embedding network, a density estimation network, and a location prediction auxiliary network. Leveraging deep learning and density estimation methods, PEDENet accurately localizes anomalous regions in images using only normal images for training. To achieve precise anomaly localization, PEDENet employs a deep encoder for dimensionality reduction, a Gaussian mixture model to estimate the distribution of block embeddings, and a multilayer perceptron (MLP) to predict the relative positions of adjacent blocks. Gudovskiy et al.  
[PDF 第 22 页 | A022_056_1] 中文：具体地说,Yu等人(2021年)提出Fast Flow,该模型使用轻量的2D归一化流模式将输入图像特性转换成标准正常分布,并将概率值用作异常分数,以高效和准确地检测异常现象并将其定位。Rudolph等人(2021年)提出了DifferNet,这是一个半监督性缺陷检测方法,旨在应对由于制造过程中缺陷样本稀缺且特征提取特点特性不明而造成的缺陷检测挑战。DifferNet利用一个脉冲神经网络进行特征提取,并利用一种归一化流来利用多尺度特征提取和多变式评分策略估算特征密度,从而能够发现高精度缺陷并实现定位。 DifferNet只有少量的训练样本,能够有效检测和定位缺陷,使之特别适合现实世界的工业应用,而获取大型缺陷数据集则具有挑战性。 张等人(2022年)建议PEDENet将区块嵌入网络、密度估计网络和位置预测辅助网络结合起来。 PEDENet利用深层学习和密度估计方法,精确地在图像中将异常区域定位,仅使用正常图像进行训练。 为了实现精确的异常位置化,PEDENet使用一个深度编码器来减少维度、一种高斯混合模型来估计块嵌入物的分布,以及一种多层感应器(MLP)来预测相邻区块的相对位置。

[PDF 第 22 页 | A022_056_2] EN: (2022) propose CFlow-AD, a real-time unsupervised anomaly detection and localization model designed for efficient anomaly detection in high-resolution images without labeled data or  
[PDF 第 22 页 | A022_056_2] 中文：(2022) 提议CFlow-AD,这是一个无监督的实时异常检测和定位模型,目的是在无标签数据或无标签的高分辨率图像中有效检测异常。

<a id="pdf-page-23"></a>

## PDF 第 23 页

[查看原 PDF 第 23 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=23>)

[PDF 第 23 页 | A023_001_1] EN: anomalous samples. CFlow-AD is built upon a conditional normalizing flow framework and incorporates a pre-trained convolutional neural network for feature extraction. A multi-scale decoder estimates the likelihood of feature vectors, enabling fast and accurate anomaly detection and localization. Wan et al. (2022) propose position-enhanced feature mapping (PEFM), a novel image anomaly detection method designed to tackle the challenges of automated visual inspection in intelligent manufacturing systems. PEFM addresses detection performance and efficiency issues for various anomalies with different sizes, shapes, and colors. It integrates position encoding and feature mapping techniques to detect and localize anomalous regions. PEFM leverages pre-trained convolutional neural networks to extract and map image features. The mapping residuals between features extracted by different pre-trained convolutional neural networks are used as anomaly detection scores. Zheng et al. (2022) propose a coarse-to-fine non-contrastive learning method to address the challenge of detecting subtle and fine-grained anomalies in high-resolution images for industrial applications. This framework employs a coarse-to-fine alignment process. First, coarse alignment is performed at both the image and feature levels to standardize the pixel positions of objects. Then, during the fine alignment stage, a self-supervised learning approach-non-contrastive learning-is used to maximize the feature similarity at all corresponding positions within a batch, enabling the learning of a compact and semantically rich representation of normal images.  
[PDF 第 23 页 | A023_001_1] 中文：Wan等人(2022年)提出了定位强化地貌图(PEFM),这是一种新颖的图像异常检测方法,旨在应对智能制造系统中自动视觉检查的挑战。 它结合了位置编码和地貌制图技术,以探测异常区域并将其定位;PEFM利用预先训练的神经神经网络提取和绘制图像特征;将各种事先训练的神经网络所采集的特征之间的绘图残留物用作异常检测分数;Zheng等人(2022年)建议采用粗略至细微的非毗连性学习方法,以应对在高分辨率图像中发现用于工业应用的微妙和细微异常现象的挑战。 此框架使用粗略到精密的对齐进程。 首先, 粗略的对齐在图像和特性级别上进行, 以使对象的像素位置标准化。 然后, 在精细的对齐阶段, 一个自监督的学习方法- 非争议性学习程序 用于在批量内所有相应位置最大限度地实现特征相似性, 从而能够学习常规图像的缩写和语义上的丰富表达 。

[PDF 第 23 页 | A023_001_2] EN: Since this method does not require any prior assumptions about anomalous samples, it enhances the model’s generalization across a wide range of anomalies and improves both anomaly detection accuracy and localization precision. Kamoona et al. (2024) propose a lightweight industrial defect detection method based on feature energy in the random finite set (RFS) framework. This method addresses the limitations of traditional global feature extraction techniques, which are not robust to lighting variations and viewing angle changes in manufacturing and fail to fully utilize geometric information. Kamoona et al. introduce transfer learning to process local features, modeling them as RFSs. They define RFS energy as the anomaly score and model the point pattern feature similarity distribution of normal samples using a multivariate Gaussian distribution. This approach enables anomaly detection parameter learning with low computational cost and significantly enhances anomaly detection accuracy. Rudolph et al. (2022) propose CS-Flow, a fully convolutional cross-scale normalization flow method designed for automatic defect detection in industrial manufacturing, particularly in scenarios where defect-free samples are unavailable. CS-Flow achieves high-precision defect detection at the image level by jointly processing feature maps at different scales and leveraging normalizing flow to flexibly estimate the density of input samples and assign probability values to them. Zhou et al. (2024) propose MSFlow, a multi-scale flow model framework designed to address the detection challenges posed by abnormal size variations in unsupervised anomaly detection.  
[PDF 第 23 页 | A023_001_2] 中文：由于这一方法不需要事先对异常抽样作任何假设,它加强了模型在各种反常现象中的概括性,提高了异常检测的准确性和定位精确度。 Kamoona等人(2024年)提出了一种基于随机限量集框架特征能量的轻量工业缺陷检测方法。 这种方法解决了传统的全球地物提取技术的局限性,这些技术不适于照明变化和观察制造业的角度变化,也没有充分利用几何信息。 Kamoona等人介绍了处理本地特征的转移学习,将其建模为RFS。他们将RFS能源定义为异常分,并用多变量分布模式模拟正常样本的相似性分布。这一方法使得以低计算成本进行异常检测参数学习并大大提高异常度检测的准确性。Rudolph等人(2022年)提出了CS-Flow方案,这是一种完全渐进式的跨比例归一化流法,旨在在工业制造中自动发现缺陷,特别是在没有无瑕疵样品的情况下。 CS-Flow通过在不同尺度上联合处理地貌图,利用归一化流来灵活估计输入样品的密度并给它们分配概率值,在图像层面实现了高精度缺陷检测。 Zhou 等人(2024年)提出了MSFlow,这是一个多级流量模型框架,旨在应对无监督的异常异常异常检测中因异常大小变化而造成的检测挑战。

[PDF 第 23 页 | A023_001_3] EN: MSFlow enhances generalization ability to abnormal size changes by incorporating heterogeneous parallel flows and fusion flows, which capture features and facilitate multi-scale information exchange. MSFlow achieves significant performance improvements in both image-level and pixel-level anomaly detection and localization tasks. Cao et al. (2023) propose collaborative difference optimization (CDO) to mitigate the overgeneralization problem in unsupervised image anomaly detection, which arises due to the high generalization ability of convolutional neural networks. CDO leverages synthetic anomalies to refine feature difference distributions by optimizing the boundary and overlap between normal and anomalous samples. It incorporates a boundary optimization module and an overlap optimization module to  
[PDF 第 23 页 | A023_001_3] 中文：Cao等人(2023年)提出协作差异优化办法,以缓解在未受监督的图像异常检测中出现的超常普及问题,这是由于动态神经网络具有高度普遍化能力而产生的。 CDO 利用合成异常,通过优化正常和异常样本之间的边界和重叠,改进特征差异分布。

<a id="pdf-page-24"></a>

## PDF 第 24 页

[查看原 PDF 第 24 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=24>)

[PDF 第 24 页 | A024_001_1] EN: enhance the separation between normal and anomalous features, reducing feature overlap and improving both anomaly detection accuracy and localization reliability. To tackle the challenge of defect localization in industrial inspection caused by uncontrollable factors, Lei et al. (2023) propose PyramidFlow, which introduces a defect comparison and localization paradigm based on potential templates, combined with a pyramid-based normalizing flow for multi-scale fusion and volume normalization. Without relying on external priors, PyramidFlow effectively enhances model generalization while achieving high-resolution defect localization. Silva et al. (2024) propose AttentDifferNet, which enhances defect detection and classification in industrial inspection images by integrating an attention module into the DifferNet architecture. AttentDifferNet significantly improves model performance across multiple industrial inspection datasets by incorporating SENet and CBAM attention modules. Milkovic et al. (2024) propose FRAnomaly, a fast image anomaly detection method based on normalizing flows. FRAnomaly reduces model parameters and inference time by utilizing multiple feature sets at a single scale and a normalizing flow model specifically designed for these features. FRAnomaly effectively addresses the limitations of traditional anomaly detection methods, which suffer from insufficient performance and high model complexity, achieving efficient and accurate image anomaly detection.  
[PDF 第 24 页 | A024_001_1] 中文：为了应对由于无法控制的因素造成的工业检查中的缺陷定位的挑战,莱等人(2023年)提议采用基于潜在模板的缺陷比较和定位模式,同时采用基于金字塔的标准化流程,实现多级聚变和体积正常化。 Silva等人(2024年)提议采用Attent DifferNet,通过将一个关注模块纳入DifferNet结构,加强工业检查图像中的缺陷检测和分类。Attent DifferNet通过纳入Senet和CBAM关注模块,大大改进了多个工业检查数据集的模型性能。Milkovic等人(2024年)提出基于归一化流程的快速图像异常检测方法FRAnomaly。 FrAnoma通过使用单一规模的多重特征集和专门为这些特征设计的归一化流程模型,减少了模型参数和推断时间。 FRANOMA有效解决了传统异常检测方法的局限性,这些方法因性能不足和模型高度复杂而受到影响,从而实现了有效和准确的图像异常检测。

[PDF 第 24 页 | A024_002_1] EN: 2.2.4 Memory bank  
[PDF 第 24 页 | A024_002_1] 中文：2.2.4 记忆库

[PDF 第 24 页 | A024_003_1] EN: Memory Bank, as an innovative long-term memory mechanism, has demonstrated unique advantages in industrial visual anomaly detection. By simulating human memory behavior, it provides a more intelligent and efficient solution for anomaly detection. Figure 6 illustrates the architecture of the memory bank-based approach. The core of memory bank lies in its adaptive memory updating mechanism. By recording and storing interaction data during industrial anomaly detection, it enables long-term retention and utilization of relevant information, thereby enhancing both detection accuracy and efficiency. Given an industrial image I, a pre-trained feature extraction network is first applied to map the image to feature space, obtaining a feature vector .  
[PDF 第 24 页 | A024_003_1] 中文：记忆库作为一种创新的长期记忆机制,在工业视觉异常检测方面显示出独特的优势。通过模拟人类记忆行为,它为异常检测提供了更聪明和更有效的解决方案。图6展示了以记忆库为基础的方法的架构。记忆库的核心在于其适应性记忆更新机制。通过记录和存储工业异常检测过程中的互动数据,它可以长期保存和利用相关信息,从而提高检测准确度和效率。根据工业图像I,先用预先训练的特征提取网络来绘制图像定位空间,获取特性矢量。

## PDF 第 26 页

[查看原 PDF 第 26 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=26>)

[PDF 第 26 页 | A026_001_1] EN: where α and β are weighting parameters.  
[PDF 第 26 页 | A026_001_1] 中文：其中α和β是加权参数。

[PDF 第 26 页 | A026_002_1] EN: In recent years, research on the application of memory bank in industrial visual anomaly detection has gradually gained attention. Researchers have propose a series of unsupervised anomaly detection methods based on memory enhancement mechanisms and explored a memory-enhanced state-space model that integrates memory bank with a state-space framework. These studies aim to enhance the accuracy and efficiency of industrial visual anomaly detection and further drive the intelligentization and automation of industrial production. Defard et al. (2021) propose Patch Distribution Modeling (PaDiM), an industrial image anomaly detection and localization framework based on one-class learning. PaDiM employs a cosine-trained convolutional neural networks to extract embedding vectors from image patches and models the normal category distribution using a multivariate Gaussian distribution. Additionally, PaDiM captures correlations across different semantic levels of the CNN to enhance anomaly localization accuracy. Roth et al. (2022) propose PatchCore, which utilizes locally-aware, mid-level feature patches extracted from a pre-trained ImageNet model and employs a core-set reduction technique to construct a compact yet highly representative memory bank. This approach enables precise anomaly detection and localization while ensuring efficient inference speeds. Tsai et al. (2022) propose MSP, a multi-scale patch-based representation learning method designed to address the challenges of detecting small or complex-textured abnormal regions in image anomaly detection and segmentation.  
[PDF 第 26 页 | A026_002_1] 中文：近年来,关于将记忆库应用于工业视觉异常检测的研究逐渐引起人们的注意,研究人员根据记忆增强机制提出了一系列无监督的异常检测方法,并探索了一种将记忆库与州空间框架相结合的增强记忆状态-空间模型。这些研究旨在提高工业视觉异常检测的准确性和效率并进一步推动工业生产的智能化和自动化。Defard等人(2021年)提出了帕奇分布模型(PadiM),这是一个以一等学习为基础的工业图像异常检测和定位框架。 PadiM 使用一个通过连线训练的神经神经网络,利用多变高斯分布法从图像图像块中提取嵌入矢量和模型作为正常分类分布法。此外, Pidima 捕捉CNN不同语义层的关联性,以提高异常定位精确度。 Roth等人(2022年)提出PatchCore 方案,其中使用了从预先训练的图像网模式中提取的当地觉悟、中等级功能图像块,并采用了核心集缩放技术来构建一个紧凑但具有高度代表性的记忆库。 Tsai等人(2022年)建议采用多尺度的图像块代议制学习方法,以应对在图像异常检测和分割中发现小型或复杂文字异常区域的挑战。

[PDF 第 26 页 | A026_002_2] EN: MSP enhances feature representation capability by sliding image patches at different scales to extract representative features, while simultaneously capturing both global and local contextual information. Additionally, MSP optimizes the loss function by incorporating K-Means clustering, cosine similarity, and self-supervised learning strategies, enabling the model to effectively learn feature representations from normal samples. Sungwook et al. (2022) propose coupled-hypersphere-based feature adaptation (CFA), an anomaly detection method designed to address the feature bias problem in traditional anomaly localization methods. CFA enhances anomaly detection and localization accuracy by adapting object dataset features through transfer learning, learnable patch descriptions, and an extensible memory bank that operates independently of the object dataset size. This enables CFA to accurately differentiate between normal and anomalous features. McIntosh and Albu (2023) propose InReaCh, an unsupervised anomaly detection method designed to overcome the limitations of traditional image anomaly detection, which relies on clean training data. InReaCh constructs channels by associating high-confidence conventional patches from the training data and develops a model based on these channels to evaluate new patches. Bae et al. (2023) propose PNI, an industrial anomaly detection and localization algorithm designed to address the lack of location and neighborhood information in industrial anomaly detection. PNI models the distribution of normal features by incorporating location and neighborhood information, utilizing a multilayer perceptron (MLP) network and histograms.

Additionally, it integrates PDM with an information compression module (ICM) to enable accurate reconstruction of diverse normal patterns and reliable severity measurement of anomalies without relying on external estimators. Kim et al. (2023) propose fast adaptive patch memory (FAPM), an industrial anomaly detection method designed to address the insufficient inference speed of existing approaches. FAPM leverages local and hierarchical memory banks to store embedded image features and employs local adaptive core-set sampling to eliminate redundant computations. This approach significantly enhances inference speed while preserving anomaly detection accuracy. Xie et al. (2023) propose GraphCore, a novel anomaly detection method that enables efficient anomaly detection with a limited number of samples. GraphCore leverages a graph neural network to extract visual isometric invariant features, enhancing detection performance under data-scarce conditions. To address the challenges in industrial product image defect detection, including the scarcity of defect samples and unclear classification standards, Yongheng et al. (2023) propose self-updating memory and central clustering (SMCC), a novel unsupervised anomaly detection and localization method. SMCC extracts image features using a pre-trained model and applies a Gaussian mixture model for clustering to obtain cluster centers, ensuring that normal sample features are tightly distributed around these centers to facilitate anomaly distinction.

[PDF 第 27 页 | A027_001_2] EN: Additionally, SMCC incorporates a self-updating memory bank to reduce memory usage and optimizes the pre-trained network parameters to adapt to the current dataset distribution, thereby achieving efficient anomaly detection and localization while significantly enhancing accuracy and robustness. Hu et al. (2024) propose DMAD, an anomaly detection framework designed to address the multi-class anomaly detection problem in real-world industrial scenarios. DMAD enhances feature representation and learns anomaly scores by constructing a dual memory bank-comprising a normal memory bank and an anomaly memory bank-to compute feature distances and feature attentions. By integrating unsupervised and semi-supervised learning, DMAD significantly improves anomaly detection accuracy and robustness. Zhou et al. (2024) propose VQ-Flow, a method designed to mitigate performance degradation caused by pattern mixing among different categories in multi-class anomaly detection. VQ-Flow incorporates hierarchical vector quantization, enabling a normalizing flow model to differentiate between distinct data concepts without requiring category labels while capturing concept-specific normal patterns. VQ-Flow employs a conceptual prototype codebook to distinguish concepts and a concept-specific pattern codebook to learn concept-specific normal patterns. The model simulates complex multi-class data distributions by modeling conditional distributions in a concept-aware manner using a Gaussian mixture model. This approach facilitates effective modeling and anomaly detection in multi-class settings. Zuo et al.

[PDF 第 28 页 | A028_001_1] EN: handle anomaly scores across different scales. PointCore reduces computational costs and interference during inference, thereby enhancing anomaly detection accuracy and efficiency. Shuai et al. (2024) propose reducing biases in representation (REB), an industrial anomaly detection method designed to address performance limitations caused by domain bias and local density bias in feature representations of pre-trained models. REB enhances anomaly detection accuracy by mitigating domain bias through self-supervised learning tasks and the DefectMaker defect generation strategy, while reducing local density bias using local-density KNN, which normalizes distances based on a local density model. Hyun et al. (2024) propose ReConPatch, a method designed to address the challenge of rare and unknown product defects in industrial manufacturing. ReConPatch utilizes contrastive representation learning to train a linearly modulated patch feature representation, incorporating pairwise and contextual similarities as pseudo labels. This enables the construction of a goal-oriented, easily separable feature space, thereby achieving high-performance industrial anomaly detection. Lee et al. (2022) propose CRAD, a novel anomaly detection method designed to address the poor generalization ability, identity shortcut, and high complexity issues in multi-class anomaly detection. CRAD enhances generalization ability and mitigates the identity shortcut problem by mapping spatial features onto coordinates in a continuous grid, fusing local and global normal features, and employing feature refinement techniques to achieve an effective representation of normal features in continuous space.  
[PDF 第 28 页 | A028_001_1] 中文：Shuai等人(2024年)提议减少表现上的偏差(REB),这是一种工业异常检测方法,旨在解决因区域偏差造成的性能限制和在预先训练的模型特征显示方面当地密度偏差。 REB通过自监督的学习任务和DeffectMaker缺陷生成战略来减轻地域偏差,从而增强异常检测准确性,同时使用本地密度KNN降低局部密度偏差,后者根据本地密度模式使距离正常化。 Hyun等人(2024年)提议采用ReConPatch,这是一个旨在应对工业制造中稀有和未知产品缺陷的挑战的方法。 ReConPatch利用对比化的演示学习来训练线性调节的图像块特征代表,将对称和背景相似性作为假标签纳入其中。这可以构建一个面向目标、容易分离的功能空间,从而实现高性能的工业异常检测。Lee等人(2022年)提出CRAD,这是一种新颖的异常检测方法,旨在解决多级异常检测中一般化能力差、身份快捷和高度复杂的问题。 CRAD通过在连续网格坐标上绘制空间地物图、锁定当地和全球的正常地物以及利用地物精细技术实现连续空间正常地物的有效体现,提高一般化能力并减轻身份捷径问题。

[PDF 第 28 页 | A028_001_2] EN: Additionally, CRAD reduces storage and computational complexity. Koshil et al. (2024) propose AnomalousPatchCore (APC), which rely solely on normal samples and fail to fully utilize anomalous sample information. APC effectively leverages both normal and anomalous samples by fine-tuning a feature extractor and integrating three auxiliary tasks: classification, segmentation, and reconstruction. Wang et al. (2024) propose MemoryMamba, a memory-enhanced state-space model designed to overcome the limitations of existing defect recognition models in handling complex and limited defect data, particularly in imbalanced or data-scarce scenarios. MemoryMamba enhances defect recognition accuracy and robustness by integrating state-space techniques with memory-enhancement mechanisms. MemoryMamba employs coarse-grained and fine-grained memory encoding modules to capture and encode defect-hierarchy features while optimizing the memory network through contrastive learning and mutual information maximization strategies. He et al. (2024) propose MambaAD, which is designed to address the challenges of capturing long-range dependencies and the high computational complexity encountered by traditional convolution neural networks and transformers in anomaly detection. MambaAD employs a novel decoder built on the Mamba architecture, which integrates global and local feature extraction through local state-space augmentation modules and hybrid state-space (HSS) blocks.

[PDF 第 29 页 | A029_054_1] EN: neously takes an image x and its corresponding textual description as input. Through its image and text encoder, the image and text are mapped into a shared embedding space. A contrastive loss  
[PDF 第 29 页 | A029_054_1] 中文：错误地将图像 x 及其相应的文字描述作为输入。 通过图像和文本编码器, 图像和文本被映射到共享的嵌入空间 。

[PDF 第 29 页 | A029_057_1] EN: applied to pull matched image-text pairs closer in this feature space and push mismatched pairs farther apart, thus achieving cross-modal alignment. Inference phase: Given a new image and text, the model’s image and text encoder each generate feature representations (Fimage and Ftext), and a similarity score is computed. If the similarity score falls below a certain threshold, it indicates a significant discrepancy between the image and its textual description, and the input is flagged as anomalous  
[PDF 第 29 页 | A029_057_1] 中文：用于拉动与此特性相匹配的图像- 文本配对, 并将不匹配的配对进一步分隔, 从而实现交叉式对齐 。 推论阶段 : 在新的图像和文本中, 模型的图像和文本编码器各自生成特征表示式( Fimage 和 Ftext) , 并计算相似性评分 。 如果相似性评分低于某个阈值, 它会显示图像与其文字描述之间的巨大差异, 而输入会被标记为异常 。

[PDF 第 30 页 | A030_083_1] EN: Contrastive learning has recently emerged as a powerful approach in industrial anomaly detection, particularly through vision-language alignment. Unlike traditional self-supervised learning methods such as image reconstruction, pseudo-anomaly generation, or masked modeling, contrastive learning builds discriminative embedding spaces by constructing positive and negative pairs. Models like CLIP and WinCLIP leverage multimodal supervision, enabling zero-shot anomaly detection and demonstrating strong generalization across object categories and domain shifts. On the other hand, traditional self-supervised methods focus on modeling intra-image structure or reconstructing visual content to detect anomalies. These methods often perform well in single-modal, structured environments and are well-suited to tasks with clearly defined defect types.  
[PDF 第 30 页 | A030_083_1] 中文：反向学习最近成为了工业异常检测的有力方法,特别是通过视觉语言校正。 与传统自监督的学习方法不同,如图像重建、假异常生成或蒙面模型制作,对比式学习通过构建正对和负对构建了歧视性嵌入空间。 类似CLIP和WinCLIP等模型利用了多式监管,使得零发异常检测得以实现,并展示出在对象类别和域变之间强烈的概括化。 另一方面,传统的自监督方法侧重于模拟内部图像结构或者重建视觉内容以发现异常现象。 这些方法往往在单一模式、结构化的环境中运作良好,适合明确界定缺陷类别的任务。

[PDF 第 30 页 | A030_084_1] EN: Recently, researchers have started to explore how contrastive learning-particularly vision-language models-can be adapted to industrial contexts to support semantic anomaly detection and localization. One representative work in this direction is WinCLIP, which builds on the CLIP foundation and introduces mechanisms specifically tailored to anomaly segmentation tasks. Specifically, Jeong et al. (2023) propose WinCLIP, which is built upon the vision-language model CLIP and achieves zero-shot anomaly segmentation aligned with  
[PDF 第 30 页 | A030_084_1] 中文：最近,研究人员开始探索如何将对比式学习(特别是愿景语言模型)适用于工业环境,以支持语义异常检测和定位。 一位代表在这方面开展工作的是WinCLIP。 WinCLIP以CLIP基金会为基础,引入了专门针对异常分解任务的机制。

<a id="pdf-page-31"></a>

## PDF 第 31 页

[查看原 PDF 第 31 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=31>)

[PDF 第 31 页 | A031_001_1] EN: text by integrating compositional prompting and multi-scale feature extraction and aggregation. Additionally, Jeong et al. introduce WinCLIP+, an enhanced version that further improves classification and segmentation accuracy by incorporating additional information from normal images. Tamura (2023) propose RWDA, which requires large numbers of anomaly samples and a separate model for each object category. RWDA leverages the CLIP as a data source and applies random data augmentation to generate diverse text embeddings as training data. These embeddings are then used to train a feedforward neural network, enabling the classification of normal and abnormal samples without requiring category-specific training images or a cumbersome prompt integration process. Chen et al. (2023) propose CLIP-AD, a zero-shot anomaly detection framework designed to address the challenge of detecting anomalies without reference images for the test object. CLIP-AD leverages the zero-shot capabilities of the large-scale CLIP and enhances text prompt design through the representative vector selection paradigm. Additionally, it introduces a structured feature modification strategy to construct a staged dual-path (SDP) model for handling abnormality graph computation. To further address feature misalignment, a fine-tuned linear layer (SDP+) is incorporated, enabling efficient zero-shot anomaly detection. To tackle the challenge of unified zero-shot anomaly detection and localization, Deng et al.  
[PDF 第 31 页 | A031_001_1] 中文：此外,Jeong等人介绍了WinCLIP+,这是一个强化版本,通过纳入正常图像中的额外信息进一步提高分类和分解准确性。Tamura (2023年) 提议使用RWDA,这需要大量异常样本和针对每个对象类别的单独模型。 RWDA将CLIP作为数据源,并随机应用增强数据生成多种文字嵌入的训练数据。 这些嵌入器随后用于训练进料神经网络,使正常和异常样本的分类无需特定类别的训练图像或繁琐的即时集成过程。Chen等人(2023年)建议采用零射反常检测框架,以解决在不为试验对象提供参考图像的情况下发现异常的挑战。 CLIP-AD利用大型CLIP的零射速能力,并通过具有代表性的矢量选择模式加强文本快速设计。 此外,它引入了结构化功能修改战略,以构建一个处理异常图解的分级双轨模型。为了进一步解决特征不匹配问题,还采用了微调线性层(SDP+),从而能够有效地检测零射异常。

[PDF 第 31 页 | A031_001_2] EN: (2023) propose AnoCLIP, which extracts local features from the CLIP model by introducing a train-free value-to-value attention mechanism and designs a domain-aware contrastive state prompt template to enhance vision-language alignment. Additionally, AnoCLIP incorporates a fast test-time adaptation mechanism that optimizes the alignment between visual markers and text prompts, significantly improving the accuracy of anomaly detection and localization. Zhou et al. (2024) propose AnomalyCLIP, an industrial anomaly detection method designed to address the challenge of model generalization to diverse domain-specific anomaly patterns in zero-shot anomaly detection through text prompt learning without object semantics. AnomalyCLIP learns object-agnostic text prompts and leverages the large-scale pre-trained CLIP to capture general normalcy and abnormality in images. This enables the model to focus on anomalous regions rather than object semantics, thereby achieving generalized anomaly recognition across various object categories. Additionally, AnomalyCLIP incorporates global and local context optimization as well as text and visual space alignment, significantly enhancing anomaly detection and segmentation performance across diverse datasets. Cai et al. (2024) propose CLIP-ADA, an anomaly detection framework designed to address the challenges of high resource consumption and low localization accuracy in multi-class industrial anomaly detection. This is achieved by introducing learnable prompts and an anomaly region refinement strategy.  
[PDF 第 31 页 | A031_001_2] 中文：(2023年)提议采用AnoCLIP,该模式通过引入无火车价值对价值注意力机制从CLIP模型中提取当地特征,并设计一个能识别域上对比状态的快速模板,以加强视觉语言对齐。此外,AnoCLIP包含一个快速测试时间适应机制,优化视觉标记和文字提示之间的匹配,大大提高异常检测和定位的准确性。Zhou等人(2024年)提出AnolyCLIP,这是一种工业异常检测方法,旨在应对模型一般化的挑战,使其在零发式异常检测过程中,通过不使用物体语义的文本快速学习来发现不同域特有的异常情况。 反常CLIP 学习对象不可知性文字会促进和利用经过预先训练的大型 CLIP 来捕捉图像的一般正常和异常情况,从而使模型能够侧重于异常区域而不是对象语义学,从而实现不同对象类别之间的普遍异常现象识别。此外, AnolyCLIP 包含全局和局部环境优化以及文本和视觉空间对齐,大大加强了不同数据集中的异常检测和分化功能。 Cai等人(2024年)提议建立一个异常检测框架,以应对多级工业异常检测中高资源消耗率和低定位准确度的挑战,通过引入可学习的提示和反常区域完善战略实现这一目标。

[PDF 第 31 页 | A031_001_3] EN: CLIP-ADA incorporates learnable textual prompts and associates them with anomalous patterns through self-supervised learning, enabling unified anomaly detection across multiple categories. Additionally, CLIP-ADA introduces a coarseto-fine anomaly region refinement strategy, utilizing the initial coarse localization results as an attention map to enhance the model’s ability to accurately identify and localize anomaly regions. Li et al. (2024) propose PromptAD, an industrial anomaly detection method designed to address the limitations of traditional anomaly detection approaches, which rely heavily on manually designed prompts and lack automation capabilities. PromptAD leverages semantic concatenation to transform normal sample prompts into abnormal sample prompts and constructs a large number of negative samples to facilitate prompt learning in single-class settings. Additionally, PromptAD introduces the concept of explicit anomaly margin, which explicitly regulates the distance between normal and abnormal prompt fea-  
[PDF 第 31 页 | A031_001_3] 中文：CLIP-ADA纳入了可学习的文字提示,并通过自监督的学习将其与异常模式联系起来,从而能够对多种类别进行统一的异常检测。 此外,CLIP-ADA引入了粗糙的异常区域改进战略,利用最初粗糙的定位结果作为关注地图,提高模型准确识别异常地区并将其定位的能力。 Li等人(2024年)提出了一种工业异常检测方法,该方法旨在解决传统异常检测方法的局限性,这些方法严重依赖人工设计的快速和缺乏自动化能力。 提示AD 利用语义解析法将正常样本导出变成异常样本,并构建了大量负面样本,以便利在单级环境中迅速学习。 此外,提示AD 引入了明显异常差值的概念,明确调节了正常和异常快速-

[PDF 第 32 页 | A032_001_1] EN: tures through hyperparameters, thereby enabling effective anomaly detection using only normal samples. To accurately detect and localize anomalies in industrial products without a priori samples for the target category, Gu et al. (2024) propose a zero-shot anomaly detection method called FiLo, which leverages fine-grained descriptions from adaptive learning and location-enhanced high-quality localization. It utilizes a large language model to generate detailed anomaly descriptions and incorporates a multi-scale, multi-shape cross-modal interaction module to enhance the localization capability for anomalies of various sizes and shapes. These enhancements significantly improve the accuracy and interpretability of zero-shot anomaly detection and localization. To address the challenge of accurately identifying and localizing anomalous patterns in visual data without training samples from the target dataset, Zhang et al. (2024) propose dual-image enhanced CLIP (DiE-CLIP), which significantly improves anomaly classification and localization performance by utilizing paired images as visual references and integrating the joint visual-linguistic scoring system of the CLIP model. Additionally, DiE-CLIP introduces a test-time adaptation model, which further enhances localization capability by synthesizing anomalies, thereby achieving substantial improvements in zero-shot anomaly detection and localization. Lee et al. (2024) propose AnoPLe, a multimodal prompting learning method designed to address the challenge of anomalous sample scarcity in small-sample anomaly detection. AnoPLe detects anomalies through bidirectional prompting learning and multimodal interaction.  
[PDF 第 32 页 | A032_001_1] 中文：为准确检测和定位工业产品中的异常现象,没有目标类别的先验样本,Gu等人(2024年)提出了一种名为FiLo的零发异常检测方法,该方法利用适应性学习和地点强化的高质量定位的细微描述。它使用一个大型语言模型生成详细的异常特征描述,并包含一个多尺度、多形状跨模式互动模块,以提高不同大小和形状异常现象的定位能力。 这些改进极大地提高了零射异常检测和定位的准确性和可解释性,为了应对在没有目标数据集的训练样本的情况下准确识别视觉数据中的异常模式并将其定位的挑战,张等人(2024年)提出双图像强化 CLIP(DiE-CLIP),通过将配对图像用作视觉参考和整合CLIP模型的视觉语言评分系统,大大改善了异常分类和定位性能。 此外,DiE-CLIP还引入了测试时间适应模型,该模型通过合成异常现象进一步提高了定位能力,从而大大改善了零发异常检测和定位。 Lee等人(2024年)提出了AnoPLe方案,这是一个多式促进学习的方法,旨在解决小样异常检测中异常样本稀缺的挑战。

[PDF 第 32 页 | A032_001_2] EN: It leverages simulated anomalies and lightweight decoders to enhance local semantic understanding, while improving image-level anomaly detection performance by integrating global and local semantics. Li et al. (2024) propose a zero-shot industrial anomaly detection framework IAD-CLIP based on visual language models. The framework contains a pre-trained CLIP model, a training-free adaptation module and a test-time adaptation mechanism. The training-free adaptation module uses a value-value attention mechanism and a state prompt space. The pre-trained CLIP model is used for feature extraction and the training-free adaptation module processes the extracted features through visual coders and text encoders for anomaly detection and localization.  
[PDF 第 32 页 | A032_001_2] 中文：它利用模拟异常和轻量分解器来提高当地语义理解,同时通过整合全局和局部语义来改进图像层面的异常检测性能。Li等人(2024年)提出基于视觉语言模型的零射工业异常检测框架IAD-CLIP。该框架包含一个经过预先训练的CLIP模型、一个没有训练的适应模块和一个测试时间适应机制。无训练的适应模块使用价值注意力机制和州快速空间。 预先训练的CLIP模型用于地貌提取和无训练的适应模块处理通过视觉编码器和文字编码器提取的特征,以便发现异常并进行定位。

[PDF 第 32 页 | A032_002_1] EN: 2.2.6 Anomaly clustering  
[PDF 第 32 页 | A032_002_1] 中文：2.2.6 异常集群

[PDF 第 32 页 | A032_003_1] EN: Wan et al. (2021) propose Gaussian clustering pre-trained features (GCPF), a framework designed to address the computational complexity and memory consumption challenges in industrial anomaly localization. GCPF extracts features using a pre-trained convolutional neural network and achieves pixel-level anomaly localization through multi-independent multivariate Gaussian clustering (MIMGC) and multi-level anomaly scoring. By significantly reducing computational time and memory storage requirements while improving anomaly localization accuracy, GCPF enhances practical applicability in industrial settings. Sohn et al. (2023) propose a framework that leverages pre-trained deep embeddings and off-the-shelf clustering methods to address the challenge of clustering different types of anomalies in abnormal images. The framework measures image similarity by defining a distance function based on patch embeddings, where the similarity weights are computed by weighted averaging the Euclidean distances of the embeddings. These weights are estimated in an unsupervised or semi-supervised manner to effectively highlight defective regions. Zhang et al. (2023) propose JRCC-Net, a segmentation network designed for unsupervised surface anomaly detection. JRCC-Net learns the decision boundary by leveraging  
[PDF 第 32 页 | A032_003_1] 中文：Wan等人(2021年)提出了Gausian聚类预先训练的特征(GCPF),这是一个旨在解决工业异常化中的计算复杂性和内存消耗挑战的框架;GCPF利用事先训练的进化神经网络提取特征,并通过多独立多变高斯聚类和多级异常评分实现像素级异位定位。通过大量减少计算时间和内存存储要求,同时提高反常性定位准确度,GCPF加强了在工业环境中的实际适用性。 Sohn等人(2023年)提出一个框架,利用预先训练的深嵌入和现成组合方法应对在异常图像中分组不同类型异常的挑战。 框架通过基于图像块嵌入的距离函数来测量图像相似性, 该选项以嵌入的欧几里德平均距离加权计算相近性权重。 这些权重是以一种无监督或半监督的方式估算的,以有效突出有缺陷的区域。 Zhang等人(2023年)提议建立JRCC-Net,这是一个用于无监督地表异常检测的分离网络。

[PDF 第 33 页 | A033_001_1] EN: the distance difference between simulated abnormal and normal samples, incorporating a simulated anomaly strategy, a joint representation, and a contrastive clustering method to enable anomaly detection during inference. Yao et al. (2024) propose hierarchical Gaussian mixture normal flow (HGAD), a modeling method designed to address the uniform mapping problem in normalizing flow-based anomaly detection methods for unified anomaly detection tasks. HGAD enhances the model’s ability to learn multi-class normal data distributions by integrating inter-class Gaussian mixture modeling and intra-class mixed class center learning, preventing performance degradation caused by mapping different classes to the same Gaussian prior. Additionally, HGAD introduces mutual information maximization loss to improve class distinction and enhance the model’s discriminative capability.  
[PDF 第 33 页 | A033_001_1] 中文：模拟异常样本与正常样本之间的距离差异（承接上文）；该方法结合模拟异常策略、联合表示和对比聚类方法，以便在推理时进行异常检测。Yao 等（2024）提出层次化高斯混合归一化流模型 HGAD，用于解决统一异常检测任务中基于归一化流的方法存在的统一映射问题。HGAD 将类间高斯混合建模与类内混合类别中心学习结合起来，增强模型学习多类别正常数据分布的能力，避免将不同类别映射到同一个高斯先验所导致的性能下降。此外，HGAD 引入互信息最大化损失，改进类别区分并增强模型的判别能力。

[PDF 第 33 页 | A033_004_1] EN: Autoencoder is an unsupervised learning neural network model that compresses input data into a low-dimensional representation through an encoder-decoder structure and then reconstructs an output similar to the original data. Figure 8 illustrates the fundamental architecture of the reconstruction-based method. The objective is to minimize reconstruction error, thereby achieving an effective data representation. An Autoencoder consists of two main components: an encoder and a decoder. The encoder compresses the input data into a low-dimensional representation, while the decoder reconstructs it to approximate the original data as closely as possible. Given an input image I, an Autoencoder can be formulated as:

In industrial anomaly detection, the assumption is that normal images are well reconstructed by the autoencoder, whereas abnormal images show higher reconstruction errors. Thus, the reconstruction error serves as a criterion for determining whether an image is anomalous. A threshold ε is set, and if the reconstruction error exceeds this threshold, the image is classified as anomalous:

[PDF 第 34 页 | A034_021_1] 中文：表示自我编码器的参数时, Ii 是训练组中的i-th 图像, m 是训练样品的数量 。

[PDF 第 34 页 | A034_022_1] EN: In industrial anomaly detection, Autoencoders learn normal data representations and identify anomalous samples that significantly deviate from them. Their advantages include unsupervised learning, effective dimensionality reduction, feature extraction, and the ability to capture complex nonlinear relationships. However, a key limitation is that an overly complex model or an excessively large bottleneck layer may reconstruct the input too accurately, failing to capture critical features. To address these issues, researchers have proposed various Autoencoder variants, such as sparse Autoencoders, denoising Autoencoders, and variational Autoencoders Nguyen et al. (2024). These variants improve the performance and applicability of Autoencoders by introducing additional constraints and optimized objective functions. Zavrtanik et al. (2021) propose DRAEM, a surface anomaly detection method designed to identify local regions in an image that significantly deviates from normal appearance. DRAEM integrates a reconstruction subnetwork and a discriminative subnetwork to jointly learn representations of abnormal images and their normal reconstructions, while simultaneously defining the decision boundary between normal and abnormal samples. This enables direct anomaly localization, allowing for accurate detection and segmentation of anomalous regions without requiring complex post-processing steps. Zavrtanik et al. (2021) propose RIAD, a visual anomaly detection method based on image restoration. RIAD detects anomalies by segmenting an image, randomly removing certain regions, and then reconstructing these regions using a trained encoder-decoder network.  
[PDF 第 34 页 | A034_022_1] 中文：在工业异常检测中,自动算术者学会了正常的数据表达方式,并发现了与它们有明显差异的异常样本,其优点包括无监督的学习、有效的维度降低、特征提取以及捕捉复杂非线性关系的能力。然而,一个关键的局限性是过于复杂的模型或过大的瓶颈层可能太精确地重建输入,无法捕捉关键特征。为了解决这些问题,研究人员提出了各种自动编码变体,如稀疏的自编码器、自定义自编码器和变异式Autencoders Nguyen等人(2024年)。 这些变体通过引入额外的限制和优化的客观功能,改善了自编码器的性能和适用性。Zavrtanik等人(2021年)提出DRAEM,这是一种表面异常检测方法,旨在识别与正常外观明显不同的图像中的地方区域。 DRAEM整合了一个重建子网络和一个具有歧视性的子网络,以共同学习不正常图像的表现及其正常重建,同时界定正常样品和异常样品之间的决定界限。这可以直接地定位异常现象,从而可以在不需要复杂的后处理步骤的情况下准确探测和分解异常区域。 Zavrtanik等人(2021年)提议采用以图像恢复为基础的目视异常检测方法RIAD。 RIAD通过对图像进行分解、随机清除某些地区,然后使用训练有素的编码器-解码器网络重建这些地区,从而检测异常现象。

[PDF 第 34 页 | A034_022_2] EN: The key idea behind RIAD is that abnormal regions are difficult to reconstruct accurately because they were not observed during training, whereas normal regions are well reconstructed. By comparing the differences between the input image and the reconstructed image, RIAD effectively detects and localizes anomalies. Additionally, RIAD introduces a gradient similarity-based loss function to train the network, significantly improving visual anomaly detection performance. Shi et al. (2021) propose DFR, an unsupervised anomaly segmentation method based on deep feature reconstruction, designed to automatically detect and  
[PDF 第 34 页 | A034_022_2] 中文：RIAD背后的关键思想是,异常区域难以准确重建,因为训练期间没有观察到这些异常区域,而正常区域则得到了良好的重建。通过比较输入图像与重建图像之间的差别,RIAD有效地检测了反常现象并将其定位。此外,RIAD引入了一个基于梯度的类似损失函数来训练网络,大大改进视觉异常现象的探测性能。 Shi等人(2021年)提出DFR,这是建立在深度特征重建基础上的一种无监督的异常分化方法,旨在自动检测和定位。

## PDF 第 35 页

[PDF 第 35 页 | A035_001_1] EN: localize small, hard-to-detect anomaly regions in images without prior knowledge of the anomalies. DFR leverages a pre-trained deep convolutional network to extract multi-scale regional features and employs an efficient convolutional autoencoder to rapidly identify anomalies through feature reconstruction. Hou et al. (2021) propose divide-and-assemble anomaly detection (DAAD), a framework that formulates image reconstruction as a “divideand-assemble” process. DAAD introduces a multi-scale block-level memory module to regulate the reconstruction ability for normal and abnormal samples, while employing adversarial learning to enhance semantic feature representation, enabling the detection of anomalies in both image space and semantic feature space. DAAD effectively addresses the challenge of distinguishing normal and abnormal images in unsupervised anomaly detection scenarios where anomalous samples are unavailable. Yang et al. (2022) propose DFC, an unsupervised anomaly detection and segmentation method based on deep feature correspondence. DFC addresses the challenge of effectively detecting and segmenting abnormal structures in images when anomalous samples are scarce or unavailable. DFC constructs an asymmetric dual-network framework, comprising a feature extraction network and a feature estimation network. DFC employs a deep feature correspondence mechanism to model and evaluate feature consistency between the two network branches, thereby enabling anomaly detection and segmentation. Additionally, DFC introduces a self-feature enhancement strategy and a multi-context residual learning module, significantly enhancing robustness and detection performance.  
[PDF 第 35 页 | A035_001_1] 中文：将小型、难以探测的异常区域定位在图像中,而不事先了解异常情况。 DFR利用一个经过预先训练的深层革命网络来提取多级区域特征,并使用高效的革命自编码器通过特征重建迅速识别异常情况。 Hou等人(2021年)提出分数和组合式异常情况检测(DAD),这一框架将图像重建作为一种“分数和组合”进程。 DAAD引入了一个多尺度块级记忆模块,以调节正常和异常样本的重建能力,同时采用对抗性学习来增强语义特征的表达方式,从而能够检测图像空间和语义特征空间中的异常现象。DAD有效地应对了在无监督异常检测情景中区分正常和异常图像的挑战,因为不存在异常现象样本。Yang等人(2022年)提出了DFC,这是一种基于深度地物通信的无监督的异常检测和分解方法。 DFC处理在异常样品稀少或不存在的情况下有效探测和分解图像中的异常结构的挑战,DFC建立一个不对称的双网络框架,包括地物提取网络和一个地物估计网络,DFC使用一个深层地物对应机制来模拟和评价两个网络分支之间的地物一致性,从而能够发现和分解异常现象。此外,DFC还引入了一种自我性能增强战略和多文本剩余学习模块,大大加强稳健性和检测性能。

[PDF 第 35 页 | A035_001_2] EN: Zavrtanik et al. (2022) propose DSR, a novel unsupervised surface anomaly detection framework designed to overcome the limitations of existing methods in synthesizing anomalies that closely resemble normal patterns. Instead of relying on traditional image synthesis-based anomaly generation. DSR employs a quantized feature space representation and introduces two decoders to reconstruct general object appearance and specific object appearance separately, thereby achieving more robust anomaly detection and localization. Schlüter et al. (2022) propose natural synthetic anomalies (NSA), a self-supervised learning method designed for anomaly detection and localization using only normal training data. NSA generates natural and diverse synthetic anomalies by seamlessly blending scaled blocks from different images into the target image using Poisson image editing techniques. This approach eliminates the obvious discontinuities present in existing methods, thereby significantly enhancing anomaly detection and localization performance. To enhance the reconstruction and identification of anomalous samples in anomaly detection, Ristea et al. (2022) propose SSPCAB, a self-supervised pre-trained convolutional attention block. SSPCAB trains the model without requiring anomalous samples by predicting masked or missing information in the input data and using reconstruction error as an anomaly score. This approach significantly improves anomaly detection performance. To address the challenges of small sample availability and cross-product category adaptation in industrial anomaly detection, Fang et al.  
[PDF 第 35 页 | A035_001_2] 中文：Zavrtanik等人(2022年)提出DSR,这是一个新的无监督的表面异常检测框架,旨在克服现有方法在合成与正常模式非常相似的异常现象方面的局限性,而不是依赖传统的图像合成异常现象生成。DSR使用一个量化的地物空间代表器,并引入两个解码器以重建普通物体外观和特定物体外观,从而实现更强有力的异常检测和定位。Schlüter等人(2022年)提出了自然合成异常现象(NSA),这是一种自监督的学习方法,仅利用正常的训练数据用于异常检测和定位。 国家安全局使用 Poisson 图像编辑技术,将不同图像的块块无缝地混入目标图像,从而产生自然和多样合成异常现象。 这一方法消除了现有方法中存在的明显的不连续性,从而大大加强了异常检测和定位性能。 为了在异常检测中加强异常样本的重建和识别, Ristea等人(2022年)提出了由自监督、事先经过训练的共变关注区块SSPCAB。 国家安全局训练模型时不需要异常样本,方法是预测输入数据中的隐蔽或缺失信息,并利用重建错误作为异常分数。 这种方法大大改善了异常检测性能。

[PDF 第 35 页 | A035_001_3] EN: (2023) propose FastRecon, a method designed for rapid anomaly detection in new products under scarce data conditions. FastRecon employs feature reconstruction, leveraging a small number of normal samples to generate a normal version of the query sample. It then applies a regression algorithm with distribution regularization to determine the optimal transformation, ensuring that the reconstructed output remains both visually similar to the query sample and consistent with normal sample characteristics. Guo et al. (2023) propose THFR, a template-guided hierarchical feature restoration method designed to detect abnormalities of various sizes in complex normal patterns. THFR preserves normal features and filters anomalous features by introducing bottleneck  
[PDF 第 35 页 | A035_001_3] 中文：(2023) 提议采用快速查询方法,用于在数据稀缺的情况下对新产品进行快速异常检测。快速查询使用特征重建,利用少量正常样本生成正常的查询样本版本。然后运用分布正规化回归算法确定最佳转换,确保重整输出在视觉上与查询样本相似并与正常样本特性一致。Guo等人(2023) 提出THFR,这是一个模板引导的等级特征恢复方法,旨在探测复杂正常模式中不同尺寸的异常情况。 THFR通过引入瓶颈来保持正常特性和过滤器异常特征

[PDF 第 36 页 | A036_001_1] EN: compression. It then restores distorted features through a template-guided compensation mechanism, converting anomalous features into normal representations. THFR enables accurate detection and localization of abnormalities across different scales. Madan and Ristea (2023) propose SSMCTB, an anomaly detection method that masks input information at any layer of the neural network and reconstructs the masked data using 3D convolutions and transformer blocks. SSMCTB is trained exclusively on normal samples, eliminating the need for anomalous data. Park et al. (2024) propose loss amplification (LAMP), a method that enhances anomaly detection performance by modifying the loss function without altering the neural network architecture. LAMP steepens the loss landscape, leading to larger reconstruction errors for unseen anomalous patterns, thereby improving detection in unsupervised anomaly detection settings. Chen et al. (2024) propose implicit foregroundguided network (IFgNet) to mitigate background noise misclassification in industrial anomaly detection. IFgNet adopts a multi-task learning framework, integrating foreground detection and image reconstruction tasks. By employing shared networks and task-specific attention mechanisms, IFgNet learns to distinguish foreground from background, thereby reducing background interference and enhancing anomaly detection accuracy. Liu and Wang (2024) propose multi-category anomaly autoencoder (MAAE), a method designed to address high storage costs, low training efficiency, and performance degradation in multi-category industrial anomaly detection.  
[PDF 第 36 页 | A036_001_1] 中文：压缩。 然后通过模板引导补偿机制恢复被扭曲的特征,将异常特征转换为正常表达方式。 THFR 能够准确检测和定位不同尺度的异常现象。 Madan 和 Ristea (2023年) 提出 SSMCTB , 这是一种卷积神经网络任何一层输入信息的异常检测方法, 并使用 3D 卷积和Transformer块重建掩码数据。 SSMCTB 专门接受正常样本训练, 不再需要异常数据 。 Park等人(2024年)建议增加损失,这是一种通过修改损失函数而不改变神经网络结构而提高异常检测性能的方法。LAMP使损失情况急剧恶化,导致对看不见异常现象模式的重建错误较大,从而改进了在无监督的异常检测环境中的探测。Chen等人(2024年)提出隐式前景引导网络(IFgNet),以缓解工业异常检测中的背景噪音误分类。 IFgNet采用了多任务学习框架,将前景检测和图像重建任务结合起来。 通过使用共享网络和具体任务注意力机制,IFgNet学会了将前景与背景区分开来,从而减少背景干扰并提高异常检测的准确性。 Liu和Wang(2024年)提出了多类异常自编码器(MAAE),该方法旨在解决多类工业异常检测中的高储存成本、低训练效率和性能退化问题。

[PDF 第 36 页 | A036_001_2] EN: MAAE captures global category information and feature distributions through spatial and channel attention mechanisms. Additionally, it introduces an adaptive noise generator and a feature fusion module to simulate real noise and preserve surface semantics, enabling unified anomaly detection across different categories while enhancing model performance and generalization ability. Fan et al. (2024) propose PatchAnomaly, a patch-level data augmentation method that reformulates unsupervised anomaly detection as a supervised object detection task. PatchAnomaly synthesizes similar anomalies through self-supervised learning, enabling direct anomaly localization without the need for complex post-processing steps. Zhang et al. (2024) propose DC-AE, an unsupervised anomaly detection framework designed to address the issue of traditional autoencoders mistakenly reconstructing anomalies as normal features. DC-AE enhances anomaly detection accuracy by integrating a double-constrained autoencoder (DCAE) and an adaptive weighted similarity space attention (AWSSA) mechanism. It employs adversarial learning and a global memory bank to restrict the model’s generalization ability, preventing incorrect reconstruction of anomalous regions. Additionally, AWSSA strengthens the model’s focus on anomalies, further improving anomaly localization accuracy.  
[PDF 第 36 页 | A036_001_2] 中文：MAAE通过空间和通道注意力机制捕捉全球类别信息和特征分布;此外,它引入了适应性噪音生成器和一个特性聚合模块,以模拟真实噪音并保存表面语义,从而能够在不同类别中统一发现异常现象,同时提高模型性能和一般化能力;Fan等人(2024年)提出Patch Anomaly,这是一个图像块级数据增强方法,将无监督的异常检测重新作为受监督的物体探测任务。PatchAomaly通过自监督的学习合成类似的异常现象,使得无需复杂的后处理步骤而直接出现异常现象的定位。 Zhang等人(2024年)建议开发DC-AE,这是一个无监督的异常检测框架,旨在解决传统自编码器错误地将异常现象重建为正常特征的问题。 DC-AE通过整合一个双重限制的自编码器(DCAE)和一个适应性加权相似的空间注意力机制(AWSSA)来提高异常检测准确度。 它使用对抗式学习和全球记忆库来限制模型的普及能力,防止异常地区的不正确重建。 此外,AWSSA加强了模型对异常的关注,进一步提高了异常定位的准确性。

[PDF 第 36 页 | A036_002_1] EN: 2.3.2 Generative adversarial networks

[PDF 第 36 页 | A036_003_1] EN: Generative adversarial networks show great potential in industrial anomaly detection. By training a model with normal samples and using the difference in feature distributions between normal and abnormal samples to identify anomalies, GANs effectively solve the problem of the scarcity of abnormal samples in industrial scenarios. In addition, generative adversarial networks can generate high-quality simulated data, which is helpful for training and testing anomaly detection models in the absence of actual abnormal data Xia et al. (2022). The goal of a GAN is to train the generator G to produce samples that closely resemble the true data distribution p  
[PDF 第 36 页 | A036_003_1] 中文：生成对抗性网络在发现工业异常现象方面显示出巨大的潜力。通过对一个具有正常样本的模型进行训练,并利用正常样本和异常样本之间特征分布的差异来识别异常现象,全球保护网有效地解决了工业情景中异常样品稀缺的问题。此外,基因对抗性网络可以产生高质量的模拟数据,这有助于在没有实际异常数据的情况下训练和测试异常检测模型(Xia等人,2022年)。全球保护网的目标是训练发电机G,以制作与真实数据分布非常相似的样本。

[PDF 第 37 页 | A037_056_1] EN: Although GANs have demonstrated strong performance in industrial anomaly detection, they still face several critical challenges. First, the training process of GANs can be unstable and is often prone to issues such as mode collapse. Second, industrial data is typically noisy and contains diverse types of anomalies, which requires GANs to exhibit strong robustness against both noise and varying defect patterns. As one of the earliest approaches used for synthetic anomaly generation, GAN-based methods learn to produce defective patterns by perturbing or reconstructing normal images, thereby enriching the training distribution with pseudo-anomalies and enhancing the model’s ability to identify defects. For instance, Yan et al. (2021) propose a semantic context-based learning method, SCADN, to address the challenge of detecting abnormal samples with low probability density in unsupervised anomaly detection datasets. SCADN generates multi-scale striped masks to obscure the semantic context of normal samples. During the inference phase, an error map is constructed by computing the differences between the reconstructed and input images, facilitating the identification of abnormal samples. This approach effectively mitigates the limitations of traditional anomaly detection methods in complex scenarios. Liang et al. (2023) introduce OCR-GAN, a novel approach to mitigating performance degradation caused by the limited reconstruction capability of traditional anomaly detection methods.  
[PDF 第 37 页 | A037_056_1] 中文：虽然GAN在工业异常检测方面表现良好,但它们仍面临若干重大挑战。 首先,GAN的训练过程可能不稳定,而且往往容易发生模式崩溃等问题。 其次,工业数据通常比较吵闹,包含各种类型的异常现象,这要求GAN对噪音和不同缺陷模式表现出强大的活力。 作为合成异常现象生成的最早方法之一,基于GAN的方法学会通过干扰或重建正常图像来产生有缺陷的模式,从而丰富了假造型者的训练分布并增强了模型识别缺陷的能力。 例如,Yan等人(2021年)提出了基于语义背景的学习方法,即SCADN,以解决在无监督的异常检测数据集中检测出概率低的异常样品的挑战。SCADN生成多尺度条纹遮罩以掩盖正常样本的语义背景。在推断阶段,通过计算重建图像和输入图像之间的差异来构建出一个错误图,便于识别异常样品。这种方法有效地缓解了复杂情况下传统异常检测方法的局限性。 Liang等人(2023年)介绍了OCR-GAN,这是减轻传统异常检测方法重建能力有限造成的性能退化的新办法。

[PDF 第 37 页 | A037_056_2] EN: OCR-GAN significantly improves reconstruction-based unsupervised anomaly detection by integrating an innovative frequency decoupling module to decompose the input image into different frequency distributions and a channel selection module to facilitate information interaction among multiple frequency branches. Duan et al. (2023) propose DFMGAN, a method for generating defective images with limited samples, to address the challenge of insufficient defect detection performance in industrial manufacturing due to the scarcity of real  
[PDF 第 37 页 | A037_056_2] 中文：OCR-GAN通过整合一个创新的频率脱钩模块,将输入图像分解成不同的频率分布,以及一个通道选择模块,促进多频分支之间的信息互动,大大改进了基于重建的、无监督的异常检测。

## PDF 第 38 页

[查看原 PDF 第 38 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=38>)

[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_001_1] EN: defective images. DFMGAN employs a two-stage training strategy: first, the StyleGAN2 backbone network is trained on a large set of defect-free images. Then, a defect perception residual block is incorporated to generate realistic defect masks. These masks are used to manipulate features within the masked regions, enabling the generation of high-quality, diverse defective images. This approach effectively augments the dataset and enhances defect detection performance.  
[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_001_1] 中文：DFMGAN采用两阶段训练策略:首先,StyleGAN2主干网用大量无瑕疵图像进行训练。然后,将一个缺陷感知残余块纳入其中以产生现实的缺陷面具。这些面具被用来操纵蒙蔽区域内的特征,从而生成高质量、多样的缺陷图像。这种方法有效地扩大了数据集并增强了缺陷检测性能。

[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_002_1] EN: 2.3.3 Transformer  
[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_002_1] 中文：2.3.3 Transformer

[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_003_1] EN: In recent years, YOLO-based models such as YOLOv5 and YOLOv8 have been widely used in industrial visual anomaly detection tasks due to their efficient inference speed and lightweight architecture. These methods are based on convolutional neural networks and are good at quickly locating and identifying defects with obvious features in images, such as scratches, breaks, or abnormal contours. They are suitable for production environments that require high real-time performance and deployment efficiency. In scenarios where the boundaries of abnormal areas are clear and there is sufficient labeled data, the YOLO method can achieve high-precision detection with low latency. In contrast, Transformer-based methods use self-attention mechanisms to construct global context relationships, which have stronger feature expression capabilities and can effectively capture long-distance dependencies and fine-grained differences in images. They are especially suitable for processing situations where abnormal patterns are fuzzy, the background is complex, or abnormal signals are embedded in local textures. These methods show higher robustness and generalization ability when dealing with uneven lighting, background interference, or irregular structures in industrial environments. Although their computational resource consumption is relatively high, with the development of lightweight Transformer architectures, their disadvantage in real-time performance is gradually being reduced.  
[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_003_1] 中文：近年来,以YOLO为主的模型(如YOLOv5和YOLOv8)因其有效推导速度和轻量级结构而被广泛用于工业视觉异常检测任务。这些方法基于进化神经网络,能够快速定位和识别图像中明显特征的缺陷,如刮伤、断裂或异常轮廓。它们适合于需要高实时性能和部署效率的生产环境。 在异常区域的界限明确且有足够标签数据的情况下,YOLO方法可以低潜势地实现高精度检测。 相反,基于变换器的方法使用自我注意力机制构建全球背景关系,这些功能表现能力更强,能够有效捕捉长距离依赖性和图像微小差异。 它们特别适合异常模式模糊、背景复杂或异常信号嵌入本地纹理的处理情形。 这些方法在应对工业环境中的不均匀照明、背景干扰或非正规结构时显示出更高的坚固性和一般化能力。 虽然它们的计算资源消耗量相对较高,但随着轻质变形器结构的发展,它们在实时性能方面的劣势正在逐渐减少。

[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_004_1] EN: Specifically, Mishra et al. (2021) propose VT-ADL, an image anomaly detection and localization method based on the vision transformer, designed to automatically identify significant changes in industrial images. VT-ADL leverages reconstruction and patch embedding techniques to preserve spatial information within the Transformer network and integrates a Gaussian mixture density network to localize anomalies. You et al. (2022) propose ADTR, an anomaly detection method designed to distinguish and detect anomalies using only normal samples. Instead of directly processing raw pixel values, ADTR employs a transformer model to reconstruct pre-trained deep features and optimizes the model with a novel loss function, enabling effective anomaly detection even in the absence of abnormal samples. To overcome the limitations of existing anomaly detection methods in capturing global information, Lee and Kang (2022) propose AnoViT, which leverages global relationships, addressing the shortcomings of traditional convolutional neural networks, which are limited to local feature extraction. By utilizing the self-attention mechanism of ViT, AnoViT captures global contextual information and integrates it with convolutional layers to preserve spatial details, enabling accurate anomaly detection and localization. De et al. (2022) propose MeTAL, a masked transformer model based on the vision transformer architecture, designed to address the limitations of existing image anomaly detection methods in reconstructing and identifying anomalous regions.  
[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_004_1] 中文：具体地说,Mishra等人(2021年)提出VT-ADL,这是一种基于视觉Transformer的图像异常检测和定位方法,旨在自动识别工业图像中的重大变化。VT-ADL利用重建和图像块嵌入技术来保护Transformer网络内的空间信息,并整合高斯混合密度网络,以定位异常现象。You等人(2022年)提出了ADTR,这是一种为仅仅使用正常样本来辨别和检测异常现象而设计的异常检测方法。 为了克服现有异常检测方法在获取全球信息方面的局限性,ADTR和Kang(2022年)提出了“AnoVIT”方案,该方案利用传统神经变迁网络的缺陷,这些网络仅限于本地特征提取。 AnoVit通过维特自知机制捕捉全球背景信息并将其与进化层整合以保存空间细节、准确的异常检测和定位。 De等人(2022年)提出METAL,这是一个以愿景Transformer结构为基础的蒙面Transformer模型,旨在解决在重建和确定异常区域方面现有图像异常检测方法的局限性。

[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_004_2] EN: MeTAL employs a masked multi-head self-attention mechanism to reconstruct each image block using only surrounding block information, effectively disregarding potential anomalies within the block itself. Addition-  
[PDF 第 38 页 | A038_004_2] 中文：METAL使用一个蒙面多头自省机制,只利用周围的块信息重建每个图像块,实际上忽略了块内的潜在异常。

## PDF 第 39 页

[查看原 PDF 第 39 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=39>)

[PDF 第 39 页 | A039_001_1] EN: ally, MeTAL introduces a multi-shaped block structure, incorporating horizontal and vertical striped blocks alongside traditional square blocks. Their encodings are then combined to form the final block representation. Compared to using only square blocks, this multi-shaped block approach significantly enhances model performance. Pirnay and Chai (2022) propose inpainting transformer (InTra), a novel anomaly detection method designed to address the misdetection problem caused by the ability of traditional convolutional autoencoders to reconstruct anomalous regions. InTra leverages a transformer model based on self-attention mechanisms to reconstruct masked patches, enabling the repair and reconstruction of image blocks without relying on convolution operations. By incorporating global contextual information, InTra achieves effective detection and localization of anomalous regions. Jiang et al. Jiang et al. (2022) propose masked swin transformer UNet (MSTUNet), an industrial anomaly detection model designed to address the limitations of traditional convolutional neural networks in extracting global contextual information. MSTUNet applies anomaly simulation and masking strategies to anomaly-free samples to generate simulated anomalies and leverages the swin transformer’s powerful global learning capability to reconstruct masked regions. Meanwhile, it incorporates a UNet network for end-to-end anomaly detection, enabling high-precision industrial anomaly detection and localization. Zhang et al. (2025) propose a novel symmetric structure ViTAD based on the pure vision transformer (ViT) for the multi-class unsupervised anomaly detection (MUAD) task.  
[PDF 第 39 页 | A039_001_1] 中文：NAY, METAL 引入了多形块结构, 将沿传统方块的横向和垂直条纹区块融合在一起。 然后将其编码组合成最后的区块表示。 与只使用方块相比, 这种多形区块方法极大地提高了模型性能。 Pirnay 和 Chai ( 2022) 提出了绘制Transformer( InTra ) , 这是一种新颖的异常检测方法, 旨在解决传统的革命自动转换器重建异常区域的能力造成的误测问题。 InTra利用一种基于自我注意机制的Transformer模型来重建遮盖板,使图像块的修复和重建无需依赖卷土重来。通过纳入全球背景信息,InTra实现了异常区域的有效检测和定位。Jiang等人(2022年)提议使用遮蔽型双TransformerUNet(MSTUNet ) , 这是一种工业异常检测模型,旨在解决传统神经网络在提取全球背景信息方面的局限性。 MSTUNet将异常现象模拟和掩码策略应用到无异常的样本中,以生成模拟异常现象,并利用双向Transformer强大的全球学习能力重建遮蔽区域。 与此同时,它包含了一个用于端对端异常检测的UNet网络,从而能够发现高精度工业异常现象并实现定位。 Zhang等人(2025年)提出了基于纯视觉变异器(VIT)的新式对称结构 VITAD,用于多级无监督的异常检测任务。

[PDF 第 39 页 | A039_001_2] EN: In contrast to existing methods that rely on complex pyramid structures and a large number of manual designs, ViTAD adopts a simple pure ViT architecture, and through macro and micro design optimizations, it achieves efficient and powerful anomaly detection capabilities. In terms of macroscopic design, ViTAD removes the jump connection of multi-scale features and only uses the features of the last stage as input, avoiding potential information leakage caused by too short information flow paths. In terms of microscopic design, ViTAD carries out meticulous structural optimization. It uses features before normalization as fusion features, retains position embedding to enhance feature expression ability, and removes category labels to avoid interference with spatial structure. Yao et al. (2023) propose focus-the-discrepancy (FOD), an image anomaly detection framework designed to overcome the limitations of traditional methods in detecting complex anomalies due to insufficient analysis of local feature differences and internal-external image correlations. FOD leverages the transformer’s self-attention mechanism to establish correlations within and between images. Additionally, it employs self-supervised learning with object relevance based on the RBF kernel, along with object relevance and entropy constraint strategies, to enhance the distinction between normal and abnormal patterns, enabling effective anomaly detection and localization. Luo et al.  
[PDF 第 39 页 | A039_001_2] 中文：与依赖复杂的金字塔结构和大量手工设计的现有方法相比,VITAD采用了简单的纯维特结构,并通过宏观和微观设计优化,实现了高效和强大的异常检测能力。 在宏观设计方面,VTAD删除了多尺度特征的跳动连接,仅将最后阶段的特性用作输入,避免信息流路径太短可能造成的信息渗漏。在微观设计方面,VITAD进行了细致的结构优化。 它利用在正常化之前的特征作为融合功能,保留嵌入位置以加强特征表示能力,并删除类别标签以避免干扰空间结构。 Yao等人(2023年)提出了焦点差异性(FOD),这是一个图像异常检测框架,目的是克服由于对本地特征差异和内外部图像关联分析不足而发现复杂异常情况的传统方法的局限性。 FOD利用Transformer的自我注意机制来建立图像内部和图像之间的关联关系。 此外,它利用基于RBF核心的自监督学习以及对象相关性和酶约束战略,以加强正常与异常模式之间的区分,从而能够有效地发现异常现象并实现定位。

[PDF 第 39 页 | A039_001_3] EN: (2024) propose AMI-Net, an adaptive mask-filling network designed to address the limitations of unsupervised reconstruction methods in industrial anomaly detection, which struggle to effectively distinguish normal and abnormal regions in defect detection. AMI-Net enhances the accuracy and real-time performance of anomaly detection by introducing an adaptive mask generator, integrating multi-scale pre-trained feature extraction, and employing a random position quantitative masking strategy. During the testing phase, AMI-Net dynamically masks defect regions while preserving normal regions using the adaptive mask generator. Yao et al. (2024) propose prior normality prompt transformer (PNPT), an industrial anomaly detection method designed to address the same mapping problem encountered by traditional methods in multi-class detection. PNPT reconstructs the inherent characteristics of samples by introducing normality prompting and a dual-stream model structure to  
[PDF 第 39 页 | A039_001_3] 中文：(2024) 提议AMI-Net,这是一个适应性掩码填补网络,旨在解决工业异常检测中无监督的重建方法的局限性,该方法力求有效区分缺陷检测中的正常和不正常区域。 AMI-Net通过引入适应性掩码生成器、整合多级预训练特征提取以及采用随机定位定量掩码策略,提高了异常检测的准确性和实时性能。在测试阶段,AMI-Net动态掩码功能缺陷区域,同时使用适应性掩码生成器保护正常区域。 Yao等人(2024年)建议采用先期正常Transformer(PNPT),这是一种工业异常检测方法,旨在解决多级探测中传统方法遇到的相同的绘图问题。

[PDF 第 40 页 | A040_001_1] EN: capture category-specific normality features. Its innovative architecture comprises four key modules-class-specific normality prompting pool, hierarchical patch embedding, semantic alignment coupling encoding, and contextual semantic conditional decoding-which effectively distinguish normal from abnormal instances, significantly enhancSwining multi-class industrial image anomaly detection performance.  
[PDF 第 40 页 | A040_001_1] 中文：捕捉特定类别常态特性。 它的创新架构包括四个关键模块, 特定类常态促动池、 等级图像块嵌入、 语义对齐混合编码以及上下文语义有条件解码, 有效地区分正常和异常情况, 大大地强化了多级工业图像异常检测性能 。

[PDF 第 40 页 | A040_002_1] EN: 2.3.4 Diffusion model  
[PDF 第 40 页 | A040_002_1] 中文：2.3.4 扩散模型

[PDF 第 40 页 | A040_003_1] EN: In industrial anomaly detection, the diffusion model reconstructs high-quality images through an inverse process that removes abnormal regions from the input image Haohao et al. (2023). Anomalies are detected by pixel-by-pixel and feature-by-feature comparisons between the input and reconstructed images. Additionally, the conditional denoising process ensures consistent recovery of the object image, enhancing anomaly detection accuracy. The diffusion model localizes anomalies through pixel-level and feature-level comparisons. Pixel-level comparisons analyze differences at the individual pixel level, while feature-level comparisons capture high-level semantic variations. Anomaly scores are computed based on the discrepancies between the input and reconstructed images, quantifying the severity of anomalies.  
[PDF 第 40 页 | A040_003_1] 中文：在工业异常检测中,扩散模型通过一个从输入图像Haohao等人(2023年)中去除不正常区域的反向过程重建高质量的图像。通过像素逐像素和对输入与重建的图像进行地貌比较检测出异常现象。此外,有条件拆卸过程确保了物体图像的一致恢复,提高了异常检测的准确性。扩散模型通过像素水平和特征级别的比较将异常现象定位。 像素级比较分析单个像素级的差异,而特性级比较则捕捉高层次语义变异。 异常分数是根据输入与重建图像之间的差异计算的,以量化异常的严重性。

[PDF 第 40 页 | A040_004_1] 中文：q(x

AnoDDPM addresses the challenge of detecting large abnormal regions in high-resolution images by replacing traditional  
[PDF 第 40 页 | A040_122_1] 中文：具体来说,Wyatt等人(2022年)提出了AnoDDPM,这是一种基于去除扩散概率模型的异常检测方法。

## PDF 第 41 页

[查看原 PDF 第 41 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=41>)

[PDF 第 41 页 | A041_001_1] EN: Gaussian noise with multi-scale simplex noise, effectively enhancing anomaly identification and localization. Zhang et al. (2023) propose DiffusionAD, an industrial anomaly detection method designed to overcome the performance limitations of traditional generative models caused by poor reconstruction quality. DiffusionAD enhances anomaly detection and localization accuracy by leveraging a denoising diffusion model and introducing a noise-to-norm paradigm and a fast one-step denoising paradigm, significantly improving both efficiency and precision. Mousakhan et al. (2023) propose Denoising Diffusion Anomaly Detection (DDAD), a novel anomaly detection method designed to overcome the limitations of traditional reconstruction-based approaches. DDAD guides the image denoising process through a conditional mechanism, integrates pixel-level and feature-level comparisons for anomaly localization, and employs domain adaptation techniques to enhance the feature extractor, significantly improving anomaly detection and localization performance. Yin et al. (2023) propose LafitE, an unsupervised multi-class anomaly detection method designed to address the “identity shortcut” problem in anomaly detection. LafitE leverages a latent diffusion model to reconstruct the normal feature distribution and incorporates a feature editing strategy to enhance reconstruction quality and reduce false positives. Additionally, LafitE introduces a hyperparameter selection strategy that constructs a pseudo-validation set by synthesizing anomalous data, significantly improving anomaly detection and localization accuracy. Zhang et al.  
[PDF 第 41 页 | A041_001_1] 中文：张等人(2023年)提出DifulationAD,这是一种工业异常检测方法,旨在克服重建质量差造成的传统基因模型的性能限制;DifiltionAD利用一个去除音量的传播模式和采用噪音到温度范式以及快速一步的拆除范式,提高异常检测和定位准确性,大大提高效率和精确性。 Mousakhan等人(2023年)提出Denoising Difulation Anomaly探测(DDAD),这是一种新颖的异常检测方法,旨在克服传统的重建办法的局限性。DDAD通过一个有条件的机制指导图像去除过程,将像素级和特征级的比较结合到异常定位中,并使用域适应技术加强地物提取器,大大改进异常检测和定位性能。Yin等人(2023年)提出了LafitE,这是一个无监督的多级异常检测方法,目的是解决异常检测中的“身份捷径”问题。 LafitE利用潜在的扩散模型来重建正常地物分布,并包含一个功能编辑策略以提高重建质量和减少假阳性（误检）。 此外,LafitE引入了一个超参数选择策略,通过合成异常数据构建一个伪验证装置,大大改进异常检测和定位精确度。

[PDF 第 41 页 | A041_001_2] EN: (2023) propose DiffAD, an unsupervised surface anomaly detection method designed to address the challenges of structural changes and reconstruction difficulties in abnormal regions faced by traditional reconstruction-based methods. DiffAD leverages a latent diffusion model to generate high-quality, diverse images and incorporates noise conditional embedding and interpolation channel techniques to enhance anomaly differentiation. By reducing pixel-level differences between reconstructed and original images, DiffAD significantly improves anomaly detection and localization accuracy. Lu et al. (2023) propose RAN, an unsupervised anomaly detection method based on a denoising diffusion model. RAN introduces random noise to mask abnormal pixels and leverages the intermediate denoising process of the generative diffusion model to compute pixel-level anomaly scores, using KL divergence to enhance detection accuracy. Additionally, RAN incorporates a gradient denoising process to smoothly transform anomalous images into normal ones, addressing the limitation of traditional reconstruction methods, where abnormal regions may be inadvertently reconstructed, leading to poor detection performance. He et al. (2024) propose DiAD, a diffusion-based multi-class anomaly detection framework designed to preserve image category and pixel-level structural integrity in anomaly detection. DiAD enhances reconstruction accuracy by integrating a spatial-aware feature fusion block with a pixel-space autoencoder, a semantic guidance network in latent space, and a pre-trained feature extractor in feature space.  
[PDF 第 41 页 | A041_001_2] 中文：(2023年)提出DiffAD,这是一种无监督的表面异常检测方法,旨在应对传统重建方法在异常区域面临的结构变化和重建困难的挑战。DiffAD利用一种潜在的扩散模型生成高质量、多样化图像,并纳入有条件嵌入和内插的噪音技术,以强化异常现象的区分。DiffAD通过减少重塑图像与原始图像之间的像素级差异,大大提高了异常检测和定位准确性。Lu等人(2023年)提出了RAN,这是基于去音传播模型的一种无监督的异常检测方法。 RAN 引入随机噪音以遮盖异常像素,并利用基因扩散模型的中间分解过程来计算像素级异常分数,使用 KL 差异来提高检测精确度。 此外, RAN 包含一个梯度分解过程,将异常图像顺利转化为正常图像,解决传统重建方法的限制问题,在这些方法中,异常区域可能会无意地重建,导致检测性能差。 He等人(2024)提出DAD,这是一个基于扩散的多级异常检测框架,旨在维护图像类别和异常检测中的像素级结构完整性。 通过将一个有空间意识的特征聚合块与像素-空间自编码器、潜在空间的语义制导网络和在地物空间的一个经过预先训练的特征提取器结合起来, DiAD提高了重建准确性。

[PDF 第 41 页 | A041_001_3] EN: By reconstructing anomalous regions while preserving the semantic integrity of the original image, DiAD significantly improves anomaly detection and localization performance. Teng et al. (2024) propose AnomalyDiffusion, a method for generating anomalous images from limited samples, designed to address the scarcity of anomalous data in industrial anomaly detection. AnomalyDiffusion leverages the latent diffusion model’s powerful prior information, integrating spatial anomaly embedding and an adaptive attention reweighting mechanism to generate high-quality anomalous images with precise anomaly mask alignment. Tebbe and Tayyub (2024) propose dynamic denoising diffusion probability model (D3AD) to address the challenge of precisely localizing anomalies of different scales in anomaly detection. D3AD enhances localization accuracy  
[PDF 第 41 页 | A041_001_3] 中文：通过重建异常区域,同时保持原始图像的语义完整性,Diad大大改善了异常检测和定位性能。 Teng等人(2024年)提出“异常扩散”方案,这是从有限的样本中生成异常图像的一种方法,旨在解决工业异常检测中的异常数据稀缺问题。 “异常扩散”方案利用了潜在传播模型先前强有力的信息,整合了空间异常嵌入和适应性关注再加权机制,以产生具有精确异常面罩校正的高质量异常图像。 Tebbe和Tayyyub (2024年) 提出了动态除去扩散概率模型(D3AD),以应对在异常检测中精确定位不同尺度异常现象的挑战。

[PDF 第 42 页 | A042_001_1] EN: by introducing a dynamically calculated noise step, leveraging latent space representations, and employing a domain-adaptive feature extractor. Specifically, D3AD incorporates a dynamic implicit conditioning mechanism that adjusts noise addition based on the initial anomaly prediction, enabling more precise anomaly segmentation. Additionally, it performs denoising in latent space rather than at the pixel level, reducing computational demands and improving reconstruction quality for large missing components. By accurately determining the optimal number of noise steps, D3AD significantly improves anomaly detection performance, particularly in anomaly localization. Yao et al. (2024) propose globally and locally adaptive diffusion (GLAD), a novel diffusion model designed to address the sample reconstruction challenge in unsupervised anomaly detection. GLAD mitigates uneven reconstruction difficulty across different anomalies by predicting specific denoising steps for each sample and spatially adaptive feature fusion, enhancing reconstruction quality and anomaly detection accuracy. Additionally, GLAD introduces an anomaly-oriented training paradigm, incorporating synthetic anomaly samples to improve the reconstruction of abnormal regions, enabling high-performance anomaly detection and localization. To overcome overgeneralization and loss of detail in traditional two-stage anomaly detection architectures, Fučka et al. (2024) propose TransFusion, which employs a single-stage iterative process to simultaneously reconstruct and localize anomalies. Yan et al.  
[PDF 第 42 页 | A042_001_1] 中文：具体地说, D3AD 包含了一个动态内含调节机制,根据最初的异常现象预测调整噪音添加,从而能够更精确地进行反常分解。此外,D3AD在隐蔽空间而不是像素层面进行除尘,减少计算需求,提高大量缺失部件的重建质量。D3AD通过准确确定最佳噪声步骤的数量,极大地提高了异常检测性能,特别是在异常定位方面。 Yao等人(2024年)提出了全局和局部适应性推广方案,这是一个新型的传播模式,旨在应对在未受监督的异常检测工作中出现的抽样重建挑战;通过预测每个样本的具体分层步骤和空间适应性特征聚合,减轻不同异常之间的不均衡重建困难,提高重建质量和异常检测准确度;此外,全球LAD引入了一个面向异常的训练模式,将合成异常样本纳入其中,以改善异常地区的重建,从而能够进行高性能异常检测和定位。 为了克服传统的两阶段异常检测结构的过分概括和细节损失,Fučka等人(2024年)提议采用一个单一阶段迭接程序,同时重建和确定异常现象。

[PDF 第 42 页 | A042_001_2] EN: (2024) propose AnomalySD, a novel anomaly detection framework designed for industrial manufacturing, where normal samples are scarce. AnomalySD leverages the zero-/few-shot repair capability of the stable diffusion model, integrating hierarchical text prompts and a foreground mask mechanism to fine-tune stable diffusion for anomaly detection. During inference, a multi-scale masking strategy and a prototype-guided masking strategy are employed to accurately mask and repair abnormal regions, enabling efficient anomaly detection and localization. Zhang et al. (2024) propose RealNet, a feature selection network designed to enhance industrial anomaly detection by generating realistic and diverse synthetic anomaly samples. RealNet integrates three key modules: (1) a strength-controllable diffusion anomaly synthesis (SDAS) module, which enables precise manipulation of defect location, intensity, and structure, facilitating the simulation of rare, subtle, or complex anomalies; (2) an anomaly-aware feature selection module that selects a representative subset of pre-trained features to enhance anomaly sensitivity; and (3) a reconstruction residual selection module that adaptively selects residuals to improve localization accuracy. Diffusion-based anomaly generation in RealNet effectively addresses the scarcity of defective samples while preserving high visual fidelity and structural diversity.  
[PDF 第 42 页 | A042_001_2] 中文：(2024年)提出了AnomalySD,这是一个为工业制造设计的新奇异常检测框架,通常样品很少。 AnomalySD利用了稳定扩散模型的零光/微光修复能力,整合了分层文字提示和前景遮罩机制,以微调稳定的异常检测传播。 在推断过程中,采用了多尺度掩码战略和原型制模掩码策略来准确掩蔽和修复异常区域,从而能够有效地发现和定位异常现象。 Zhang等人(2024年)提议建立RealNet,这是一个地物选择网络,目的是通过生成现实和多样的合成异常物样本,加强工业异常检测。 RealNet综合了三个关键模块:(1) 强度可控扩散异常物合成模块,该模块能够精确操纵缺陷位置、强度和结构,便利对稀有、微妙或复杂的异常物进行模拟;(2) 异常特征选择模块,选择具有代表性的预先训练的特性子集,以提高异常度;(3) 重建剩余物选择模块,以适应性方式选择残留物,提高定位准确性。 在RealNet中,基于传播的异常生成有效地解决了缺陷样品稀缺的问题,同时保持高视觉忠诚度和结构多样性。

[PDF 第 43 页 | A043_001_1] EN: model based on the visual question paradigm, for zero-shot anomaly detection. Zhang et al. propose GPT-4V-AD, a framework that leverages granular region segmentation, carefully crafted prompts, and text-to-segmentation conversion to precisely localize and assess abnormal regions in images. Zhaopeng et al. (2024) propose AnomalyGPT, a novel industrial anomaly detection method based on large-scale vision-language models. Traditional industrial anomaly detection methods require manual threshold setting and struggle to adapt to complex industrial scenarios. AnomalyGPT addresses this limitation by simulating abnormal image data through an image decoder and a prompt learner. AnomalyGPT enables automatic anomaly detection and localization, directly evaluates anomalies without manual threshold adjustment, and supports multi-turn dialogue and few-shot contextual learning, significantly enhancing both the practicality and accuracy of industrial anomaly detection tasks. Xu et al. (2024) propose Customizable-VLM, a method for adapting a general vision-language model to enhance the generalization ability of anomaly detection in industrial scenarios. The approach integrates expert domain knowledge through a multimodal prompting strategy, guiding the model in anomaly detection and reasoning. By unifying the representation of multimodal inputs, the study demonstrates that combining visual and linguistic prompts significantly improves anomaly detection performance across different data modalities while enabling general anomaly detection and reasoning in diverse industrial scenarios. Zhang et al. Zhang et al.

[PDF 第 43 页 | A043_001_2] EN: (2024) propose LogiCode, a novel framework designed to address the challenge of detecting logic anomalies in industrial environments, where traditional methods often struggle. Unlike conventional approaches, LogiCode leverages the logical reasoning capabilities of large language models to identify anomalies such as incorrect component counts or missing elements by automatically generating Python code, while also providing detailed anomaly explanations. LogiCode automates the entire process from logical rule definition to anomaly detection through three key modules: code prompt, code generation, and code execution, offering robust support for industrial quality control and reducing manual inspection workloads. Hattori et al. (2024) propose segment elementbased anomaly detection (SEAD), an anomaly detection method designed to address the limitations of existing approaches in detecting logical anomalies that affect entire images. SEAD builds on the traditional ComAD method and detects anomalies through SegGPT segmentation, a memory bank that stores the color and pixel count of each element in normal images, and anomaly detection based on the Euclidean distance between test images and the memory bank.  
[PDF 第 43 页 | A043_001_2] 中文：LogiCode(2024)提出一个新颖的框架,旨在应对在传统方法经常挣扎的工业环境中发现逻辑异常的挑战。 LogiCode与常规方法不同,它利用大型语言模型的逻辑推理能力来识别异常现象,如不正确的组件计数或缺失元素,同时自动生成 Python 代码,并提供详细的异常解释。 LogiCode通过三个关键模块将整个过程自动化,从逻辑规则定义到异常检测:代码快速、代码生成和代码执行,为工业质量控制提供强有力的支持并减少人工检查工作量。 Hattori 等人(2024年) 提出了基于部分元素异常值的检测方法,这是一种异常值检测方法,旨在解决现有方法在检测影响整个图像的逻辑异常值方面的局限性。 SEAD以传统的ComAD方法为基础,并通过SegGPT分割法检测异常值。 SegGPT分离法是一个存储正常图像中每个元素的颜色和像素计数的记忆库,以及基于测试图像与记忆库之间的Euclidean距离的异常值检测。
