[PDF 第 44 页 | A044_002_1] EN: Figure 10 presents the SAM-based industrial vision anomaly detection framework. Cao et al. (2023) address the reliance of existing anomaly segmentation models on domain-specific fine-tuning and their limited generalization ability. They propose SAA+, a novel framework for segmenting anomalies in images without training. SAA+ leverages hybrid prompting regularization to adapt the base model using domain expert knowledge and the target image context, enhancing its generalization across different domains. It generates anomalous regions by cascading a prompt-guided object detection and segmentation model and introduces hybrid prompting to reduce linguistic ambiguity and improve segmentation accuracy. Liu et al. (2024) propose UCAD, an unsupervised continual anomaly detection framework designed to address the continual learning challenge in industrial manufacturing caused by the lack of labeled data. UCAD enables unsupervised anomaly detection with continual learning capabilities through contrastively learned prompts, mitigating catastrophic forgetting and reducing computational burden. The framework comprises two core modules: continuous prompt model (CPM) and structure-based contrastive learning (SCL). CPM utilizes a key prompt knowledge memory to guide anomaly predictions while maintaining a consistent task objective. SCL, integrated with the segment anything model, enhances feature representation via contrastive learning, improving anomaly segmentation performance. Li et al. (2024) propose SAM-guided two-stream lightweight model (STLM) enhance model efficiency and mobility in industrial anomaly detection.  
[PDF 第 44 页 | A044_002_1] 中文：图10展示了基于SAM的工业影像异常检测框架,Cao等人(2023年)探讨了现有异常现象分类模型对特定领域微调的依赖及其有限的泛化能力,他们提出了SAA+,这是一个在不经过训练的情况下对图像异常现象进行分解的新框架。SAA+利用域内专家知识和目标图象环境来推动对基准模型进行混合规范化,加强不同领域的常规化。它通过串联快速引导对象检测和分解模式生成异常区域,并引入混合催化,以减少语言模糊性并提高分解准确性。 Liu等人(2024年)提出UCAD,这是一个无监督的持续异常检测框架,旨在应对工业制造中因缺少标签数据而造成的持续学习挑战。UCAD通过反向学习的提示、减轻灾难性遗忘和减少计算负担,使无监管的异常检测具有持续学习能力。该框架由两个核心模块组成:连续快速模型和基于结构的对比性学习。CPM利用关键快速知识记忆指导异常现象预测,同时保持一个一致的任务目标。 Li等人(2024年)提出SAM引导的双流轻量模型(STLM)可提高模型在工业异常检测方面的效率和流动性。

[PDF 第 44 页 | A044_002_2] EN: The framework leverages SAM’s strong generalization ability to achieve unsupervised anomaly detection using two lightweight image encoders (two-stream lightweight modules). One stream generates discriminative row-wise and universal feature representations of normal and abnormal regions, while the other reconstructs anomaly-free images, enhancing feature discriminability when detecting anomalies. Additionally, a shared mask decoder and feature aggregation module generate anomaly maps, significantly reducing model parameters and inference time while maintaining high performance, effectively addressing the dual challenges of efficiency and mobility in industrial anomaly detection.  
[PDF 第 44 页 | A044_002_2] 中文：该框架利用SAM的强力泛化能力,通过两个轻量级图像编码器(两流轻量级模块)实现无监督的异常检测。 一个流生成了正常和异常地区的有差别的行和通用特征表示,而另一个流则重建无异常图像,在发现异常时强化了特性差异性。 此外,一个共享的遮罩解码器和特征汇总模块生成了异常图,极大地减少了模型参数和推算时间,同时保持高性能,有效地应对了工业异常检测中的效率和流动性的双重挑战。

[PDF 第 45 页 | A045_001_1] EN: to more clearly reflect the technological progress in industrial visual anomaly detection. Specifically, Chen et al. (2023) propose EasyNet, a 3D industrial anomaly detection model designed to address challenges in deployment, storage, and real-time performance faced by existing methods. EasyNet reconstructs segmentation maps of abnormal regions using a multi-scale, multi-modal feature encoder-decoder and employs an attention-based information entropy fusion module to integrate features during inference. EasyNet enables efficient, real-time 3D anomaly detection without pre-trained models or memory banks, significantly enhancing detection performance and speed. Zhu et al. (2024) propose Group3AD, a novel network architecture designed to address challenges in high-resolution 3D power anomaly detection, including a large number of sample points, feature degradation, and the small proportion of anomalous regions. Group3AD enhances anomaly detection efficiency and accuracy through group-level feature comparison and learning. The framework introduces an inter-cluster uniformity network (IUN) and an intra-cluster alignment network (IAN) to optimize feature space distribution. Additionally, it employs an adaptive group center selection (AGCS) mechanism based on geometric information to improve the detection of potentially anomalous regions, significantly enhancing detection accuracy and efficiency. Li et al. (2024) propose DRAIN, a 3D industrial anomaly detection method designed to address the limitations of traditional approaches in effectively utilizing depth information.  
[PDF 第 45 页 | A045_001_1] 中文：具体来说,Chen等人(2023年)提出了3D型工业异常检测模型,目的是应对现有方法在部署、储存和实时性能方面面临的挑战。 EnterNet利用一个多尺度、多模式特性编码器-解码器重新绘制了异常区域的分解图,并使用基于注意的信息聚合模块来整合推理过程中的特征。EnterNet使得无需预先训练的模型或记忆库就能进行高效、实时的3D异常检测,大大提高了检测的性能和速度。 Zhu等人(2024年)提议建立3D组,这是一个新颖的网络架构,旨在应对高分辨率3D功率异常检测方面的挑战,包括大量抽样点、特征退化和异常区域比例小的问题。Group3AD通过群体级特征比较和学习提高异常检测效率和准确性。框架引入了集群间统一网络和集群内部对齐网络,以优化地貌空间分布。此外,它利用基于几何信息的适应性小组中心选择机制,以改进潜在异常区域的检测,大大提高探测的准确性和效率。 Li等人(2024年)提出DRAIN,这是一种3D工业异常检测方法,旨在解决有效利用深度信息的传统做法的局限性。

[PDF 第 45 页 | A045_001_2] EN: DRAIN employs a dual reconstruction network, consisting of a 3D reconstruction network and a 3D segmentation network, to process RGB and depth images using an encoder-decoder architecture. Additionally, it integrates a lightweight multi-layer perceptron (MLP) and a dual attention mechanism to fuse features, enabling high-performance 3D industrial anomaly detection. Zhou et al. (2024) propose R3D-AD, a novel anomaly detection method designed to address the challenges of high resource consumption and incomplete anomaly region reconstruction in existing 3D anomaly detection approaches. R3D-AD reconstructs point clouds by incorporating a diffusion model and employs Patch-Gen, a self-supervised learning strategy that simulates diverse defect shapes to enhance model generalization. Ultimately, R3D-AD achieves complete reconstruction of anomalous point clouds and efficient anomaly detection. Cao et al. (2024) propose complementary pseudo-multimodal features (CPMF) to address the challenge of point cloud anomaly detection. CPMF enhances detection performance by fusing local geometric and global semantic features. CPMF combines local geometric features extracted via 3D hand-crafted descriptors with global semantic features derived from multi-view images using a 2D pre-trained neural network. Additionally, CPMF employs multi-view image projection and feature aggregation techniques to further improve anomaly detection accuracy. Cheng et al. (2024) propose MVP-PCLIP, a multi-view projection framework (MVP) that projects point cloud data into multi-view depth images and integrates anomaly detection with pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs).  
[PDF 第 45 页 | A045_001_2] 中文：DRAIN使用由3D重建网络和3D分割网络组成的双重重建网络,利用编码器-解码器结构处理RGB和深度图像;此外,它还结合了轻量多层传感器(MLP)和引信特性的双重注意力机制,从而能够发现高性能的3D工业异常现象。 Zhou等人(2024年)提议采用R3D-AD,这是一种新颖的异常检测方法,旨在应对资源消耗高以及现有3D异常检测办法中未完全恢复异常区域的挑战。 R3D-AD通过采用扩散模型来重建点云,并使用Patch-Gen,这是一种自监督的学习战略,模拟各种缺陷形状,以加强模型的概括化;最终,R3D-AD能够完全重建异常云和有效探测异常现象;Cao等人(2024年)提出了补充性假多式特征,以应对点云异常检测的挑战;CPMF利用当地的几何和全球语义特征提高探测性能。 CPMF将利用3D手工制作的描述符提取的地方几何特征与使用2D预先训练神经网络从多视图像中得出的全球语义特征结合起来,此外,CPMF还采用多视图像投影和地貌汇总技术,以进一步提高异常检测准确性。 Cheng等人(2024年)提出MVP-PCLIP,这是一个多视图预测框架,用于将云数据点到多视深度图像,并将异常检测与预先训练的视觉语言模型(VLMs)相结合。

Additionally, MVP-PCLIP enhances model performance by introducing learning visual cues and adaptive text prompts, which improve the model’s focus on anomalous regions and the accuracy of textual descriptions, significantly boosting zero-shot point cloud anomaly detection. Zhou et al. (2024) propose PointAD, a novel zero-shot 3D anomaly detection method designed to detect anomalies in unseen objects without target 3D training samples. PointAD constructs a unified framework that integrates R-CNN, 3D point clouds, and 2D pixel representations to enhance 3D anomaly understanding and detection. PointAD  
[PDF 第 45 页 | A045_001_3] 中文：MVP-PCLIP解决了常规的无监督点云异常检测中一般化不良、数据获取成本高和“冷点启动”问题等挑战。 此外,MVP-PCLIP引入了学习视觉提示和适应性文字提示来增强模型性能,这提高了模型对异常区域和文字描述的准确度,极大地促进了零射点云异常检测。 Zhou等人(2024年)提出了一种新颖的零射3D异常检测方法,该方法旨在在没有目标3D训练样本的情况下探测看不见物体中的异常现象。 PointAD 构建一个统一的框架,将 R-CNN、 3D点云和 2D 像素表示法结合起来,以加强对 3D 异常现象的理解和检测。 PointAD

[PDF 第 46 页 | A046_001_1] EN: employs multi-view rendering to project 3D point clouds into multiple 2D views, extracts pixel representations using a CLIP model, and maps them back to 3D space. Additionally, hybrid representation learning optimizes text prompt comprehension from both 3D and 2D information, enabling robust anomaly detection across diverse 3D objects. Furthermore, PointAD can seamlessly incorporate 2D RGB information during inference for multimodal 3D anomaly detection without requiring additional training. Liu et al. (2024) address the scarcity of high-precision point cloud anomaly detection datasets and the absence of systematic benchmarks by constructing Real3D-AD, a comprehensive dataset for 3D industrial anomaly detection. Additionally, they propose Reg3D-AD, a registration-based 3D anomaly detection method that leverages a feature memory bank to store local and global features, significantly enhancing detection accuracy. Costanzino et al. (2024) present CFM, a novel multimodal industrial anomaly detection framework designed to overcome the limitations of traditional methods in identifying complex industrial anomalies. CFM extracts 2D image and 3D point cloud features using a Transformer-based architecture and trains a lightweight neural network to learn the feature mapping between modalities. Anomalies are detected by identifying inconsistencies between observed and mapped features. Additionally, CFM introduces a layer pruning technique to optimize memory and computational efficiency, significantly enhancing detection and segmentation performance while achieving faster inference speeds and lower memory usage.

[PDF 第 46 页 | A046_002_1] EN: 3 Datasets  
[PDF 第 46 页 | A046_002_1] 中文：3 数据集

[PDF 第 46 页 | A046_003_1] EN: In industrial visual inspection tasks, anomalies can generally be divided into two categories: structural anomalies and semantic anomalies. Structural anomalies are usually manifested as geometric defects or physical damage on the surface of the product, such as scratches, cracks, holes, dents, etc., and can be effectively identified by local texture or edge information. Semantic anomalies are more biased towards errors at the high-level semantic level, such as missing parts, logical mismatches, and unreasonable positions, which are often difficult to directly determine using low-level pixel features. In the existing data sets, MVTec AD mainly contains structural anomalies, covering typical types of industrial surface defects, such as fabric (fabric tears), bottle (deformed bottle mouth), transistor (soldering point anomaly), etc. MVTec LOCO AD further introduces anomalies at the semantic level, such as “logical anomaly” (incorrect component quantity, misalignment during installation, etc.), which is closer to the complex visual judgment requirements in actual industrial scenarios. Therefore, we have specially marked the performance differences of each method in dealing with different types of anomalies in the experimental comparison to help understand the boundaries of the methods’ capabilities.  
[PDF 第 46 页 | A046_003_1] 中文：在工业直观检查任务中,异常一般可以分为两类:结构异常和语义异常;结构性异常通常表现为产品表面的几何缺陷或物理损坏,如刮痕、裂缝、孔洞、凹陷等,并且可以通过当地质地或边缘信息有效识别。 语义异常更偏向于高层语义错误,例如缺失部件、逻辑错配以及不合理的位置,而这些地方往往难以直接使用低水平像素特征确定。 在现有数据集中,MVTec AD主要包含结构异常,涵盖典型的工业表面缺陷类型,如织物(纤维眼泪)、瓶子(畸形瓶口)、晶体管(售货点异常)等。 MVTec LOCO AD在语义层面还引入了异常现象,如“逻辑异常”(组件数量不正确、安装过程中不协调等),这更接近实际工业情景中的复杂视觉判断要求。 因此,我们在实验性比较中特别标明了在处理不同类型的异常现象时每种方法的性能差异,以帮助了解这些方法能力的界限。

[PDF 第 46 页 | A046_004_1] EN: This section presents a comprehensive overview of widely used industrial anomaly detection datasets. Specifically, Sect. 3.1 discusses 2D datasets, while Sect. 3.2 focuses on 3D datasets. In addition, this paper provides a multi-dimensional analysis and categorization of these datasets. Based on factors such as task complexity, visual structural diversity, cross-domain generalization, and alignment with real-world industrial scenarios, the datasets are organized into three hierarchical tiers, as illustrated in Fig. 11.  
[PDF 第 46 页 | A046_004_1] 中文：本节全面概述广泛使用的工业异常检测数据集,具体而言,第3.1节讨论了2D数据集,而第3.2节侧重于3D数据集,此外,本文件对这些数据集进行了多层面分析和分类,根据任务复杂性、视觉结构多样性、跨域概括化以及与现实世界工业情景保持一致等因素,将数据集分为三个层次,如图11所示。

[PDF 第 47 页 | A047_003_1] EN: The MVTec AD dataset Bergmann et al. (2019) models real-world industrial production scenarios and serves as a benchmark for anomaly detection. It consists of fifteen industrial product categories, including five textured and ten object categories. The training set contains only normal images, whereas the test set includes both normal and anomalous images with pixel-level annotations for the latter. As a result, this dataset has become a widely used benchmark for unsupervised anomaly detection and localization.  
[PDF 第 47 页 | A047_003_1] 中文：MVTec AD数据集 Bergmann等人(2019年)模拟了真实世界工业生产情景,并用作异常检测的基准。该模型由15个工业产品类别组成,包括5个纹理和10个对象类别。训练集仅包含正常图像,而测试组则包含正常和异常的图像,带有像素级的说明。因此,这一数据集已成为无监督的异常检测和定位的广泛使用基准。

[PDF 第 47 页 | A047_004_1] EN: The VisA dataset Zou et al. (2022) employs a self-supervised learning approach, pretraining the model using a Spot-the-Difference task to improve anomaly detection and segmentation performance. The dataset comprises 12 subsets, each representing a distinct object, with a total of 10,821 images-9,621 normal and 1,200 anomalous. Four subsets feature printed circuit boards (PCB1, PCB2, PCB3, and PCB4) with complex structures, including transistors, capacitors, and chips. For multi-instance objects in the same field of view, VisA includes four subsets: Capsules, Candles, Macaroni1, and Macaroni2, with Capsules and Macaroni2 exhibiting significant positional and pose variations. Additionally, VisA includes four subsets-Cashew, Chewing Gum, Fryum, and Pipe Fryum-in which objects are approximately aligned. The anomalous images encompass various defects, which fall into two categories: surface defects (e.g., scratches, dents, stains, or cracks) and structural defects (e.g., misalignment or missing parts).  
[PDF 第 47 页 | A047_004_1] 中文：VisA数据集Zou等人(2022年)采用自监督的学习方法,利用点差任务对模型进行预先训练,以提高异常检测和分解性能,该数据集包括12个子集,每个子集代表一个不同的物体,共10 821张图像-9 621正常和1 200个异常。四个子集具有复杂的结构,包括晶体管、电容器和芯片,具有印刷线路板(PCB1、PCB2、PCB3和PCB4)的特点。 对于同一视野领域的多因子天体,VisA包括四个子集:Capsules、Candles、Macaroni1和Macaroni2, Capsules 和 Macaroni2 具有显著位置并造成变异。此外, VisA 包括四个子集-Cashew、 Chewing Gum、Fryum 和 Pipe Fryum-in,其中对象大致对齐。异常图像包含各种缺陷,分为两类:表面缺陷(例如抓痕、凹痕、污点或裂缝)和结构缺陷(如误差或缺失的部分)。

[PDF 第 47 页 | A047_005_1] EN: The BTAD dataset Mishra et al. (2021) features defects on both the body and surface of industrial products, comprising 1,800 normal images for training. In comparison to MVTec

[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_001_1] EN: AD, BTAD does not provide annotations for fine details and is better suited for large-scale anomaly detection.

[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_002_1] EN: Unlike previous datasets such as MVTec AD and VisA, which primarily focus on defect detection, AeBAD Zhang et al. (2023) emphasizes diversity within the same data category across different domains. AeBAD aims to automatically detect anomalies in aeroengine blades to ensure operational stability. It consists of two subsets: the single-blade dataset (AeBAD-S) and the video-based anomaly detection dataset (AeBAD-V). AeBAD-S comprises single-blade images with varying scales and aspect ratios. Its primary characteristics include misalignment and significant scale variations. Additionally, there is a domain shift between the normal images in the training and test sets, primarily due to variations in lighting and viewpoints. AeBAD-V comprises videos of assembled aeroengine blades, which facilitate anomaly detection during blisk rotation. A key challenge in AeBAD-V is the discrepancy in viewing angles between the training and test sets.  
[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_002_1] 中文：与以往主要侧重于发现缺陷的MVTec AD和VisA等数据集不同,AeBAD Zhang等人(2023年)强调不同领域同一数据类别的多样性。AeBAD的目标是自动检测气动刀片中的异常现象以确保操作稳定性。它由两个子集组成:单片数据集(AeBAD-S)和视频异常检测数据集(AeBAD-V)。 AeBAD-S由不同比例尺和侧面比例的单片图像组成,其主要特征包括不匹配和大比例变异。此外,训练和测试组中正常图像之间的区域变化主要归因于照明和视图的变化。AeBAD-V包含组合成气动刀片的视频,有助于在blisk旋转期间发现异常现象。AeBAD-V的主要挑战在于对训练组与测试组之间角度的差异。

[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_003_1] EN: MPDD Jezek et al. (2021) is an unsupervised dataset for industrial anomaly detection, designed to assess a model’s performance in detecting and segmenting defects in industrial production. Specifically, MPDD focuses on painted metal parts and consists of 6,600 images spanning six categories. The training set comprises 5,280 images, while the test set comprises 1,320 images. MPDD features multiple objects across different orientations, positions, and distances, under diverse lighting conditions and non-uniform backgrounds. Additionally, it provides pixel-level annotations for anomalous images, facilitating precise defect localization.  
[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_003_1] 中文：MPDD Jezek等人(2021年)是一套用于工业异常检测的无监督的数据集,旨在评估模型在发现和分解工业生产缺陷方面的性能。 具体地说,MPDD侧重于油漆金属部件,由6,600个图像组成,覆盖六大类。 训练集包括5,280张图像,而测试组则包含1,320幅图像。 在不同的照明条件和非统一背景下,MPDD具有不同方向、位置和距离的多个对象。 此外,它还为异常图像提供像素级说明,便利精确的缺陷定位。

[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_004_1] EN: MVTec LOCO-AD Bergmann et al. (2022) is a benchmark dataset for assessing unsupervised anomaly detection and localization algorithms. It comprises 3,644 images captured from five real-world industrial inspection scenarios, incorporating both structural and logical anomalies. Structural anomalies encompass scratches, dents, and contamination on manufactured products. Logical anomalies result from implicit constraint violations, e.g., misplaced allowed objects or missing required objects.  
[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_004_1] 中文：MVTec LOCO-AD Bergmann等人(2022年)是用于评估无监督的异常检测和定位算法的基准数据集,包括从五个真实世界工业检查情景中采集的3 644张图像,其中既包含结构上的异常,也包含逻辑上的异常现象。结构性异常包括刮伤、凹痕和制成品的污染。逻辑异常是由于隐含的限制性违规造成的,例如错失允许的物体或缺失所需的物件。

It features high-resolution scans acquired using industrial-grade 3D sensors and consists of ten object categories, each containing defectfree training and validation images, along with test images exhibiting various defects such as scratches, dents, holes, contamination, and deformation. Additionally, MVTec 3D-AD provides detailed pixel-wise annotations for each anomalous test image.  
[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_006_1] 中文：MVTec 3D-AD Bergmann等人(2022年)是用于无监督的异常检测和定位的大规模三维基准数据集,其特征是使用工业级三维传感器获得的高分辨率扫描,由10个对象类别组成,每个类别都包含无缺陷训练和验证图像,以及显示各种缺陷的测试图像,如刮痕、凹痕、孔、污染和变形。此外,MVTec 3D-AD为每个异常试验图像提供了详细的像素说明。

[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_007_1] EN: Eyecandies Bonfiglioli et al. (2022) is a benchmark dataset for unsupervised anomaly detection and localization. It comprises 14,234 images across ten candy categories, each posing unique challenges, including complex textures, self-occlusion, and specular reflections.  
[PDF 第 48 页 | A048_007_1] 中文：Bonfiglioli等人(2022年)是无监督的异常检测和定位的基准数据集,由14 234张图像组成,覆盖10个糖果类别,每个类别都构成独特的挑战,包括复杂的纹理、自我隔离和镜像反射。

Additionally, it includes depth images, which provide a more detailed representation of object shapes using depth information. All objects and parts in Real3D-AD are sourced from real-world industrial production

[PDF 第 49 页 | A049_002_1] EN: Real-IAD Wang et al. (2024) is a large-scale multi-view industrial anomaly detection dataset. It comprises over 150,000 high-resolution images of industrial products made from 30 different materials, including metal, plastic, wood, ceramics, and mixed materials. Each product is captured from five distinct perspectives, offering multi-view coverage with detailed pixel-level defect annotations. Real-IAD not only surpasses existing datasets in scale but also introduces image-level evaluation metrics and, for the first time, a fully supervised industrial anomaly detection setting. This better simulates real-world industrial anomaly detection scenarios, advancing both research and practical applications in anomaly detection technology.

[PDF 第 49 页 | A049_026_1] EN: indicates better model performance in balancing precision and recall. Image-AUROC is derived from the image-level Area Under the Curve (AUC) of the ROC curve (Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve), which measures a model’s performance in image-level classification. For each image, the model outputs an anomaly score representing the probability of the image being anomalous. Based on the true labels (normal/abnormal) and the predicted scores for all images, an ROC curve is plotted, with the False Positive Rate (FPR) on the horizontal axis and the True Positive Rate (TPR) on the  
[PDF 第 49 页 | A049_026_1] 中文：说明模型在平衡精确率和召回率方面表现越好。Image-AUROC 来自图像级 ROC 曲线（受试者工作特征曲线）的曲线下面积（AUC），用于衡量模型在图像级分类任务中的表现。对于每幅图像，模型输出一个异常分数，表示该图像为异常图像的概率。根据所有图像的真实标签（正常/异常）及预测分数绘制 ROC 曲线，横轴是假正例率（FPR），纵轴是真正例率（TPR）（下页续）。

<a id="pdf-page-50"></a>

## PDF 第 50 页

[查看原 PDF 第 50 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=50>)

[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_001_1] EN: vertical axis. The AUC value is obtained by calculating the area under the ROC curve. The AUROC value ranges from 0 to 1, with higher values indicating better model classification performance. It primarily evaluates a model’s ability to distinguish between normal and abnormal images. Additionally, it assesses the presence of anomalies in an entire image without considering their specific locations.  
[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_001_1] 中文：纵轴（承接上页）。通过计算 ROC 曲线下的面积得到 AUC。AUROC 的取值范围为 0 到 1，数值越高，模型的分类表现越好。它主要评价模型区分正常图像与异常图像的能力；此外，它判断整幅图像是否存在异常，而不考虑异常的具体位置。

[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_002_1] EN: Pixel-AUROC is derived from pixel-level ROC curves, measuring a model’s ability to distinguish anomalous pixels from normal ones. For each pixel, the model outputs an anomaly score representing the probability of the pixel being anomalous. The true labels (normal/abnormal) of all pixels are compared with their corresponding predicted scores, and the ROC curve is then constructed using the pixel-level false positive rate (FPR) on the horizontal axis and the pixel-level true positive rate (TPR) on the vertical axis. The area under the ROC curve is computed to obtain the AUC value.  
[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_002_1] 中文：Pixel-AUROC 来自像素级 ROC 曲线，用于衡量模型区分异常像素与正常像素的能力。模型为每个像素输出一个异常分数，表示该像素为异常像素的概率。将所有像素的真实标签（正常/异常）与对应预测分数进行比较，以像素级假正例率（FPR）为横轴、像素级真正例率（TPR）为纵轴构建 ROC 曲线，再计算曲线下的面积得到 AUC。

[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_003_1] EN: Per-Region Overlap (PRO) is a metric designed for pixel-level anomaly segmentation. It quantifies the overlap between the predicted and ground truth defect regions. Unlike traditional approaches that merely count pixel-level TP and FP, PRO first segments the ground truth defect areas into one or more independent regions and then computes their overlap with the corresponding predicted regions. This approach better captures the overall effectiveness of defect detection and localization, instead of evaluating correctness at an individual pixel level.  
[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_003_1] 中文：区域重叠度（Per-Region Overlap，PRO）是一种用于像素级异常分割的指标，用来量化预测缺陷区域与真实缺陷区域的重叠程度。与仅统计像素级 TP 和 FP 的传统方法不同，PRO 首先将真实缺陷区域划分成一个或多个独立区域，再计算它们与对应预测区域的重叠。这种方式更能反映缺陷检测和定位的整体效果，而不是只评价单个像素是否正确。

[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_004_1] EN: sPRO Bergmann et al. (2022) builds upon PRO with a normalization strategy to provide a fairer evaluation of anomaly detection performance. By normalizing PRO, sPRO mitigates evaluation bias arising from the presence of extremely small or large defect regions, thus ensuring a more equitable comparison across different algorithms.  
[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_004_1] 中文：Bergmann 等（2022）提出的 sPRO 在 PRO 的基础上引入归一化策略，以更公平地评价异常检测性能。通过对 PRO 进行归一化，sPRO 减轻极小或极大缺陷区域引起的评价偏差，从而使不同算法之间的比较更加公平。

[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_005_1] EN: In industrial anomaly detection, it is sometimes necessary to account for variations in anomaly shape, size, and unknown detection scenarios. To address this challenge, AUPRO is commonly employed. AUPRO is derived from the area under the PRO curve and quantifies a model’s overall performance in anomaly region detection, with a particular emphasis on localization accuracy. For each anomalous region, the IoU between the ground truth and predicted anomaly regions is computed as a function of varying prediction thresholds. The horizontal axis represents the Normalized Pixel Coverage (NPC) of the predicted region, defined as the ratio of the predicted region’s area to the ground truth anomaly region’s area. The vertical axis represents the IoU values, and the area under the PRO curve is integrated to obtain the AUPRO value. Compared with AUROC, AUPRO places greater emphasis on the accuracy of anomaly localization instead of solely evaluating classification performance. Model sensitivity to varying anomaly sizes can be analyzed by adjusting the prediction threshold.  
[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_005_1] 中文：在工业异常检测中，有时需要考虑异常形状、尺寸和未知检测场景的变化。为应对这一挑战，通常使用 AUPRO。AUPRO 来自 PRO 曲线下的面积，用于量化模型在异常区域检测方面的整体表现，尤其关注定位精度。对于每个异常区域，随着预测阈值变化，计算真实异常区域与预测异常区域之间的 IoU。横轴表示预测区域的归一化像素覆盖率（Normalized Pixel Coverage，NPC），其定义为预测区域面积与真实异常区域面积的比值；纵轴为 IoU，并通过对 PRO 曲线下的面积积分得到 AUPRO。与 AUROC 相比，AUPRO 更重视异常定位的准确性，而不是仅评价分类性能。通过调节预测阈值，可以分析模型对不同异常尺寸的敏感性。〔此段按原文翻译；其中的指标定义和坐标描述需要结合原始评测协议核对。〕

[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_006_1] EN: Overall, Image-AUPRO, Pixel-AUPRO, PRO, AUPRO, and sPRO form a multi-level evaluation framework for industrial defect detection, particularly in pixel-level segmentation tasks. They emphasize precise localization and identification of defects, incorporating various calculation methods and threshold adjustments to address extreme cases. In industrial applications requiring precise localization and comprehensive detection, these metrics offer a more targeted and interpretable evaluation compared to pixel-level precision and recall alone, thereby enabling researchers to more effectively compare and refine various defect detection algorithms.  
[PDF 第 50 页 | A050_006_1] 中文：总体而言，Image-AUPRO、Pixel-AUPRO、PRO、AUPRO 和 sPRO 构成了面向工业缺陷检测的多层次评价框架，尤其适用于像素级分割任务。它们强调缺陷的精确定位与识别，并通过不同计算方法和阈值调节处理极端情况。对于需要精确定位和全面检测的工业应用，相较于仅使用像素级精确率与召回率，这些指标能够提供更有针对性、更具可解释性的评价，从而帮助研究人员更有效地比较和改进不同缺陷检测算法。〔Image-AUPRO、Pixel-AUPRO 的拼写按原文保留。〕

## PDF 第 51 页

[查看原 PDF 第 51 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=51>)

[PDF 第 51 页 | A051_001_1] EN: 4.2 Experiment results  
[PDF 第 51 页 | A051_001_1] 中文：4.2 实验结果。

[PDF 第 51 页 | A051_002_1] EN: This section presents the experimental results of industrial anomaly detection, aiming to provide researchers with a clear comparative analysis in order to more accurately select anomaly detection methods suitable for different scenarios. All experimental results are excerpted from the original papers of each method, fully adopting their publicly available training–testing splits, input resolutions, backbone networks, and evaluation scripts. To ensure a representative and diverse evaluation, we cover the main categories introduced in Sect. 2. Specifically, these include reconstruction-based (e.g., CutPaste, PatchCore), feature embedding-based (e.g., FastFlow, SPADE), and diffusion-based models (e.g., RealNet). The selection also spans different supervision paradigms, including unsupervised, weakly supervised, and few-shot approaches. This broad coverage allows for a more comprehensive comparison across various learning strategies and detection paradigms. When exploring the applicability in different manufacturing environments, the quality control method proposed by Bono et al. (2023) adopts a strategy that combines soft sensors with anomaly detectors, which effectively solves the problem of quality monitoring under highly unstable working conditions. Based on this research, the experimental data presented in this paper were collected from diverse industrial production environments, which fully reflects the actual performance of the anomaly detection method in each scenario. As shown in Table 2, we summarize the highlights of some methods in order to provide researchers with a strong reference when selecting anomaly detection strategies.  
[PDF 第 51 页 | A051_002_1] 中文：本节介绍工业异常检测的实验结果,目的是向研究人员提供明确的比较分析,以便更准确地选择适合不同情景的异常检测方法。 所有实验结果都摘自每种方法的原始文件,完全采用其公开可得到的训练 — — 测试分解、输入分辨率、主干网络和评估脚本。 为确保具有代表性和多样性的评价,我们涵盖了第2节引入的主要类别。 具体而言,这些类别包括基于重建(如CutPaste、PatchCore)、基于嵌入功能(如快速流、SPADE)以及基于推广的模式(如RealNet)。 选择还涉及不同的监督模式,包括无人监督、监管薄弱和少见的方法。这种广泛的覆盖面使得可以对各种学习战略和检测模式进行更全面的比较。在探索不同制造环境中的适用性时,Bono等人(2023年)提出的质量控制方法采用了将软传感器与异常检测器结合起来的战略,有效地解决了在高度不稳定的工作条件下的质量监测问题。根据这项研究,本文件提出的实验数据来自不同的工业生产环境,这充分反映了每种情况下异常检测方法的实际表现。 如表2所示,我们概述了一些方法的要点,以便在选择异常检测战略时为研究人员提供有力的参考。

[PDF 第 51 页 | A051_002_2] EN: Table 3 summarizes the experimental results of different methods and their resource consumption on the MVTec AD and VisA datasets. Tables 4 and 5 illustrate the image-level and pixel-level results for each category on the MVTec AD dataset, respectively. Similarly, Tables 6 and 7 show the image-level and pixel-level results for each category on the VisA dataset. Table 8 presents the experimental results on the MVTec 3D AD dataset, and Table 10 shows the experimental results on the MVTec LOCO AD dataset. In addition, Fig. 12 presents the experimental results on the MVTec AD dataset, intuitively illustrating the differences in anomaly detection performance among various methods through visualizations. Figure 13 presents the visualization results of anomaly detection experiments on the VisA dataset. These results clearly highlight each method’s capability in detecting anomalies in complex structures and multi-instance objects, as well as their effectiveness on single-instance objects. Figure 14 presents the visualized experimental results on the MVTec 3D AD dataset, clearly demonstrating the performance of different methods in handling three-dimensional anomaly detection tasks. The MVTec 3D AD dataset provides high-quality RGB-D data with realistic 3D surface defects in multiple object categories. Unlike 2D datasets that focus primarily on texture anomalies, this dataset enables the evaluation of methods capable of capturing geometric deformations, depth anomalies, and irregular surface topology, which are becoming increasingly important in modern industrial inspection scenarios.  
[PDF 第 51 页 | A051_002_2] 中文：表3总结了不同方法的实验结果及其在MVTec AD和VisA数据集的资源消耗情况,表4和5分别说明了MVTec AD数据集中每个类别的图像水平和像素水平的结果,表6和7显示了VisA数据集中每个类别的图像水平和像素水平的结果。表8列出了MVTec 3D AD数据集中的试验结果,表10显示了MVTec LOCO AD数据集中的实验结果。 此外,图12还介绍了MVTec AD数据集的实验结果,直观地说明了各种方法之间通过可视化发现异常性能的差异。图13展示了VisA数据集中异常性检测试验的可视化结果。这些结果明确显示了每种方法在复杂结构和多因子天体中探测异常性的能力及其在单向物体上的效力。图14展示了MVTec 3D AD数据集的可视性实验结果,清楚地表明了处理三维异常性探测任务的不同方法的绩效。 MVTec 3D AD数据集提供了高品质的 RGB-D 数据,在多个对象类别中具有切合实际的 3D 表面缺陷。 与主要侧重于质谱异常的 2D 数据集不同,该数据集使得能够评估能够捕捉几何变形、深度异常和不规则表面地形的方法,这些在现代工业检查情况下变得越来越重要。

[PDF 第 51 页 | A051_002_3] EN: Furthermore, MVTec 3D AD has been widely used in recent literature as a benchmark for evaluating 3D vision anomaly detection algorithms, ensuring the comparability and reproducibility of experimental results. These characteristics make it a suitable and representative dataset for validating the performance of reconstruction- and depth-aware anomaly detection methods. Figure 15 demonstrates the detection results on the MVTec LOCO AD dataset, and Fig. 16 shows the detection results on the BTAD, MPDD, AeBAD-S datasets which compares the advantages of the methods in a more intuitive way.  
[PDF 第 51 页 | A051_002_3] 中文：此外,在最近的文献中广泛使用MVTec 3DAD作为评估三维异常检测算法的基准,确保实验结果的可比性和可复制性。这些特点使它成为验证重建和深度觉察异常检测方法表现的适当和有代表性的数据集。图15显示了MVTec LOCO AD数据集的检测结果,图16显示了BTAD、MPDD、AeBAD-S数据集的检测结果,以更直观的方式比较了这种方法的优点。

## PDF 第 54 页

[查看原 PDF 第 54 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=54>)

[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_001_1] EN: In practical industrial visual anomaly detection, parameter selection and tuning remain critical challenges due to the diversity of inspection environments, object types, and defect patterns. Different industrial scenarios may require different settings for thresholds, feature dimensions, and backbone depths to achieve optimal performance. To address these issues, several strategies have been commonly adopted. One approach is to initialize parameters using configurations that perform robustly across multiple public datasets (e.g., MVTec AD, VisA), providing a good starting point. Additionally, lightweight tuning can be performed on a small set of validation images collected from the target environment to calibrate decision thresholds or refine feature selection layers. Furthermore, the use of adaptive modulessuch as attention mechanisms, multi-scale fusion, or learnable normalization layers-has been proven effective in reducing the sensitivity of models to fixed parameter values. These techniques help models generalize better under changing lighting conditions, background textures, and defect shapes. As industrial systems move toward real-world deployment, balancing generality and adaptability in parameter design remains an essential consideration for anomaly detection frameworks. These parameter adaptation strategies have been explored and validated in several representative works, including CutPaste Li et al. (2021), PatchCore Roth et al. (2022), and WinCLIP Jeong et al. (2023), which demonstrate strong robustness and transferability across industrial datasets.  
[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_001_1] 中文：在实际的工业视觉异常检测中,参数选择和调试由于检查环境、对象类型和缺陷模式的多样性而仍然是关键的挑战。不同的工业情景可能需要对阈值、特征尺寸和主干深度的不同设置来实现最佳性能。为了解决这些问题,通常采取了若干战略。一种办法是利用在多个公共数据集(例如MVTec AD、VisA)上表现强劲的配置来初始化参数,提供一个良好的起点。 此外,对从目标环境中收集的、用于校准决定阈值或改进特征选择层的一小套验证图像进行轻量级调试。此外,使用适应性模块(如注意机制、多尺度聚变或可学习的正常化层)已被证明在降低模型对固定参数值的敏感性方面是有效的。这些技术有助于模型在不断变化的照明条件、背景质地和缺陷形状下更普遍化。随着工业系统转向现实世界部署,在参数设计中平衡一般性和适应性仍然是异常检测框架的一个必要考虑因素。 这些参数适应战略已在一些具有代表性的工作中得到探讨和验证,其中包括CutPaste Li等人(2021年)、PatchCore Roth等人(2022年)和WinCLIP Jeong等人(2023年),它们表明工业数据集的可靠性和可转移性很强。

[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_002_1] EN: The experimental results further reveal the differences between different algorithms in dealing with structural and semantic anomalies. Taking MVTec LOCO AD as an example, we have calculated the AUROC metrics of multiple methods under structural anomalies (SA) and logical/semantic anomalies (LA). As can be seen from Table 9, GCAD performs better in dealing with structural anomalies (e.g., the SA-AUROC of the Connector class reaches 99.0), while ComAD has certain advantages in logical anomaly detection, indicating that the method is more capable of modeling contextual semantic relationships. These results suggest that different types of anomalies may require different modeling strategies. Structural anomalies rely more on fine-grained boundary reconstruction and low-level feature modeling, while semantic anomalies rely more on global context understanding and high-level feature comparison.  
[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_002_1] 中文：实验结果进一步揭示了处理结构异常和语义异常的不同算法之间的差异。以MVTec LOCO AD为例,我们计算出了在结构异常和逻辑/语义异常下多种方法的AUROC衡量标准。 从表9中可以看出,GCAD在处理结构异常方面表现得更好(例如连接器级SA-AUROC达到99.0),而COMAD在逻辑异常检测方面具有某些优势,表明该方法更有能力模拟背景语义关系。 这些结果表明,不同类型的异常可能需要不同的建模战略。 结构性异常更多地依赖于细微的边界重建和低级地物建模,而语义异常则更多依赖全球背景理解和高级别地物比较。

[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_003_1] EN: Based on the results presented in Tables 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, and 9 and Figures 12, 13, 14, 15 and 16, we observe that feature embedding-based methods such as PatchCore and FastFlow perform well in detecting structural anomalies, showing strong generalization across data-sets with clear defect patterns. Diffusion-based methods like RealNet achieve superior performance in both localization and anomaly synthesis, particularly in complex backgrounds or low-contrast regions. Meanwhile, reconstruction-based methods demonstrate solid detection capabilities but tend to be more sensitive to anomaly scale and texture variability. These findings highlight the trade-offs between detection accuracy, robustness, and computational efficiency, providing useful guidance for selecting appropriate methods under different industrial scenarios.  
[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_003_1] 中文：根据表3、4、5、6、7、8和9以及图12、13、14、15和16所示的结果,我们观察到基于嵌入的特征方法,如PatchCore和FastFlow在发现结构异常方面表现良好,显示出对各数据集的强烈概括性,并有明显的缺陷模式。RealNet等基于扩散的方法在定位和异常合成两方面都取得了优异的绩效,特别是在复杂背景或低相干区域。与此同时,基于重建的方法显示了坚实的检测能力,但往往对异常的规模和质地变更敏感。 这些调查结果突出表明了检测准确性、稳健性和计算效率之间的权衡,为在不同工业情景下选择适当方法提供了有益的指导。

[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_004_1] EN: 4.3 Parameter selection  
[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_004_1] 中文：4.3 参数选择

[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_005_1] EN: In real-world deployment of industrial visual anomaly detection (IVAD) systems, the selection and optimization of hyperparameters remains one of the major challenges in model implementation. This difficulty primarily stems from the diversity and complexity of industrial environments, where production lines can vary significantly in terms of camera angles,  
[PDF 第 54 页 | A054_005_1] 中文：在实际部署工业视觉异常检测系统时,选择和优化超参数仍然是模型实施的主要挑战之一。

## PDF 第 62 页

[查看原 PDF 第 62 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=62>)

[PDF 第 62 页 | A062_001_1] EN: lighting conditions, defect manifestations, and dimensional tolerances of acceptable products. Such variations hinder the unified optimization of key hyperparameters, including decision thresholds, feature dimensions, backbone network depth, and batch size. Moreover, environmental changes during production-such as equipment wear, illumination drift, or material variation-can introduce domain shifts, leading to performance degradation over time. These dynamic factors further complicate parameter selection and reduce the longterm reliability of fixed hyperparameter configurations.  
[PDF 第 62 页 | A062_001_1] 中文：光照条件、缺陷表现以及合格产品的尺寸公差（承接上页）。这些差异妨碍了对决策阈值、特征维度、骨干网络深度和批量大小等关键超参数进行统一优化。此外，生产期间的设备磨损、光照漂移或材料变化可能引起域偏移，导致性能随时间下降。这些动态因素进一步增加参数选择的复杂性，降低固定超参数配置的长期可靠性。

[PDF 第 62 页 | A062_002_1] EN: To address these issues, a common practice is to adopt parameter configurations that have shown robust performance across multiple public datasets (e.g., MVTec AD, VisA) as initial baselines. These are then lightly fine-tuned using small-scale in-field data to calibrate thresholds or adjust feature-related settings. Additionally, incorporating adaptive modules-such as attention mechanisms, multi-scale feature fusion, and learnable normalization layers-can mitigate the model’s sensitivity to fixed parameters and improve generalization under shifting production conditions. These strategies have been widely validated across various industrial scenarios, demonstrating their effectiveness and practical utility in improving deployment robustness.  
[PDF 第 62 页 | A062_002_1] 中文：为应对这些问题，常见做法是将已在多个公开数据集（如 MVTec AD、VisA）上表现稳健的参数配置作为初始基线，再利用少量现场数据进行轻量微调，以校准阈值或调整特征相关设置。此外，引入注意力机制、多尺度特征融合和可学习归一化层等自适应模块，可以减轻模型对固定参数的敏感性，提高模型在生产条件变化时的泛化能力。这些策略已在多种工业场景中得到广泛验证，表现出提升部署稳健性的效果与实用价值。

[PDF 第 62 页 | A062_003_1] EN: In the actual deployment of industrial vision anomaly detection systems, threshold setting plays a critical role in determining both practicality and economic efficiency. An overly low threshold can result in excessive false alarms, leading to frequent production line interruptions and increased manual inspection costs. Conversely, a high threshold may cause real defects to be missed, posing significant quality risks. Therefore, selecting an appropriate threshold is essential for effective model deployment. A common approach involves using a small amount of labeled data in the offline phase to determine the initial threshold by maximizing the F1 score. When only normal samples are available, the threshold is typically set at a high-percentile point of the anomaly score distribution that meets a target false alarm rate, based on statistical modeling of normal data. However, once the model is deployed, the anomaly score distribution often drifts due to factors such as lighting variations, equipment aging, and batch-to-batch differences in production. To cope with this, sliding window statistics or cost-sensitive dynamic calibration methods are employed to maintain an optimal balance between false positives and false negatives. Industrial practice has shown that a well-designed threshold management strategy can reduce the false positive rate by over 40% while maintaining the false negative rate below 0.1%, underscoring the critical role and practical value of threshold optimization in real-world applications.  
[PDF 第 62 页 | A062_003_1] 中文：在工业视觉异常检测系统的实际部署中，阈值设置对于系统的实用性和经济性至关重要。阈值过低可能产生过多误报，造成产线频繁中断，并增加人工检查成本；阈值过高则可能漏掉真实缺陷，带来显著质量风险。因此，选择适当阈值是有效部署模型的必要条件。一种常见做法是在离线阶段使用少量带标签数据，通过最大化 F1 分数确定初始阈值。只有正常样本时，通常根据正常数据的统计模型，在异常分数分布的较高百分位处设置满足目标误报率的阈值。然而，模型部署后，光照变化、设备老化和生产批次差异等因素常使异常分数分布发生漂移。为此，可以使用滑动窗口统计或代价敏感的动态校准方法，在误检与漏检之间维持合适平衡。原文称，工业实践表明，设计良好的阈值管理策略能够在将漏检率保持在 0.1% 以下的同时，使误检率下降超过 40%，说明阈值优化在实际应用中具有关键作用与实用价值。〔这些数值为原文陈述，并不构成对你的数据或实验的性能承诺。〕

## PDF 第 64 页

[查看原 PDF 第 64 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=64>)

[PDF 第 64 页 | A064_001_1] EN: point cloud data, while providing 3D shape information, cannot penetrate deep into objects. Therefore, relying solely on visible light or surface-based detection techniques may still result in missing key internal anomalies. To improve detection coverage and accuracy, it is essential to construct a comprehensive dataset incorporating multimodal data (e.g., RGB images, point clouds, X-ray imaging, and ultrasonic scanning). Through multimodal collaboration, industrial systems can gain a more comprehensive evaluation of the internal and external states of products, thus enabling more precise localization and identification of complex anomalies. This presents both a challenge in data collection and fusion and a key future direction in industrial anomaly detection. Additionally, multi-perspective data collection solutions are indispensable for capturing object anomalies from multiple viewpoints. Taking Real-IAD as an example, this dataset is designed for real industrial scenarios and employs large-scale, multi-perspective collection methods. Each object is captured from at least five different viewpoints to fully account for potential appearance variations. This type of multi-view and multimodal data resource not only adapts to varying object forms and complex backgrounds in industrial scenarios but also supports deep learning algorithms in defect recognition, localization, and classification, ultimately forming a solid foundation for developing a high-precision and robust industrial anomaly detection system.  
[PDF 第 64 页 | A064_001_1] 中文：点云数据虽然提供了三维形状信息,但无法深入到物体中。 因此,仅仅依靠可见光或地面探测技术仍可能导致缺失关键的内部异常现象。 为了提高检测范围和准确性,必须构建一个包含多式数据的综合数据集(如RGB图像、点云、X射线成像和超声波扫描 ) 。 通过多式联运合作,工业系统可以对产品的内部和外部状态进行更全面的评估,从而能够更精确地定位和识别复杂的异常现象。 这既提出了数据收集和聚合方面的挑战,也提出了工业异常检测的关键未来方向。此外,多视角数据收集解决方案对于从多重角度捕捉物体异常现象必不可少。以真实的IAD为例,该数据集是为实际工业情景设计的,并采用大规模、多视角收集方法。每个对象都从至少五个不同的角度采集,以充分说明潜在的外观变化。这种多视图和多式联运数据资源不仅适应了工业情景中不同的天体形式和复杂背景,而且还支持缺陷识别、定位和分类方面的深层次学习算法,最终为开发高精度和强健的工业异常检测系统奠定了坚实的基础。

[PDF 第 64 页 | A064_002_1] EN: 5.2 Precision and latency in real-time  
[PDF 第 64 页 | A064_002_1] 中文：5.2 实时精确度和延迟度

[PDF 第 64 页 | A064_003_1] EN: In the industrial visual inspection scenario, there are significant differences between real-time anomaly detection and batch processing detection methods in terms of system design, performance requirements and application goals. Real-time detection emphasizes millisecond image acquisition and inference response, and is widely used in scenarios that are extremely sensitive to response speed, such as high-takt production lines, semiconductor defect detection. Take EfficientAD as an example. It can achieve a single-image inference latency of about 2ms on the NVIDIA RTX A6000 GPU, processing up to 600 images per second, demonstrating excellent real-time detection performance and providing a reference for industrial deployment. However, in actual deployment, deep learning models still face many computational bottlenecks in achieving real-time performance. First, models such as convolutional neural networks and Transformers have a large number of parameters, which require a large number of matrix operations and convolution operations, resulting in a significant increase in inference latency. Second, industrial images are often high-resolution, and a large number of intermediate features and model parameters can cause memory bottlenecks and bandwidth pressure. At the same time, data pre-processing and transmission also pose challenges for I/O performance. The limited computing power of edge devices often makes it difficult to support the stable operation of complex models. Power consumption and thermal design constraints are also issues that cannot be ignored.  
[PDF 第 64 页 | A064_003_1] 中文：在工业直观检查设想中,在系统设计、性能要求和应用目标方面,实时异常检测和批次处理检测方法之间差别很大。实时检测强调毫秒图像采集和推断反应,并广泛用于对响应速度极为敏感的情景,如高塔克特生产线、半导体缺陷检测。以高效AD为例。它可以在NVIDIA RTX A6000 GPU上达到大约2米的单一图像推算延度,每秒处理多达600张图像,显示极好的实时检测性能并为工业部署提供参考。 然而,在实际部署中,深层学习模式在实现实时性能方面仍面临许多计算瓶颈,首先,诸如变异神经网络和变异器等模型有许多参数,需要大量的矩阵操作和变异操作,导致推导延迟度大幅提高;其次,工业图像往往是高分辨率的,而大量中间特征和模型参数可造成记忆瓶颈和带宽压力;与此同时,数据预处理和传输也给I/O性能带来挑战。 边缘设备的计算能力有限,往往难以支持复杂模型的稳定运行。 电力消耗和热设计限制也是不容忽视的问题。

[PDF 第 64 页 | A064_003_2] EN: In addition, the latency of data collection, pre-processing, model inference and post-processing has a cumulative effect. Without system-level coordinated optimization, it significantly slow down the overall response. In contrast, the batch processing method is more suitable for offline quality analysis, defect statistics, and exception tracing tasks. This method allows for greater freedom in model expressiveness by caching or centrally processing images, which allows for the use of more complex model structures and more powerful post-processing modules. However, there is a feedback delay in the detection results, which is difficult to meet real-time industrial scenarios that require immediate intervention or control.  
[PDF 第 64 页 | A064_003_2] 中文：此外,数据收集、预处理、模型推理和后处理的延迟性具有累积效应,没有系统一级的协调优化,就会大大减缓整体反应;相比之下,批量处理方法更适合离线质量分析、缺陷统计和例外追踪任务;这种方法通过缓存或集中处理图像使模型表达更加自由,从而可以使用更复杂的模型结构和更强大的后处理模块。 然而,检测结果出现反馈延迟,难以满足需要立即干预或控制的实时工业情景。

## PDF 第 66 页

[查看原 PDF 第 66 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=66>)

[PDF 第 66 页 | A066_001_1] EN: From a technical implementation perspective, real-time detection usually relies on lightweight models, model compression techniques, and efficient inference engines, and is deployed on edge devices, FPGAs, or AI modules. Batch processing, on the other hand, can leverage cloud resources to run large models, enabling higher resolution or multi-modal detection capabilities. In summary, real-time detection emphasizes low latency, high responsiveness, and edge deployment capabilities. Batch processing methods, on the other hand, focus on complexity and depth of analysis, and are more suitable for strategic quality  
[PDF 第 66 页 | A066_001_1] 中文：从技术执行的角度来看,实时探测通常依赖轻量级模型、模型压缩技术和高效推导引擎,并部署在边缘设备、燃料元件控制GAs或AI模块上。另一方面,批量处理可以利用云资源运行大型模型,从而能够提高分辨率或多模式检测能力。简言之,实时探测强调低延迟度、高反应能力和边缘部署能力。而批量处理方法则侧重于分析的复杂性和深度,更适合战略质量。

[PDF 第 67 页 | A067_001_1] EN: monitoring and post-model evaluation. In actual industrial deployment, a hybrid architecture that combines real-time detection at the edge with batch processing and analysis in the cloud has become the mainstream trend, taking into account both immediate intervention and system scalability.  
[PDF 第 67 页 | A067_001_1] 中文：在实际工业部署中,将边缘的实时检测与云层中的批量处理和分析相结合的混合结构已成为主流趋势,同时考虑到即时干预和系统可缩放性。

[PDF 第 67 页 | A067_002_1] EN: 5.3 Generative anomaly detection  
[PDF 第 67 页 | A067_002_1] 中文：5.3 产生异常检测

[PDF 第 67 页 | A067_003_1] EN: In the field of industrial visual anomaly detection, generative detection methods are gaining increasing prominence. These methods learn the distribution of ’normal’ data through deep generative models, enabling anomaly detection during inference. This approach is especially effective in industrial scenarios where normal samples are readily available, but abnormal samples are rare or difficult to obtain. As generative models continue to advance in network architectures, training strategies, inference efficiency, and interpretability, industrial visual anomaly detection is evolving towards greater accuracy, enhanced adaptability, and improved interpretability. Notably, when combined with weakly supervised, semi-supervised, and multimodal data fusion techniques, the potential of generative anomaly detection in industrial applications can be further unlocked, enabling highly automated, reliable industrial systems and more robust solutions to adapt to dynamic manufacturing processes and diverse defect patterns.  
[PDF 第 67 页 | A067_003_1] 中文：在工业视觉异常检测领域,基因检测方法越来越突出。 这些方法通过深层基因模型学习了“正常”数据的分布,从而得以在推理过程中发现异常。 这种方法在通常样本容易获得但异常样品很少或难以获取的工业情景中特别有效。 随着基因模型在网络结构、训练策略、推断效率和可解释性方面不断进步,工业视觉异常检测正在朝着更高的准确度、更强的适应性和更好的解释性发展。 值得注意的是,如果与监管薄弱、半监督和多式数据聚合技术相结合,工业应用中的基因异常检测潜力可以进一步释放,使高度自动化和可靠的工业系统以及更强有力的解决方案能够适应动态制造过程和各种缺陷模式。

[PDF 第 67 页 | A067_004_1] 中文：图16 BTAD、MPDD和AeBAD-S数据集中的实验结果

## PDF 第 68 页

[查看原 PDF 第 68 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=68>)

[PDF 第 68 页 | A068_001_1] EN: 5.4 Lightweighting and hardware acceleration  
[PDF 第 68 页 | A068_001_1] 中文：5.4 轻量级和硬件加速

[PDF 第 68 页 | A068_002_1] EN: With the rapid advancement of industrial automation and intelligent manufacturing, the application value of the industrial internet of things and edge computing technology in industrial visual anomaly detection is becoming increasingly prominent. Industrial internet of things connects high-resolution industrial cameras, intelligent sensors, and actuators to continuously and frequently collect and distribute multi-source image data from the production process, providing rich and real-time visual information for anomaly detection models. However, traditional cloud-based detection methods are limited by image transmission delays, bandwidth bottlenecks, and data privacy issues, making it difficult to meet the actual needs of high-takt production lines for low latency and high responsiveness.  
[PDF 第 68 页 | A068_002_1] 中文：随着工业自动化和智能制造的迅猛发展,在工业视觉异常检测中,工业用互联网和边际计算技术的应用价值日益显著。 工业用互联网将高分辨率工业照相机、智能传感器和促动器连接起来,以不断和经常地收集和发布生产过程中的多来源图像数据,为异常检测模型提供丰富的实时视觉信息。 然而,传统的云基检测方法由于图像传输延迟、带宽瓶颈和数据隐私问题而受到限制,因此难以满足高塔特生产线对低耐久性和高响应性的实际需求。

[PDF 第 68 页 | A068_003_1] EN: To address these challenges, edge computing is gradually becoming the mainstream choice. By deploying the visual anomaly detection model directly on edge devices close to the data source, the system can achieve local inference and millisecond-level response, significantly reducing transmission latency and communication load. In this process, lightweight neural network architectures are optimized using techniques such as pruning, quantization, and knowledge distillation, enabling stable operation on resource-constrained hardware platforms. For example, edge GPUs: the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano runs an INT8- quantised EfficientAD model at approximately 1.5ms per 640  
[PDF 第 68 页 | A068_003_1] 中文：为了应对这些挑战,边缘计算正在逐渐成为主流选择。 通过直接在接近数据源的边缘设备上部署视觉异常检测模型,该系统可以实现本地推论和毫秒反应,从而显著降低传输延迟度和通信负荷。 在这一过程中,轻量神经网络结构被优化了,使用了诸如裁剪、量化和知识蒸馏等技术,使得资源限制的硬件平台能够稳定运行。 例如,边缘 GPUs: NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano 运行了一个INT8 定量高效AD模型,每640人约1.5米。

## PDF 第 72 页

[查看原 PDF 第 72 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=72>)

[PDF 第 72 页 | A072_001_1] EN: ers an inference latency of 12ms on a MobileNet-based detector after full INT8 compilation. Industrial FPGAs: AMD (Xilinx) Kria KV260 and Versal AI Edge modules, pre-loaded with the DPU or Vitis-AI, achieve 5–7ms per 1080p frame, while guaranteeing deterministic latency and low power consumption. In addition, dedicated hardware accelerators (such as TPU and FPGA) offer unique advantages in terms of parallelism, energy efficiency and customizability, providing strong support for visual inspection tasks deployed at the edge. Furthermore, an edge-cloud collaborative architecture is also widely used in industrial scenarios: real-time detection and initial screening are performed at the edge, while the cloud is responsible for complex scene modeling and high-precision model updates, thereby balancing response speed and detection accuracy. In the future, industrial visual inspection systems continue to integrate low-power vision modules, multimodal sensing fusion mechanisms, and adaptive structural search technologies at the edge to better meet the multiple needs of industrial sites for high accuracy, high efficiency, and low power consumption, and promote the widespread implementation of intelligent quality control systems.  
[PDF 第 72 页 | A072_001_1] 中文：在完全编译INT8之后,移动网络探测器的推论延迟度为12米。 工业FPGAs:AMD(Xilinx)Kria KV260和Versal AI Edge模块,预先与 DPU 或 Vitis-AI 一起装入,每1080p 框架能达到5-7米,同时保证确定性延迟度和低耗电量。 此外,专用硬件加速器(如TPU 和 FPGA)在平行、能源效率和可定制性方面提供了独特的优势,为边缘部署的视觉检查任务提供了强有力的支持。 此外,在工业场景中也广泛使用边宽合作结构:实时检测和初步筛查在边缘进行,而云层负责复杂的现场建模和高精度模型更新,从而平衡反应速度和探测准确性。 在未来,工业视觉检查系统继续整合低功率视觉模块、多式感应聚合机制以及边缘的适应性结构性搜索技术,以更好地满足工业场地对高精确度、高效性和低电耗的多重需求,并促进智能质量控制系统的广泛实施。

[PDF 第 72 页 | A072_002_1] EN: In addition, while improving system efficiency and deployment flexibility, the network security of the model is also an issue that cannot be ignored in industrial deployment. AI-driven anomaly detection models may face various risks such as adversarial attacks (misleading the model by constructing inputs), data poisoning (tampering with training samples), and model leakage (reconstructing input data through output). If these security risks are exploited, they not only affect system reliability, but may also lead to abnormal production lines or even the disclosure of sensitive data. Therefore, security mechanisms such as adversarial training, access control, and encrypted model compression should be introduced in actual deployments to ensure the robustness and trustworthiness of industrial intelligent detection systems.  
[PDF 第 72 页 | A072_002_1] 中文：此外,在提高系统效率和部署灵活性的同时,该模型的网络安全也是工业部署中不容忽视的一个问题。 AI驱动的异常检测模型可能面临各种风险,如对抗性攻击(通过构建输入物误导模型 ) 、 数据中毒(使用训练样本)和模型渗漏(通过输出重建投入数据 ) 。 如果这些安全风险被利用,它们不仅影响系统的可靠性,还可能导致生产线异常,甚至敏感数据的披露。 因此,在实际部署中应采用对抗性训练、出入控制和加密模型压缩等安全机制,以确保工业智能探测系统的可靠性和可信赖性。

[PDF 第 72 页 | A072_003_1] EN: 5.5 Incremental learning for industrial visual anomaly detection  
[PDF 第 72 页 | A072_003_1] 中文：5.5 工业视觉异常检测的递增学习

[PDF 第 72 页 | A072_004_1] EN: In industrial production, evolving product designs, manufacturing processes, and defect types often render static models ineffective, necessitating costly full retraining. Incremental learning offers a practical solution by enabling models to adapt to emerging defect categories and shifting data distributions while retaining previously acquired knowledge. This approach significantly reduces the time and computational resources required for frequent retraining and supports sustained model performance. In real-world industrial visual anomaly detection deployments, incremental learning typically unfolds in two complementary stages. The first stage involves the automatic discovery of novel anomalies, commonly achieved through clustering or visual inspection of flagged samples. Once a new defect pattern is confirmed, the model undergoes targeted incremental updates-either during low-load periods or directly on edge devices-to swiftly integrate the new characteristics. The second stage focuses on gradual adaptation to distributional shifts caused by changing lighting conditions, material variations, or equipment wear. In this phase, the model is continuously updated using small batches of incoming data, often leveraging techniques such as experience replay or regularization constraints to prevent catastrophic forgetting of previously learned defect information. Looking ahead, as production lines become increasingly autonomous and flexible, incremental learning will play a vital role in ensuring that industrial visual anomaly detection systems maintain high robustness and responsiveness  
[PDF 第 72 页 | A072_004_1] 中文：在工业生产中,产品设计、制造流程和缺陷类型往往使静态模型失效,需要花费大量时间进行全面再训练。 渐进式学习通过让模型适应新出现的缺陷类别和改变数据分布而同时保留先前获得的知识提供了一种实用的解决办法。这种方法大大减少了频繁再训练所需的时间和计算资源并支持了持续的模型性能。在现实世界中的视觉异常检测部署中,递增学习通常分为两个互补阶段。 第一阶段是自动发现新奇的异常现象,通常是通过集群或目视检查标记样品实现的。 一旦新缺陷模式得到确认,模型将进行有针对性的渐进更新,无论是在低载期还是直接在边缘装置上,以迅速整合新的特征;第二阶段的重点是逐步适应因照明条件变化、物质变异或设备磨损而造成的分布变化;在这一阶段,模型将不断使用少量收到的数据加以更新,往往利用经验重播或正规化限制等技术来防止灾难性地忘记以前学到的缺陷信息。 展望未来,随着生产线越来越自主和灵活,增量学习将在确保工业视觉异常检测系统保持高度稳健和反应能力方面发挥关键作用。

<a id="pdf-page-73"></a>

## PDF 第 73 页

[查看原 PDF 第 73 页](<A survey of deep learning for industrial visual anomaly detection.pdf#page=73>)

[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_001_1] EN: to unforeseen defect modes. This capability is essential for sustaining consistent quality control in dynamic industrial environments.  
[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_001_1] 中文：这种能力对于在充满活力的工业环境中保持连贯的质量控制至关重要。

[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_002_1] EN: 5.6 Federated learning and privacy-preserving techniques  
[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_002_1] 中文：5.6 联邦学习和隐私保护技术

[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_003_1] EN: With the increasing emphasis on data security in industrial settings, federated learning and privacy-preserving techniques have garnered growing attention in visual anomaly detection. Traditional training paradigms often rely on centralized data aggregation, which poses potential risks of data leakage and regulatory non-compliance. In contrast, federated learning enhances data security and legal compliance by distributing model training across multiple local clients and performing parameter updates without transmitting raw image data.  
[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_003_1] 中文：随着工业环境对数据安全的日益重视,联盟式学习和隐私保护技术在视觉异常检测方面得到了越来越多的关注。 传统的训练模式往往依赖集中化的数据汇总,这带来了数据泄漏和监管不合规的潜在风险。 相反,联盟式学习通过向多个本地客户分发模型训练和进行参数更新而不传输原始图像数据来增强数据安全和法律合规性。

[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_004_1] EN: In industrial visual inspection tasks, data distributions often vary significantly across different factories, devices, or production lines. Federated learning facilitates collaborative model training while preserving the domain-specific characteristics of each node, thereby improving generalization in heterogeneous environments. Moreover, the integration of differential privacy, homomorphic encryption, and secure aggregation techniques can further prevent the inference or reconstruction of sensitive data from model updates, ensuring robust protection at the edge.  
[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_004_1] 中文：在工业直观检查任务中,不同工厂、设备或生产线的数据分布往往差异很大。 联邦学习有助于合作模式训练,同时保留每个节点的域特性,从而改善多元环境中的通用性。 此外,将差异隐私、同质加密和安全聚合技术结合起来可以进一步防止从模型更新中推断或重建敏感数据,确保边缘的保护力。

[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_005_1] EN: Recent studies on federated learning-based anomaly detection frameworks have begun to explore strategies for knowledge sharing among distributed edge nodes, especially in response to concept drift and non-stationary data streams. Looking forward, the integration of federated learning with multimodal fusion, model compression, and knowledge distillation is expected to drive the development of more secure, efficient, and scalable industrial visual anomaly detection systems.  
[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_005_1] 中文：最近关于联合学习异常检测框架的研究已经开始探索分布边缘节点之间的知识共享战略,特别是针对概念漂移和非静止数据流的战略。 展望未来,将联合学习与多式聚合、模型压缩和知识蒸馏相结合有望推动更安全、高效和可扩展的工业视觉异常检测系统的发展。

[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_006_1] EN: 6 Conclusion  
[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_006_1] 中文：6 结论

[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_007_1] EN: In this paper, we systematically explore the current developments and emerging trends in industrial visual anomaly detection, spanning methodologies to practical applications. Based on supervision levels, we classify existing methods into five categories-fully supervised, semi-supervised, self-supervised, weakly supervised, and unsupervised-based on their supervisory signals. We also conduct an in-depth analysis of their design motivations, implementation challenges, and performance characteristics with specific case studies. Additionally, we review embedding-based and reconstruction-based industrial visual anomaly detection methods and compare their strengths and limitations in enhancing detection accuracy and reducing inference costs. Through a comprehensive analysis of existing datasets and their application scenarios, we summarize the main challenges in industrial visual anomaly detection, including insufficient data diversity, high annotation costs, and the pressing need for lightweight models. To address these challenges, we propose several promising research directions, including introducing generative models, developing lightweight methods, fusing multimodal data, and developing systematic and integrated approaches for industrial anomaly detection. We hope that our in-depth exploration of these directions further drive the real-world adoption of industrial visual anomaly detection, enhancing efficiency and reliability in industrial production and quality control.  
[PDF 第 73 页 | A073_007_1] 中文：在本文中,我们系统地探讨了工业视觉异常检测方面的当前动态和新趋势,将各种方法运用到实际应用中。根据监督水平,我们将现有方法分为五个类别:完全受监督、半监督、自监督、监管薄弱、无监督,并以其监督信号为基础。我们还深入分析了这些方法的设计动机、实施挑战和性能特点以及具体的案例研究。此外,我们还审查了基于嵌入和重建的工业视觉异常检测方法,并比较其在提高探测准确性和降低推断成本方面的优势和局限性。 通过对现有数据集及其应用情景的全面分析,我们总结了在工业视觉异常检测方面的主要挑战,包括数据多样性不足、标注成本高和对轻量模型的迫切需要。为了应对这些挑战,我们提出了若干有希望的研究方向,包括引入基因模型、开发轻型方法、使用多式数据和制定系统综合的工业异常检测方法。我们希望对这些方向的深入探索能够进一步推动现实世界采用工业视觉异常检测,提高工业生产和质量控制的效率和可靠性。
